 516 /* logs directly */ 
 517 int 
 518 lys_check_id(struct lys_node *node, struct lys_node *parent, struct lys_module *module) 
 519 { 
 520     struct lys_node *start, *stop, *iter; 
 521     struct lys_node_grp *grp; 
 522     int down, up; 
 523  
 524     assert(node); 
 525  
 526     if (!parent) { 
 527         assert(module); 
 528     } else { 
 529         module = parent->module; 
 530     } 
 531     module = lys_main_module(module); 
 532  
 533     switch (node->nodetype) { 
 534     case LYS_GROUPING: 
 535         /* 6.2.1, rule 6 */ 
 536         if (parent) { 
 537             start = *lys_child(parent, LYS_GROUPING); 
 538             if (!start) { 
 539                 down = 0; 
 540                 start = parent; 
 541             } else { 
 542                 down = 1; 
 543             } 
 544             if (parent->nodetype == LYS_EXT) { 
 545                 up = 0; 
 546             } else { 
 547                 up = 1; 
 548             } 
 549         } else { 
 550             down = up = 1; 
 551             start = module->data; 
 552         } 
 553         /* go up */ 
 554         if (up && lys_find_grouping_up(node->name, start)) { 
 555             LOGVAL(LYE_DUPID, LY_VLOG_LYS, node, "grouping", node->name); 
 556             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 557         } 
 558         /* go down, because grouping can be defined after e.g. container in which is collision */ 
 559         if (down) { 
 560             for (iter = start, stop = NULL; iter; iter = iter->prev) { 
 561                 if (!stop) { 
 562                     stop = start; 
 563                 } else if (iter == stop) { 
 564                     break; 
 565                 } 
 566                 if (!(iter->nodetype & (LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_LIST | LYS_GROUPING | LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT))) { 
 567                     continue; 
 568                 } 
 569  
 570                 grp = NULL; 
 571                 while ((grp = lys_get_next_grouping(grp, iter))) { 
 572                     if (ly_strequal(node->name, grp->name, 1)) { 
 573                         LOGVAL(LYE_DUPID,LY_VLOG_LYS, node, "grouping", node->name); 
 574                         return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 575                     } 
 576                 } 
 577             } 
 578         } 
 579         break; 
 580     case LYS_LEAF: 
 581     case LYS_LEAFLIST: 
 582     case LYS_LIST: 
 583     case LYS_CONTAINER: 
 584     case LYS_CHOICE: 
 585     case LYS_ANYDATA: 
 586         /* 6.2.1, rule 7 */ 
 587         if (parent) { 
 588             iter = parent; 
 589             while (iter && (iter->nodetype & (LYS_USES | LYS_CASE | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_AUGMENT))) { 
 590                 if (iter->nodetype == LYS_AUGMENT) { 
 591                     if (((struct lys_node_augment *)iter)->target) { 
 592                         /* augment is resolved, go up */ 
 593                         iter = ((struct lys_node_augment *)iter)->target; 
 594                         continue; 
 595                     } 
 596                     /* augment is not resolved, this is the final parent */ 
 597                     break; 
 598                 } 
 599                 iter = iter->parent; 
 600             } 
 601  
 602             if (!iter) { 
 603                 stop = NULL; 
 604                 iter = module->data; 
 605             } else if (iter->nodetype == LYS_EXT) { 
 606                 stop = iter; 
 607                 iter = *lys_child(iter, node->nodetype); 
 608             } else { 
 609                 stop = iter; 
 610                 iter = iter->child; 
 611             } 
 612         } else { 
 613             stop = NULL; 
 614             iter = module->data; 
 615         } 
 616         while (iter) { 
 617             if (iter->nodetype & (LYS_USES | LYS_CASE)) { 
 618                 iter = iter->child; 
 619                 continue; 
 620             } 
 621  
 622             if (iter->nodetype & (LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST | LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_ANYDATA)) { 
 623                 if (iter->module == node->module && ly_strequal(iter->name, node->name, 1)) { 
 624                     LOGVAL(LYE_DUPID, LY_VLOG_LYS, node, strnodetype(node->nodetype), node->name); 
 625                     return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 626                 } 
 627             } 
 628  
 629             /* special case for choice - we must check the choice's name as 
 630              * well as the names of nodes under the choice 
 631              */ 
 632             if (iter->nodetype == LYS_CHOICE) { 
 633                 iter = iter->child; 
 634                 continue; 
 635             } 
 636  
 637             /* go to siblings */ 
 638             if (!iter->next) { 
 639                 /* no sibling, go to parent's sibling */ 
 640                 do { 
 641                     /* for parent LYS_AUGMENT */ 
 642                     if (iter->parent == stop) { 
 643                         iter = stop; 
 644                         break; 
 645                     } 
 646                     iter = lys_parent(iter); 
 647                     if (iter && iter->next) { 
 648                         break; 
 649                     } 
 650                 } while (iter != stop); 
 651  
 652                 if (iter == stop) { 
 653                     break; 
 654                 } 
 655             } 
 656             iter = iter->next; 
 657         } 
 658         break; 
 659     case LYS_CASE: 
 660         /* 6.2.1, rule 8 */ 
 661         if (parent) { 
 662             start = *lys_child(parent, LYS_CASE); 
 663         } else { 
 664             start = module->data; 
 665         } 
 666  
 667         LY_TREE_FOR(start, iter) { 
 668             if (!(iter->nodetype & (LYS_ANYDATA | LYS_CASE | LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST))) { 
 669                 continue; 
 670             } 
 671  
 672             if (iter->module == node->module && ly_strequal(iter->name, node->name, 1)) { 
 673                 LOGVAL(LYE_DUPID, LY_VLOG_LYS, node, "case", node->name); 
 674                 return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 675             } 
 676         } 
 677         break; 
 678     default: 
 679         /* no check needed */ 
 680         break; 
 681     } 
 682  
 683     return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
 684 } 
 685  
 686 /* logs directly */ 
 687 int 
 688 lys_node_addchild(struct lys_node *parent, struct lys_module *module, struct lys_node *child) 
 689 { 
 690     struct lys_node *iter, **pchild; 
 691     struct lys_node_inout *in, *out, *inout; 
 692     struct lys_node_case *c; 
 693     int type, shortcase = 0; 
 694     void *p; 
 695     struct lyext_substmt *info = NULL; 
 696  
 697     assert(child); 
 698  
 699     if (parent) { 
 700         type = parent->nodetype; 
 701         module = parent->module; 
 702     } else { 
 703         assert(module); 
 704         assert(!(child->nodetype & (LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT))); 
 705         type = 0; 
 706     } 
 707  
 708     /* checks */ 
 709     switch (type) { 
 710     case LYS_CONTAINER: 
 711     case LYS_LIST: 
 712     case LYS_GROUPING: 
 713     case LYS_USES: 
 714         if (!(child->nodetype & 
 715                 (LYS_ANYDATA | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_GROUPING | LYS_LEAF | 
 716                  LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST | LYS_USES | LYS_ACTION | LYS_NOTIF))) { 
 717             LOGVAL(LYE_INCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, parent, strnodetype(child->nodetype), strnodetype(parent->nodetype)); 
 718             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 719         } 
 720         break; 
 721     case LYS_INPUT: 
 722     case LYS_OUTPUT: 
 723     case LYS_NOTIF: 
 724         if (!(child->nodetype & 
 725                 (LYS_ANYDATA | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_GROUPING | LYS_LEAF | 
 726                  LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST | LYS_USES))) { 
 727             LOGVAL(LYE_INCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, parent, strnodetype(child->nodetype), strnodetype(parent->nodetype)); 
 728             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 729         } 
 730         break; 
 731     case LYS_CHOICE: 
 732         if (!(child->nodetype & 
 733                 (LYS_ANYDATA | LYS_CASE | LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST | LYS_CHOICE))) { 
 734             LOGVAL(LYE_INCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, parent, strnodetype(child->nodetype), "choice"); 
 735             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 736         } 
 737         if (child->nodetype != LYS_CASE) { 
 738             shortcase = 1; 
 739         } 
 740         break; 
 741     case LYS_CASE: 
 742         if (!(child->nodetype & 
 743                 (LYS_ANYDATA | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST | LYS_USES))) { 
 744             LOGVAL(LYE_INCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, parent, strnodetype(child->nodetype), "case"); 
 745             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 746         } 
 747         break; 
 748     case LYS_RPC: 
 749     case LYS_ACTION: 
 750         if (!(child->nodetype & (LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT | LYS_GROUPING))) { 
 751             LOGVAL(LYE_INCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, parent, strnodetype(child->nodetype), "rpc"); 
 752             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 753         } 
 754         break; 
 755     case LYS_LEAF: 
 756     case LYS_LEAFLIST: 
 757     case LYS_ANYXML: 
 758     case LYS_ANYDATA: 
 759         LOGVAL(LYE_INCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, parent, strnodetype(child->nodetype), strnodetype(parent->nodetype)); 
 760         LOGVAL(LYE_SPEC, LY_VLOG_PREV, NULL, "The \"%s\" statement cannot have any data substatement.", 
 761                strnodetype(parent->nodetype)); 
 762         return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 763     case LYS_AUGMENT: 
 764         if (!(child->nodetype & 
 765                 (LYS_ANYDATA | LYS_CASE | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_LEAF 
 766                 | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST | LYS_USES | LYS_ACTION | LYS_NOTIF))) { 
 767             LOGVAL(LYE_INCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, parent, strnodetype(child->nodetype), strnodetype(parent->nodetype)); 
 768             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 769         } 
 770         break; 
 771     case LYS_UNKNOWN: 
 772         /* top level */ 
 773         if (!(child->nodetype & 
 774                 (LYS_ANYDATA | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_LEAF | LYS_GROUPING 
 775                 | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST | LYS_USES | LYS_RPC | LYS_NOTIF | LYS_AUGMENT))) { 
 776             LOGVAL(LYE_INCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, parent, strnodetype(child->nodetype), "(sub)module"); 
 777             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 778         } 
 779         break; 
 780     case LYS_EXT: 
 781         /* plugin-defined */ 
 782         p = lys_ext_complex_get_substmt(lys_snode2stmt(child->nodetype), (struct lys_ext_instance_complex*)parent, &info); 
 783         if (!p) { 
 784             LOGVAL(LYE_INCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, parent, strnodetype(child->nodetype), 
 785                    ((struct lys_ext_instance_complex*)parent)->def->name); 
 786             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 787         } 
 788         /* TODO check cardinality */ 
 789         break; 
 790     } 
 791  
 792     /* check identifier uniqueness */ 
 793     if (!(module->ctx->models.flags & LY_CTX_TRUSTED) && lys_check_id(child, parent, module)) { 
 794         return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 795     } 
 796  
 797     if (child->parent) { 
 798         lys_node_unlink(child); 
 799     } 
 800  
 801     if ((child->nodetype & (LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT)) && parent->nodetype != LYS_EXT) { 
 802         /* replace the implicit input/output node */ 
 803         if (child->nodetype == LYS_OUTPUT) { 
 804             inout = (struct lys_node_inout *)parent->child->next; 
 805         } else { /* LYS_INPUT */ 
 806             inout = (struct lys_node_inout *)parent->child; 
 807             parent->child = child; 
 808         } 
 809         if (inout->next) { 
 810             child->next = inout->next; 
 811             inout->next->prev = child; 
 812             inout->next = NULL; 
 813         } else { 
 814             parent->child->prev = child; 
 815         } 
 816         child->prev = inout->prev; 
 817         if (inout->prev->next) { 
 818             inout->prev->next = child; 
 819         } 
 820         inout->prev = (struct lys_node *)inout; 
 821         child->parent = parent; 
 822         inout->parent = NULL; 
 823         lys_node_free((struct lys_node *)inout, NULL, 0); 
 824     } else { 
 825         if (shortcase) { 
 826             /* create the implicit case to allow it to serve as a target of the augments, 
 827              * it won't be printed, but it will be present in the tree */ 
 828             c = calloc(1, sizeof *c); 
 829             LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!c, LOGMEM, EXIT_FAILURE); 
 830             c->name = lydict_insert(module->ctx, child->name, 0); 
 831             c->flags = LYS_IMPLICIT; 
 832             c->module = module; 
 833             c->nodetype = LYS_CASE; 
 834             c->prev = (struct lys_node*)c; 
 835             lys_node_addchild(parent, module, (struct lys_node*)c); 
 836             parent = (struct lys_node*)c; 
 837         } 
 838         /* connect the child correctly */ 
 839         if (!parent) { 
 840             if (module->data) { 
 841                 module->data->prev->next = child; 
 842                 child->prev = module->data->prev; 
 843                 module->data->prev = child; 
 844             } else { 
 845                 module->data = child; 
 846             } 
 847         } else { 
 848             pchild = lys_child(parent, child->nodetype); 
 849             assert(pchild); 
 850  
 851             child->parent = parent; 
 852             if (!(*pchild)) { 
 853                 /* the only/first child of the parent */ 
 854                 *pchild = child; 
 855                 iter = child; 
 856             } else { 
 857                 /* add a new child at the end of parent's child list */ 
 858                 iter = (*pchild)->prev; 
 859                 iter->next = child; 
 860                 child->prev = iter; 
 861             } 
 862             while (iter->next) { 
 863                 iter = iter->next; 
 864                 iter->parent = parent; 
 865             } 
 866             (*pchild)->prev = iter; 
 867         } 
 868     } 
 869  
 870     /* check config value (but ignore them in groupings and augments) */ 
 871     for (iter = parent; iter && !(iter->nodetype & (LYS_GROUPING | LYS_AUGMENT | LYS_EXT)); iter = iter->parent); 
 872     if (parent && !iter) { 
 873         for (iter = child; iter && !(iter->nodetype & (LYS_NOTIF | LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT | LYS_RPC)); iter = iter->parent); 
 874         if (!iter && (parent->flags & LYS_CONFIG_R) && (child->flags & LYS_CONFIG_W)) { 
 875             LOGVAL(LYE_INARG, LY_VLOG_LYS, child, "true", "config"); 
 876             LOGVAL(LYE_SPEC, LY_VLOG_PREV, NULL, "State nodes cannot have configuration nodes as children."); 
 877             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 878         } 
 879     } 
 880  
 881     /* propagate information about status data presence */ 
 882     if ((child->nodetype & (LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST | LYS_ANYDATA)) && 
 883             (child->flags & LYS_INCL_STATUS)) { 
 884         for(iter = parent; iter; iter = lys_parent(iter)) { 
 885             /* store it only into container or list - the only data inner nodes */ 
 886             if (iter->nodetype & (LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_LIST)) { 
 887                 if (iter->flags & LYS_INCL_STATUS) { 
 888                     /* done, someone else set it already from here */ 
 889                     break; 
 890                 } 
 891                 /* set flag about including status data */ 
 892                 iter->flags |= LYS_INCL_STATUS; 
 893             } 
 894         } 
 895     } 
 896  
 897     /* create implicit input/output nodes to have available them as possible target for augment */ 
 898     if ((child->nodetype & (LYS_RPC | LYS_ACTION)) && !child->child) { 
 899         in = calloc(1, sizeof *in); 
 900         out = calloc(1, sizeof *out); 
 901         if (!in || !out) { 
 902             LOGMEM; 
 903             free(in); 
 904             free(out); 
 905             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 906         } 
 907         in->nodetype = LYS_INPUT; 
 908         in->name = lydict_insert(child->module->ctx, "input", 5); 
 909         out->nodetype = LYS_OUTPUT; 
 910         out->name = lydict_insert(child->module->ctx, "output", 6); 
 911         in->module = out->module = child->module; 
 912         in->parent = out->parent = child; 
 913         in->flags = out->flags = LYS_IMPLICIT; 
 914         in->next = (struct lys_node *)out; 
 915         in->prev = (struct lys_node *)out; 
 916         out->prev = (struct lys_node *)in; 
 917         child->child = (struct lys_node *)in; 
 918     } 
 919     return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
 920 } 
 921  
 922 const struct lys_module * 
 923 lys_parse_mem_(struct ly_ctx *ctx, const char *data, LYS_INFORMAT format, int internal, int implement) 
 924 { 
 925     char *enlarged_data = NULL; 
 926     struct lys_module *mod = NULL; 
 927     unsigned int len; 
 928     struct ly_ctx *ctx_prev = ly_parser_data.ctx; 
 929  
 930     ly_err_clean(ctx, 1); 
 931  
 932     if (!ctx || !data) { 
 933         LOGERR(LY_EINVAL, "%s: Invalid parameter.", __func__); 
 934         return NULL; 
 935     } 
 936  
 937     /* set parser context */ 
 938     ly_parser_data.ctx = ctx; 
 939  
 940     if (!internal && format == LYS_IN_YANG) { 
 941         /* enlarge data by 2 bytes for flex */ 
 942         len = strlen(data); 
 943         enlarged_data = malloc((len + 2) * sizeof *enlarged_data); 
 944         LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!enlarged_data, LOGMEM, NULL); 
 945         memcpy(enlarged_data, data, len); 
 946         enlarged_data[len] = enlarged_data[len + 1] = '\0'; 
 947         data = enlarged_data; 
 948     } 
 949  
 950     switch (format) { 
 951     case LYS_IN_YIN: 
 952         mod = yin_read_module(ctx, data, NULL, implement); 
 953         break; 
 954     case LYS_IN_YANG: 
 955         mod = yang_read_module(ctx, data, 0, NULL, implement); 
 956         break; 
 957     default: 
 958         LOGERR(LY_EINVAL, "Invalid schema input format."); 
 959         break; 
 960     } 
 961  
 962     free(enlarged_data); 
 963  
 964     /* hack for NETCONF's edit-config's operation attribute. It is not defined in the schema, but since libyang 
 965      * implements YANG metadata (annotations), we need its definition. Because the ietf-netconf schema is not the 
 966      * internal part of libyang, we cannot add the annotation into the schema source, but we do it here to have 
 967      * the anotation definitions available in the internal schema structure. There is another hack in schema 
 968      * printers to do not print this internally added annotation. */ 
 969     if (mod && ly_strequal(mod->name, "ietf-netconf", 0)) { 
 970         if (lyp_add_ietf_netconf_annotations(mod)) { 
 971             lys_free(mod, NULL, 1); 
 972             return NULL; 
 973         } 
 974     } 
 975  
 976     /* reset parser context */ 
 977     ly_parser_data.ctx = ctx_prev; 
 978  
 979     return mod; 
 980 } 
 981  
 982 API const struct lys_module * 
 983 lys_parse_mem(struct ly_ctx *ctx, const char *data, LYS_INFORMAT format) 
 984 { 
 985     return lys_parse_mem_(ctx, data, format, 0, 1); 
 986 } 
 987  
 988 struct lys_submodule * 
 989 lys_sub_parse_mem(struct lys_module *module, const char *data, LYS_INFORMAT format, struct unres_schema *unres) 
 990 { 
 991     char *enlarged_data = NULL; 
 992     struct lys_submodule *submod = NULL; 
 993     unsigned int len; 
 994  
 995     assert(module); 
 996     assert(data); 
 997  
 998     if (format == LYS_IN_YANG) { 
 999         /* enlarge data by 2 bytes for flex */ 
1000         len = strlen(data); 
1001         enlarged_data = malloc((len + 2) * sizeof *enlarged_data); 
1002         LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!enlarged_data, LOGMEM, NULL); 
1003         memcpy(enlarged_data, data, len); 
1004         enlarged_data[len] = enlarged_data[len + 1] = '\0'; 
1005         data = enlarged_data; 
1006     } 
1007  
1008     /* get the main module */ 
1009     module = lys_main_module(module); 
1010  
1011     switch (format) { 
1012     case LYS_IN_YIN: 
1013         submod = yin_read_submodule(module, data, unres); 
1014         break; 
1015     case LYS_IN_YANG: 
1016         submod = yang_read_submodule(module, data, 0, unres); 
1017         break; 
1018     default: 
1019         assert(0); 
1020         break; 
1021     } 
1022  
1023     free(enlarged_data); 
1024     return submod; 
1025 } 
1026  
1027 API const struct lys_module * 
1028 lys_parse_path(struct ly_ctx *ctx, const char *path, LYS_INFORMAT format) 
1029 { 
1030     int fd; 
1031     const struct lys_module *ret; 
1032     const char *rev, *dot, *filename; 
1033     size_t len; 
1034  
1035     if (!ctx || !path) { 
1036         LOGERR(LY_EINVAL, "%s: Invalid parameter.", __func__); 
1037         return NULL; 
1038     } 
1039  
1040     fd = open(path, O_RDONLY); 
1041     if (fd == -1) { 
1042         LOGERR(LY_ESYS, "Opening file \"%s\" failed (%s).", path, strerror(errno)); 
1043         return NULL; 
1044     } 
1045  
1046     ret = lys_parse_fd(ctx, fd, format); 
1047     close(fd); 
1048  
1049     if (!ret) { 
1050         /* error */ 
1051         return NULL; 
1052     } 
1053  
1054     /* check that name and revision match filename */ 
1055     filename = strrchr(path, '/'); 
1056     if (!filename) { 
1057         filename = path; 
1058     } else { 
1059         filename++; 
1060     } 
1061     rev = strchr(filename, '@'); 
1062     dot = strrchr(filename, '.'); 
1063  
1064     /* name */ 
1065     len = strlen(ret->name); 
1066     if (strncmp(filename, ret->name, len) || 
1067             ((rev && rev != &filename[len]) || (!rev && dot != &filename[len]))) { 
1068         LOGWRN("File name \"%s\" does not match module name \"%s\".", filename, ret->name); 
1069     } 
1070     if (rev) { 
1071         len = dot - ++rev; 
1072         if (!ret->rev_size || len != 10 || strncmp(ret->rev[0].date, rev, len)) { 
1073             LOGWRN("File name \"%s\" does not match module revision \"%s\".", filename, 
1074                    ret->rev_size ? ret->rev[0].date : "none"); 
1075         } 
1076     } 
1077  
1078     if (!ret->filepath) { 
1079         /* store URI */ 
1080         ((struct lys_module *)ret)->filepath = lydict_insert(ctx, path, 0); 
1081     } 
1082  
1083     return ret; 
1084 } 
1085  
1086 API const struct lys_module * 
1087 lys_parse_fd(struct ly_ctx *ctx, int fd, LYS_INFORMAT format) 
1088 { 
1089     const struct lys_module *module; 
1090     size_t length; 
1091     char *addr; 
1092     char buf[PATH_MAX]; 
1093     int len; 
1094  
1095     if (!ctx || fd < 0) { 
1096         LOGERR(LY_EINVAL, "%s: Invalid parameter.", __func__); 
1097         return NULL; 
1098     } 
1099  
1100     addr = lyp_mmap(fd, format == LYS_IN_YANG ? 1 : 0, &length); 
1101     if (addr == MAP_FAILED) { 
1102         LOGERR(LY_ESYS, "Mapping file descriptor into memory failed (%s()).", __func__); 
1103         return NULL; 
1104     } else if (!addr) { 
1105         LOGERR(LY_EINVAL, "Empty schema file."); 
1106         return NULL; 
1107     } 
1108  
1109     module = lys_parse_mem_(ctx, addr, format, 1, 1); 
1110     lyp_munmap(addr, length); 
1111  
1112     if (module && !module->filepath) { 
1113         /* get URI if there is /proc */ 
1114         addr = NULL; 
1115         if (asprintf(&addr, "/proc/self/fd/%d", fd) != -1) { 
1116             if ((len = readlink(addr, buf, PATH_MAX - 1)) > 0) { 
1117                 ((struct lys_module *)module)->filepath = lydict_insert(ctx, buf, len); 
1118             } 
1119             free(addr); 
1120         } 
1121     } 
1122  
1123     return module; 
1124 } 
1125  
1126 struct lys_submodule * 
1127 lys_sub_parse_fd(struct lys_module *module, int fd, LYS_INFORMAT format, struct unres_schema *unres) 
1128 { 
1129     struct lys_submodule *submodule; 
1130     size_t length; 
1131     char *addr; 
1132  
1133     assert(module); 
1134     assert(fd >= 0); 
1135  
1136     addr = lyp_mmap(fd, format == LYS_IN_YANG ? 1 : 0, &length); 
1137     if (addr == MAP_FAILED) { 
1138         LOGERR(LY_ESYS, "Mapping file descriptor into memory failed (%s()).", __func__); 
1139         return NULL; 
1140     } else if (!addr) { 
1141         LOGERR(LY_EINVAL, "Empty submodule schema file."); 
1142         return NULL; 
1143     } 
1144  
1145     /* get the main module */ 
1146     module = lys_main_module(module); 
1147  
1148     switch (format) { 
1149     case LYS_IN_YIN: 
1150         submodule = yin_read_submodule(module, addr, unres); 
1151         break; 
1152     case LYS_IN_YANG: 
1153         submodule = yang_read_submodule(module, addr, 0, unres); 
1154         break; 
1155     default: 
1156         LOGINT; 
1157         return NULL; 
1158     } 
1159  
1160     lyp_munmap(addr, length); 
1161     return submodule; 
1162  
1163 } 
1164  
1165 int 
1166 lys_ext_iter(struct lys_ext_instance **ext, uint8_t ext_size, uint8_t start, LYEXT_SUBSTMT substmt) 
1167 { 
1168     unsigned int u; 
1169  
1170     for (u = start; u < ext_size; u++) { 
1171         if (ext[u]->insubstmt == substmt) { 
1172             return u; 
1173         } 
1174     } 
1175  
1176     return -1; 
1177 } 
1178  
1179 /* 
1180  * duplicate extension instance 
1181  */ 
1182 int 
1183 lys_ext_dup(struct lys_module *mod, struct lys_ext_instance **orig, uint8_t size, void *parent, LYEXT_PAR parent_type, 
1184             struct lys_ext_instance ***new, int shallow, struct unres_schema *unres) 
1185 { 
1186     int i; 
1187     uint8_t u = 0; 
1188     struct lys_ext_instance **result; 
1189     struct unres_ext *info, *info_orig; 
1190     size_t len; 
1191  
1192     assert(new); 
1193  
1194     if (!size) { 
1195         if (orig) { 
1196             LOGINT; 
1197             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
1198         } 
1199         (*new) = NULL; 
1200         return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
1201     } 
1202  
1203     (*new) = result = calloc(size, sizeof *result); 
1204     LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!result, LOGMEM, EXIT_FAILURE); 
1205     for (u = 0; u < size; u++) { 
1206         if (orig[u]) { 
1207             /* resolved extension instance, just duplicate it */ 
1208             switch(orig[u]->ext_type) { 
1209             case LYEXT_FLAG: 
1210                 result[u] = malloc(sizeof(struct lys_ext_instance)); 
1211                 LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(!result[u], LOGMEM, error); 
1212                 break; 
1213             case LYEXT_COMPLEX: 
1214                 len = ((struct lyext_plugin_complex*)orig[u]->def->plugin)->instance_size; 
1215                 result[u] = calloc(1, len); 
1216                 LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(!result[u], LOGMEM, error); 
1217  
1218                 ((struct lys_ext_instance_complex*)result[u])->substmt = ((struct lyext_plugin_complex*)orig[u]->def->plugin)->substmt; 
1219                 /* TODO duplicate data in extension instance content */ 
1220                 memcpy((void*)result[u] + sizeof(**orig), (void*)orig[u] + sizeof(**orig), len - sizeof(**orig)); 
1221                 break; 
1222             } 
1223             /* generic part */ 
1224             result[u]->def = orig[u]->def; 
1225             result[u]->flags = LYEXT_OPT_CONTENT; 
1226             result[u]->arg_value = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, orig[u]->arg_value, 0); 
1227             result[u]->parent = parent; 
1228             result[u]->parent_type = parent_type; 
1229             result[u]->insubstmt = orig[u]->insubstmt; 
1230             result[u]->insubstmt_index = orig[u]->insubstmt_index; 
1231             result[u]->ext_type = orig[u]->ext_type; 
1232             result[u]->priv = NULL; 
1233             result[u]->nodetype = LYS_EXT; 
1234             result[u]->module = mod; 
1235  
1236             /* extensions */ 
1237             result[u]->ext_size = orig[u]->ext_size; 
1238             if (lys_ext_dup(mod, orig[u]->ext, orig[u]->ext_size, result[u], 
1239                             LYEXT_PAR_EXTINST, &result[u]->ext, shallow, unres)) { 
1240                 goto error; 
1241             } 
1242  
1243             /* in case of shallow copy (duplication for deviation), duplicate only the link to private data 
1244              * in a new copy, otherwise (grouping instantiation) do not duplicate the private data */ 
1245             if (shallow) { 
1246                 result[u]->priv = orig[u]->priv; 
1247             } 
1248         } else { 
1249             /* original extension is not yet resolved, so duplicate it in unres */ 
1250             i = unres_schema_find(unres, -1, &orig, UNRES_EXT); 
1251             if (i == -1) { 
1252                 /* extension not found in unres */ 
1253                 LOGINT; 
1254                 goto error; 
1255             } 
1256             info_orig = unres->str_snode[i]; 
1257             info = malloc(sizeof *info); 
1258             LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(!info, LOGMEM, error); 
1259             info->datatype = info_orig->datatype; 
1260             if (info->datatype == LYS_IN_YIN) { 
1261                 info->data.yin = lyxml_dup_elem(mod->ctx, info_orig->data.yin, NULL, 1); 
1262             } /* else TODO YANG */ 
1263             info->parent = parent; 
1264             info->mod = mod; 
1265             info->parent_type = parent_type; 
1266             info->ext_index = u; 
1267             if (unres_schema_add_node(info->mod, unres, new, UNRES_EXT, (struct lys_node *)info) == -1) { 
1268                 goto error; 
1269             } 
1270         } 
1271     } 
1272  
1273     return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
1274  
1275 error: 
1276     (*new) = NULL; 
1277     lys_extension_instances_free(mod->ctx, result, u, NULL); 
1278     return EXIT_FAILURE; 
1279 } 
1280  
1281 static struct lys_restr * 
1282 lys_restr_dup(struct lys_module *mod, struct lys_restr *old, int size, int shallow, struct unres_schema *unres) 
1283 { 
1284     struct lys_restr *result; 
1285     int i; 
1286  
1287     if (!size) { 
1288         return NULL; 
1289     } 
1290  
1291     result = calloc(size, sizeof *result); 
1292     LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!result, LOGMEM, NULL); 
1293  
1294     for (i = 0; i < size; i++) { 
1295         result[i].ext_size = old[i].ext_size; 
1296         lys_ext_dup(mod, old[i].ext, old[i].ext_size, &result[i], LYEXT_PAR_RESTR, &result[i].ext, shallow, unres); 
1297         result[i].expr = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, old[i].expr, 0); 
1298         result[i].dsc = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, old[i].dsc, 0); 
1299         result[i].ref = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, old[i].ref, 0); 
1300         result[i].eapptag = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, old[i].eapptag, 0); 
1301         result[i].emsg = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, old[i].emsg, 0); 
1302     } 
1303  
1304     return result; 
1305 } 
1306  
1307 void 
1308 lys_restr_free(struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lys_restr *restr, 
1309                void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
1310 { 
1311     assert(ctx); 
1312     if (!restr) { 
1313         return; 
1314     } 
1315  
1316     lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, restr->ext, restr->ext_size, private_destructor); 
1317     lydict_remove(ctx, restr->expr); 
1318     lydict_remove(ctx, restr->dsc); 
1319     lydict_remove(ctx, restr->ref); 
1320     lydict_remove(ctx, restr->eapptag); 
1321     lydict_remove(ctx, restr->emsg); 
1322 } 
1323  
1324 void 
1325 lys_iffeature_free(struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lys_iffeature *iffeature, uint8_t iffeature_size, 
1326                    int shallow, void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
1327 { 
1328     uint8_t i; 
1329  
1330     for (i = 0; i < iffeature_size; ++i) { 
1331         lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, iffeature[i].ext, iffeature[i].ext_size, private_destructor); 
1332         if (!shallow) { 
1333             free(iffeature[i].expr); 
1334             free(iffeature[i].features); 
1335         } 
1336     } 
1337     free(iffeature); 
1338 } 
1339  
1340 static int 
1341 type_dup(struct lys_module *mod, struct lys_node *parent, struct lys_type *new, struct lys_type *old, 
1342          LY_DATA_TYPE base, int in_grp, int shallow, struct unres_schema *unres) 
1343 { 
1344     int i; 
1345     unsigned int u; 
1346  
1347     switch (base) { 
1348     case LY_TYPE_BINARY: 
1349         if (old->info.binary.length) { 
1350             new->info.binary.length = lys_restr_dup(mod, old->info.binary.length, 1, shallow, unres); 
1351         } 
1352         break; 
1353  
1354     case LY_TYPE_BITS: 
1355         new->info.bits.count = old->info.bits.count; 
1356         if (new->info.bits.count) { 
1357             new->info.bits.bit = calloc(new->info.bits.count, sizeof *new->info.bits.bit); 
1358             LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!new->info.bits.bit, LOGMEM, -1); 
1359  
1360             for (u = 0; u < new->info.bits.count; u++) { 
1361                 new->info.bits.bit[u].name = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, old->info.bits.bit[u].name, 0); 
1362                 new->info.bits.bit[u].dsc = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, old->info.bits.bit[u].dsc, 0); 
1363                 new->info.bits.bit[u].ref = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, old->info.bits.bit[u].ref, 0); 
1364                 new->info.bits.bit[u].flags = old->info.bits.bit[u].flags; 
1365                 new->info.bits.bit[u].pos = old->info.bits.bit[u].pos; 
1366                 new->info.bits.bit[u].ext_size = old->info.bits.bit[u].ext_size; 
1367                 if (lys_ext_dup(mod, old->info.bits.bit[u].ext, old->info.bits.bit[u].ext_size, 
1368                                 &new->info.bits.bit[u], LYEXT_PAR_TYPE_BIT, 
1369                                 &new->info.bits.bit[u].ext, shallow, unres)) { 
1370                     return -1; 
1371                 } 
1372             } 
1373         } 
1374         break; 
1375  
1376     case LY_TYPE_DEC64: 
1377         new->info.dec64.dig = old->info.dec64.dig; 
1378         new->info.dec64.div = old->info.dec64.div; 
1379         if (old->info.dec64.range) { 
1380             new->info.dec64.range = lys_restr_dup(mod, old->info.dec64.range, 1, shallow, unres); 
1381         } 
1382         break; 
1383  
1384     case LY_TYPE_ENUM: 
1385         new->info.enums.count = old->info.enums.count; 
1386         if (new->info.enums.count) { 
1387             new->info.enums.enm = calloc(new->info.enums.count, sizeof *new->info.enums.enm); 
1388             LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!new->info.enums.enm, LOGMEM, -1); 
1389  
1390             for (u = 0; u < new->info.enums.count; u++) { 
1391                 new->info.enums.enm[u].name = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, old->info.enums.enm[u].name, 0); 
1392                 new->info.enums.enm[u].dsc = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, old->info.enums.enm[u].dsc, 0); 
1393                 new->info.enums.enm[u].ref = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, old->info.enums.enm[u].ref, 0); 
1394                 new->info.enums.enm[u].flags = old->info.enums.enm[u].flags; 
1395                 new->info.enums.enm[u].value = old->info.enums.enm[u].value; 
1396                 new->info.enums.enm[u].ext_size = old->info.enums.enm[u].ext_size; 
1397                 if (lys_ext_dup(mod, old->info.enums.enm[u].ext, old->info.enums.enm[u].ext_size, 
1398                                 &new->info.enums.enm[u], LYEXT_PAR_TYPE_ENUM, 
1399                                 &new->info.enums.enm[u].ext, shallow, unres)) { 
1400                     return -1; 
1401                 } 
1402             } 
1403         } 
1404         break; 
1405  
1406     case LY_TYPE_IDENT: 
1407         new->info.ident.count = old->info.ident.count; 
1408         if (old->info.ident.count) { 
1409             new->info.ident.ref = malloc(old->info.ident.count * sizeof *new->info.ident.ref); 
1410             LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!new->info.ident.ref, LOGMEM, -1); 
1411             memcpy(new->info.ident.ref, old->info.ident.ref, old->info.ident.count * sizeof *new->info.ident.ref); 
1412         } else { 
1413             /* there can be several unresolved base identities, duplicate them all */ 
1414             i = -1; 
1415             do { 
1416                 i = unres_schema_find(unres, i, old, UNRES_TYPE_IDENTREF); 
1417                 if (i != -1) { 
1418                     if (unres_schema_add_str(mod, unres, new, UNRES_TYPE_IDENTREF, unres->str_snode[i]) == -1) { 
1419                         return -1; 
1420                     } 
1421                 } 
1422                 --i; 
1423             } while (i > -1); 
1424         } 
1425         break; 
1426  
1427     case LY_TYPE_INST: 
1428         new->info.inst.req = old->info.inst.req; 
1429         break; 
1430  
1431     case LY_TYPE_INT8: 
1432     case LY_TYPE_INT16: 
1433     case LY_TYPE_INT32: 
1434     case LY_TYPE_INT64: 
1435     case LY_TYPE_UINT8: 
1436     case LY_TYPE_UINT16: 
1437     case LY_TYPE_UINT32: 
1438     case LY_TYPE_UINT64: 
1439         if (old->info.num.range) { 
1440             new->info.num.range = lys_restr_dup(mod, old->info.num.range, 1, shallow, unres); 
1441         } 
1442         break; 
1443  
1444     case LY_TYPE_LEAFREF: 
1445         if (old->info.lref.path) { 
1446             new->info.lref.path = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, old->info.lref.path, 0); 
1447             if (!in_grp && unres_schema_add_node(mod, unres, new, UNRES_TYPE_LEAFREF, parent) == -1) { 
1448                 return -1; 
1449             } 
1450         } 
1451         break; 
1452  
1453     case LY_TYPE_STRING: 
1454         if (old->info.str.length) { 
1455             new->info.str.length = lys_restr_dup(mod, old->info.str.length, 1, shallow, unres); 
1456         } 
1457         if (old->info.str.pat_count) { 
1458             new->info.str.patterns = lys_restr_dup(mod, old->info.str.patterns, old->info.str.pat_count, shallow, unres); 
1459             new->info.str.pat_count = old->info.str.pat_count; 
1460 #ifdef LY_ENABLED_CACHE 
1461             if (!in_grp) { 
1462                 new->info.str.patterns_pcre = malloc(new->info.str.pat_count * 2 * sizeof *new->info.str.patterns_pcre); 
1463                 LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!new->info.str.patterns_pcre, LOGMEM, -1); 
1464                 for (u = 0; u < new->info.str.pat_count; u++) { 
1465                     if (lyp_precompile_pattern(&new->info.str.patterns[u].expr[1], 
1466                                               (pcre**)&new->info.str.patterns_pcre[2 * u], 
1467                                               (pcre_extra**)&new->info.str.patterns_pcre[2 * u + 1])) { 
1468                         free(new->info.str.patterns_pcre); 
1469                         new->info.str.patterns_pcre = NULL; 
1470                         return -1; 
1471                     } 
1472                 } 
1473             } 
1474 #endif 
1475         } 
1476         break; 
1477  
1478     case LY_TYPE_UNION: 
1479         new->info.uni.has_ptr_type = old->info.uni.has_ptr_type; 
1480         new->info.uni.count = old->info.uni.count; 
1481         if (new->info.uni.count) { 
1482             new->info.uni.types = calloc(new->info.uni.count, sizeof *new->info.uni.types); 
1483             LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!new->info.uni.types, LOGMEM, -1); 
1484  
1485             for (u = 0; u < new->info.uni.count; u++) { 
1486                 if (lys_type_dup(mod, parent, &(new->info.uni.types[u]), &(old->info.uni.types[u]), in_grp, 
1487                         shallow, unres)) { 
1488                     return -1; 
1489                 } 
1490             } 
1491         } 
1492         break; 
1493  
1494     default: 
1495         /* nothing to do for LY_TYPE_BOOL, LY_TYPE_EMPTY */ 
1496         break; 
1497     } 
1498  
1499     return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
1500 } 
1501  
1502 struct yang_type * 
1503 lys_yang_type_dup(struct lys_module *module, struct lys_node *parent, struct yang_type *old, struct lys_type *type, 
1504                   int in_grp, int shallow, struct unres_schema *unres) 
1505 { 
1506     struct yang_type *new; 
1507  
1508     new = calloc(1, sizeof *new); 
1509     LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!new, LOGMEM, NULL); 
1510     new->flags = old->flags; 
1511     new->base = old->base; 
1512     new->name = lydict_insert(module->ctx, old->name, 0); 
1513     new->type = type; 
1514     if (!new->name) { 
1515         LOGMEM; 
1516         goto error; 
1517     } 
1518     if (type_dup(module, parent, type, old->type, new->base, in_grp, shallow, unres)) { 
1519         new->type->base = new->base; 
1520         lys_type_free(module->ctx, new->type, NULL); 
1521         memset(&new->type->info, 0, sizeof new->type->info); 
1522         goto error; 
1523     } 
1524     return new; 
1525  
1526     error: 
1527     free(new); 
1528     return NULL; 
1529 } 
1530  
1531 int 
1532 lys_copy_union_leafrefs(struct lys_module *mod, struct lys_node *parent, struct lys_type *type, struct lys_type *prev_new, 
1533                         struct unres_schema *unres) 
1534 { 
1535     struct lys_type new; 
1536     unsigned int i, top_type; 
1537     struct lys_ext_instance **ext; 
1538     uint8_t ext_size; 
1539     void *reloc; 
1540  
1541     if (!prev_new) { 
1542         /* this is the "top-level" type, meaning it is a real type and no typedef directly above */ 
1543         top_type = 1; 
1544  
1545         memset(&new, 0, sizeof new); 
1546  
1547         new.module_name = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, type->module_name, 0); 
1548         new.base = type->base; 
1549         new.parent = (struct lys_tpdf *)parent; 
1550  
1551         prev_new = &new; 
1552     } else { 
1553         /* this is not top-level type, just a type of a typedef */ 
1554         top_type = 0; 
1555     } 
1556  
1557     assert(type->der); 
1558     if (type->der->module) { 
1559         /* typedef, skip it, but keep the extensions */ 
1560         ext_size = type->ext_size; 
1561         if (lys_ext_dup(mod, type->ext, type->ext_size, prev_new, LYEXT_PAR_TYPE, &ext, 0, unres)) { 
1562             return -1; 
1563         } 
1564         if (prev_new->ext) { 
1565             reloc = realloc(prev_new->ext, (prev_new->ext_size + ext_size) * sizeof *prev_new->ext); 
1566             LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!reloc, LOGMEM, -1); 
1567             prev_new->ext = reloc; 
1568  
1569             memcpy(prev_new->ext + prev_new->ext_size, ext, ext_size * sizeof *ext); 
1570             free(ext); 
1571  
1572             prev_new->ext_size += ext_size; 
1573         } else { 
1574             prev_new->ext = ext; 
1575             prev_new->ext_size = ext_size; 
1576         } 
1577  
1578         if (lys_copy_union_leafrefs(mod, parent, &type->der->type, prev_new, unres)) { 
1579             return -1; 
1580         } 
1581     } else { 
1582         /* type, just make a deep copy */ 
1583         switch (type->base) { 
1584         case LY_TYPE_UNION: 
1585             prev_new->info.uni.has_ptr_type = type->info.uni.has_ptr_type; 
1586             prev_new->info.uni.count = type->info.uni.count; 
1587             /* this cannot be a typedef anymore */ 
1588             assert(prev_new->info.uni.count); 
1589  
1590             prev_new->info.uni.types = calloc(prev_new->info.uni.count, sizeof *prev_new->info.uni.types); 
1591             LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!prev_new->info.uni.types, LOGMEM, -1); 
1592  
1593             for (i = 0; i < prev_new->info.uni.count; i++) { 
1594                 if (lys_copy_union_leafrefs(mod, parent, &(type->info.uni.types[i]), &(prev_new->info.uni.types[i]), unres)) { 
1595                     return -1; 
1596                 } 
1597             } 
1598  
1599             prev_new->der = type->der; 
1600             break; 
1601         default: 
1602             if (lys_type_dup(mod, parent, prev_new, type, 0, 0, unres)) { 
1603                 return -1; 
1604             } 
1605             break; 
1606         } 
1607     } 
1608  
1609     if (top_type) { 
1610         memcpy(type, prev_new, sizeof *type); 
1611     } 
1612     return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
1613 } 
1614  
1615 API const void * 
1616 lys_ext_instance_substmt(const struct lys_ext_instance *ext) 
1617 { 
1618     if (!ext) { 
1619         return NULL; 
1620     } 
1621  
1622     switch (ext->insubstmt) { 
1623     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_SELF: 
1624     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_MODIFIER: 
1625     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_VERSION: 
1626         return NULL; 
1627     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_ARGUMENT: 
1628         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_EXT) { 
1629             return ((struct lys_ext_instance*)ext->parent)->arg_value; 
1630         } 
1631         break; 
1632     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_BASE: 
1633         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_TYPE) { 
1634             return ((struct lys_type*)ext->parent)->info.ident.ref[ext->insubstmt_index]; 
1635         } else if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_IDENT) { 
1636             return ((struct lys_ident*)ext->parent)->base[ext->insubstmt_index]; 
1637         } 
1638         break; 
1639     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_BELONGSTO: 
1640         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_MODULE && ((struct lys_module*)ext->parent)->type) { 
1641             return ((struct lys_submodule*)ext->parent)->belongsto; 
1642         } 
1643         break; 
1644     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_CONFIG: 
1645     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_MANDATORY: 
1646         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_NODE) { 
1647             return &((struct lys_node*)ext->parent)->flags; 
1648         } else if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_DEVIATE) { 
1649             return &((struct lys_deviate*)ext->parent)->flags; 
1650         } else if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_REFINE) { 
1651             return &((struct lys_refine*)ext->parent)->flags; 
1652         } 
1653         break; 
1654     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_CONTACT: 
1655         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_MODULE) { 
1656             return ((struct lys_module*)ext->parent)->contact; 
1657         } 
1658         break; 
1659     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_DEFAULT: 
1660         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_NODE) { 
1661             switch (((struct lys_node*)ext->parent)->nodetype) { 
1662             case LYS_LEAF: 
1663             case LYS_LEAFLIST: 
1664                 /* in case of leaf, the index is supposed to be 0, so it will return the 
1665                  * correct pointer despite the leaf structure does not have dflt as array */ 
1666                 return ((struct lys_node_leaflist*)ext->parent)->dflt[ext->insubstmt_index]; 
1667             case LYS_CHOICE: 
1668                 return ((struct lys_node_choice*)ext->parent)->dflt; 
1669             default: 
1670                 /* internal error */ 
1671                 break; 
1672             } 
1673         } else if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_TPDF) { 
1674             return ((struct lys_tpdf*)ext->parent)->dflt; 
1675         } else if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_DEVIATE) { 
1676             return ((struct lys_deviate*)ext->parent)->dflt[ext->insubstmt_index]; 
1677         } else if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_REFINE) { 
1678             return &((struct lys_refine*)ext->parent)->dflt[ext->insubstmt_index]; 
1679         } 
1680         break; 
1681     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_DESCRIPTION: 
1682         switch (ext->parent_type) { 
1683         case LYEXT_PAR_NODE: 
1684             return ((struct lys_node*)ext->parent)->dsc; 
1685         case LYEXT_PAR_MODULE: 
1686             return ((struct lys_module*)ext->parent)->dsc; 
1687         case LYEXT_PAR_IMPORT: 
1688             return ((struct lys_import*)ext->parent)->dsc; 
1689         case LYEXT_PAR_INCLUDE: 
1690             return ((struct lys_include*)ext->parent)->dsc; 
1691         case LYEXT_PAR_EXT: 
1692             return ((struct lys_ext*)ext->parent)->dsc; 
1693         case LYEXT_PAR_FEATURE: 
1694             return ((struct lys_feature*)ext->parent)->dsc; 
1695         case LYEXT_PAR_TPDF: 
1696             return ((struct lys_tpdf*)ext->parent)->dsc; 
1697         case LYEXT_PAR_TYPE_BIT: 
1698             return ((struct lys_type_bit*)ext->parent)->dsc; 
1699         case LYEXT_PAR_TYPE_ENUM: 
1700             return ((struct lys_type_enum*)ext->parent)->dsc; 
1701         case LYEXT_PAR_RESTR: 
1702             return ((struct lys_restr*)ext->parent)->dsc; 
1703         case LYEXT_PAR_WHEN: 
1704             return ((struct lys_when*)ext->parent)->dsc; 
1705         case LYEXT_PAR_IDENT: 
1706             return ((struct lys_ident*)ext->parent)->dsc; 
1707         case LYEXT_PAR_DEVIATION: 
1708             return ((struct lys_deviation*)ext->parent)->dsc; 
1709         case LYEXT_PAR_REVISION: 
1710             return ((struct lys_revision*)ext->parent)->dsc; 
1711         case LYEXT_PAR_REFINE: 
1712             return ((struct lys_refine*)ext->parent)->dsc; 
1713         default: 
1714             break; 
1715         } 
1716         break; 
1717     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_ERRTAG: 
1718         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_RESTR) { 
1719             return ((struct lys_restr*)ext->parent)->eapptag; 
1720         } 
1721         break; 
1722     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_ERRMSG: 
1723         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_RESTR) { 
1724             return ((struct lys_restr*)ext->parent)->emsg; 
1725         } 
1726         break; 
1727     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_DIGITS: 
1728         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_TYPE && ((struct lys_type*)ext->parent)->base == LY_TYPE_DEC64) { 
1729             return &((struct lys_type*)ext->parent)->info.dec64.dig; 
1730         } 
1731         break; 
1732     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_KEY: 
1733         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_NODE && ((struct lys_node*)ext->parent)->nodetype == LYS_LIST) { 
1734             return ((struct lys_node_list*)ext->parent)->keys; 
1735         } 
1736         break; 
1737     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_MAX: 
1738         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_NODE) { 
1739             if (((struct lys_node*)ext->parent)->nodetype == LYS_LIST) { 
1740                 return &((struct lys_node_list*)ext->parent)->max; 
1741             } else if (((struct lys_node*)ext->parent)->nodetype == LYS_LEAFLIST) { 
1742                 return &((struct lys_node_leaflist*)ext->parent)->max; 
1743             } 
1744         } else if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_REFINE) { 
1745             return &((struct lys_refine*)ext->parent)->mod.list.max; 
1746         } 
1747         break; 
1748     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_MIN: 
1749         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_NODE) { 
1750             if (((struct lys_node*)ext->parent)->nodetype == LYS_LIST) { 
1751                 return &((struct lys_node_list*)ext->parent)->min; 
1752             } else if (((struct lys_node*)ext->parent)->nodetype == LYS_LEAFLIST) { 
1753                 return &((struct lys_node_leaflist*)ext->parent)->min; 
1754             } 
1755         } else if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_REFINE) { 
1756             return &((struct lys_refine*)ext->parent)->mod.list.min; 
1757         } 
1758         break; 
1759     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_NAMESPACE: 
1760         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_MODULE && !((struct lys_module*)ext->parent)->type) { 
1761             return ((struct lys_module*)ext->parent)->ns; 
1762         } 
1763         break; 
1764     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_ORDEREDBY: 
1765         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_NODE && 
1766                 (((struct lys_node*)ext->parent)->nodetype & (LYS_LIST | LYS_LEAFLIST))) { 
1767             return &((struct lys_node_list*)ext->parent)->flags; 
1768         } 
1769         break; 
1770     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_ORGANIZATION: 
1771         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_MODULE) { 
1772             return ((struct lys_module*)ext->parent)->org; 
1773         } 
1774         break; 
1775     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_PATH: 
1776         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_TYPE && ((struct lys_type*)ext->parent)->base == LY_TYPE_LEAFREF) { 
1777             return ((struct lys_type*)ext->parent)->info.lref.path; 
1778         } 
1779         break; 
1780     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_POSITION: 
1781         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_TYPE_BIT) { 
1782             return &((struct lys_type_bit*)ext->parent)->pos; 
1783         } 
1784         break; 
1785     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_PREFIX: 
1786         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_MODULE) { 
1787             /* covers also lys_submodule */ 
1788             return ((struct lys_module*)ext->parent)->prefix; 
1789         } else if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_IMPORT) { 
1790             return ((struct lys_import*)ext->parent)->prefix; 
1791         } 
1792         break; 
1793     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_PRESENCE: 
1794         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_NODE && ((struct lys_node*)ext->parent)->nodetype == LYS_CONTAINER) { 
1795             return ((struct lys_node_container*)ext->parent)->presence; 
1796         } else if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_REFINE) { 
1797             return ((struct lys_refine*)ext->parent)->mod.presence; 
1798         } 
1799         break; 
1800     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_REFERENCE: 
1801         switch (ext->parent_type) { 
1802         case LYEXT_PAR_NODE: 
1803             return ((struct lys_node*)ext->parent)->ref; 
1804         case LYEXT_PAR_MODULE: 
1805             return ((struct lys_module*)ext->parent)->ref; 
1806         case LYEXT_PAR_IMPORT: 
1807             return ((struct lys_import*)ext->parent)->ref; 
1808         case LYEXT_PAR_INCLUDE: 
1809             return ((struct lys_include*)ext->parent)->ref; 
1810         case LYEXT_PAR_EXT: 
1811             return ((struct lys_ext*)ext->parent)->ref; 
1812         case LYEXT_PAR_FEATURE: 
1813             return ((struct lys_feature*)ext->parent)->ref; 
1814         case LYEXT_PAR_TPDF: 
1815             return ((struct lys_tpdf*)ext->parent)->ref; 
1816         case LYEXT_PAR_TYPE_BIT: 
1817             return ((struct lys_type_bit*)ext->parent)->ref; 
1818         case LYEXT_PAR_TYPE_ENUM: 
1819             return ((struct lys_type_enum*)ext->parent)->ref; 
1820         case LYEXT_PAR_RESTR: 
1821             return ((struct lys_restr*)ext->parent)->ref; 
1822         case LYEXT_PAR_WHEN: 
1823             return ((struct lys_when*)ext->parent)->ref; 
1824         case LYEXT_PAR_IDENT: 
1825             return ((struct lys_ident*)ext->parent)->ref; 
1826         case LYEXT_PAR_DEVIATION: 
1827             return ((struct lys_deviation*)ext->parent)->ref; 
1828         case LYEXT_PAR_REVISION: 
1829             return ((struct lys_revision*)ext->parent)->ref; 
1830         case LYEXT_PAR_REFINE: 
1831             return ((struct lys_refine*)ext->parent)->ref; 
1832         default: 
1833             break; 
1834         } 
1835         break; 
1836     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_REQINSTANCE: 
1837         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_TYPE) { 
1838             if (((struct lys_type*)ext->parent)->base == LY_TYPE_LEAFREF) { 
1839                 return &((struct lys_type*)ext->parent)->info.lref.req; 
1840             } else if (((struct lys_type*)ext->parent)->base == LY_TYPE_INST) { 
1841                 return &((struct lys_type*)ext->parent)->info.inst.req; 
1842             } 
1843         } 
1844         break; 
1845     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_REVISIONDATE: 
1846         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_IMPORT) { 
1847             return ((struct lys_import*)ext->parent)->rev; 
1848         } else if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_INCLUDE) { 
1849             return ((struct lys_include*)ext->parent)->rev; 
1850         } 
1851         break; 
1852     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_STATUS: 
1853         switch (ext->parent_type) { 
1854         case LYEXT_PAR_NODE: 
1855         case LYEXT_PAR_IDENT: 
1856         case LYEXT_PAR_TPDF: 
1857         case LYEXT_PAR_EXT: 
1858         case LYEXT_PAR_FEATURE: 
1859         case LYEXT_PAR_TYPE_ENUM: 
1860         case LYEXT_PAR_TYPE_BIT: 
1861             /* in all structures the flags member is at the same offset */ 
1862             return &((struct lys_node*)ext->parent)->flags; 
1863         default: 
1864             break; 
1865         } 
1866         break; 
1867     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_UNIQUE: 
1868         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_DEVIATE) { 
1869             return &((struct lys_deviate*)ext->parent)->unique[ext->insubstmt_index]; 
1870         } else if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_NODE && ((struct lys_node*)ext->parent)->nodetype == LYS_LIST) { 
1871             return &((struct lys_node_list*)ext->parent)->unique[ext->insubstmt_index]; 
1872         } 
1873         break; 
1874     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_UNITS: 
1875         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_NODE && 
1876                 (((struct lys_node*)ext->parent)->nodetype & (LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST))) { 
1877             /* units is at the same offset in both lys_node_leaf and lys_node_leaflist */ 
1878             return ((struct lys_node_leaf*)ext->parent)->units; 
1879         } else if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_TPDF) { 
1880             return ((struct lys_tpdf*)ext->parent)->units; 
1881         } else if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_DEVIATE) { 
1882             return ((struct lys_deviate*)ext->parent)->units; 
1883         } 
1884         break; 
1885     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_VALUE: 
1886         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_TYPE_ENUM) { 
1887             return &((struct lys_type_enum*)ext->parent)->value; 
1888         } 
1889         break; 
1890     case LYEXT_SUBSTMT_YINELEM: 
1891         if (ext->parent_type == LYEXT_PAR_EXT) { 
1892             return &((struct lys_ext*)ext->parent)->flags; 
1893         } 
1894         break; 
1895     } 
1896     LOGINT; 
1897     return NULL; 
1898 } 
1899  
1900 static int 
1901 lys_type_dup(struct lys_module *mod, struct lys_node *parent, struct lys_type *new, struct lys_type *old, 
1902             int in_grp, int shallow, struct unres_schema *unres) 
1903 { 
1904     int i; 
1905  
1906     new->module_name = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, old->module_name, 0); 
1907     new->base = old->base; 
1908     new->der = old->der; 
1909     new->parent = (struct lys_tpdf *)parent; 
1910     new->ext_size = old->ext_size; 
1911     if (lys_ext_dup(mod, old->ext, old->ext_size, new, LYEXT_PAR_TYPE, &new->ext, shallow, unres)) { 
1912         return -1; 
1913     } 
1914  
1915     i = unres_schema_find(unres, -1, old, UNRES_TYPE_DER); 
1916     if (i != -1) { 
1917         /* HACK (serious one) for unres */ 
1918         /* nothing else we can do but duplicate it immediately */ 
1919         if (((struct lyxml_elem *)old->der)->flags & LY_YANG_STRUCTURE_FLAG) { 
1920             new->der = (struct lys_tpdf *)lys_yang_type_dup(mod, parent, (struct yang_type *)old->der, new, in_grp, 
1921                                                             shallow, unres); 
1922         } else { 
1923             new->der = (struct lys_tpdf *)lyxml_dup_elem(mod->ctx, (struct lyxml_elem *)old->der, NULL, 1); 
1924         } 
1925         /* all these unres additions can fail even though they did not before */ 
1926         if (!new->der || (unres_schema_add_node(mod, unres, new, UNRES_TYPE_DER, parent) == -1)) { 
1927             return -1; 
1928         } 
1929         return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
1930     } 
1931  
1932     return type_dup(mod, parent, new, old, new->base, in_grp, shallow, unres); 
1933 } 
1934  
1935 void 
1936 lys_type_free(struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lys_type *type, 
1937               void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
1938 { 
1939     unsigned int i; 
1940  
1941     assert(ctx); 
1942     if (!type) { 
1943         return; 
1944     } 
1945  
1946     lydict_remove(ctx, type->module_name); 
1947  
1948     lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, type->ext, type->ext_size, private_destructor); 
1949  
1950     switch (type->base) { 
1951     case LY_TYPE_BINARY: 
1952         lys_restr_free(ctx, type->info.binary.length, private_destructor); 
1953         free(type->info.binary.length); 
1954         break; 
1955     case LY_TYPE_BITS: 
1956         for (i = 0; i < type->info.bits.count; i++) { 
1957             lydict_remove(ctx, type->info.bits.bit[i].name); 
1958             lydict_remove(ctx, type->info.bits.bit[i].dsc); 
1959             lydict_remove(ctx, type->info.bits.bit[i].ref); 
1960             lys_iffeature_free(ctx, type->info.bits.bit[i].iffeature, type->info.bits.bit[i].iffeature_size, 0, 
1961                                private_destructor); 
1962             lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, type->info.bits.bit[i].ext, type->info.bits.bit[i].ext_size, 
1963                                          private_destructor); 
1964         } 
1965         free(type->info.bits.bit); 
1966         break; 
1967  
1968     case LY_TYPE_DEC64: 
1969         lys_restr_free(ctx, type->info.dec64.range, private_destructor); 
1970         free(type->info.dec64.range); 
1971         break; 
1972  
1973     case LY_TYPE_ENUM: 
1974         for (i = 0; i < type->info.enums.count; i++) { 
1975             lydict_remove(ctx, type->info.enums.enm[i].name); 
1976             lydict_remove(ctx, type->info.enums.enm[i].dsc); 
1977             lydict_remove(ctx, type->info.enums.enm[i].ref); 
1978             lys_iffeature_free(ctx, type->info.enums.enm[i].iffeature, type->info.enums.enm[i].iffeature_size, 0, 
1979                                private_destructor); 
1980             lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, type->info.enums.enm[i].ext, type->info.enums.enm[i].ext_size, 
1981                                          private_destructor); 
1982         } 
1983         free(type->info.enums.enm); 
1984         break; 
1985  
1986     case LY_TYPE_INT8: 
1987     case LY_TYPE_INT16: 
1988     case LY_TYPE_INT32: 
1989     case LY_TYPE_INT64: 
1990     case LY_TYPE_UINT8: 
1991     case LY_TYPE_UINT16: 
1992     case LY_TYPE_UINT32: 
1993     case LY_TYPE_UINT64: 
1994         lys_restr_free(ctx, type->info.num.range, private_destructor); 
1995         free(type->info.num.range); 
1996         break; 
1997  
1998     case LY_TYPE_LEAFREF: 
1999         lydict_remove(ctx, type->info.lref.path); 
2000         break; 
2001  
2002     case LY_TYPE_STRING: 
2003         lys_restr_free(ctx, type->info.str.length, private_destructor); 
2004         free(type->info.str.length); 
2005         for (i = 0; i < type->info.str.pat_count; i++) { 
2006             lys_restr_free(ctx, &type->info.str.patterns[i], private_destructor); 
2007 #ifdef LY_ENABLED_CACHE 
2008             if (type->info.str.patterns_pcre) { 
2009                 pcre_free((pcre*)type->info.str.patterns_pcre[2 * i]); 
2010                 pcre_free_study((pcre_extra*)type->info.str.patterns_pcre[2 * i + 1]); 
2011             } 
2012 #endif 
2013         } 
2014         free(type->info.str.patterns); 
2015 #ifdef LY_ENABLED_CACHE 
2016         free(type->info.str.patterns_pcre); 
2017 #endif 
2018         break; 
2019  
2020     case LY_TYPE_UNION: 
2021         for (i = 0; i < type->info.uni.count; i++) { 
2022             lys_type_free(ctx, &type->info.uni.types[i], private_destructor); 
2023         } 
2024         free(type->info.uni.types); 
2025         break; 
2026  
2027     case LY_TYPE_IDENT: 
2028         free(type->info.ident.ref); 
2029         break; 
2030  
2031     default: 
2032         /* nothing to do for LY_TYPE_INST, LY_TYPE_BOOL, LY_TYPE_EMPTY */ 
2033         break; 
2034     } 
2035 } 
2036  
2037 static void 
2038 lys_tpdf_free(struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lys_tpdf *tpdf, 
2039               void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
2040 { 
2041     assert(ctx); 
2042     if (!tpdf) { 
2043         return; 
2044     } 
2045  
2046     lydict_remove(ctx, tpdf->name); 
2047     lydict_remove(ctx, tpdf->dsc); 
2048     lydict_remove(ctx, tpdf->ref); 
2049  
2050     lys_type_free(ctx, &tpdf->type, private_destructor); 
2051  
2052     lydict_remove(ctx, tpdf->units); 
2053     lydict_remove(ctx, tpdf->dflt); 
2054  
2055     lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, tpdf->ext, tpdf->ext_size, private_destructor); 
2056 } 
2057  
2058 static struct lys_when * 
2059 lys_when_dup(struct lys_module *mod, struct lys_when *old, int shallow, struct unres_schema *unres) 
2060 { 
2061     struct lys_when *new; 
2062  
2063     if (!old) { 
2064         return NULL; 
2065     } 
2066  
2067     new = calloc(1, sizeof *new); 
2068     LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!new, LOGMEM, NULL); 
2069     new->cond = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, old->cond, 0); 
2070     new->dsc = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, old->dsc, 0); 
2071     new->ref = lydict_insert(mod->ctx, old->ref, 0); 
2072     new->ext_size = old->ext_size; 
2073     lys_ext_dup(mod, old->ext, old->ext_size, new, LYEXT_PAR_WHEN, &new->ext, shallow, unres); 
2074  
2075     return new; 
2076 } 
2077  
2078 void 
2079 lys_when_free(struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lys_when *w, 
2080               void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
2081 { 
2082     if (!w) { 
2083         return; 
2084     } 
2085  
2086     lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, w->ext, w->ext_size, private_destructor); 
2087     lydict_remove(ctx, w->cond); 
2088     lydict_remove(ctx, w->dsc); 
2089     lydict_remove(ctx, w->ref); 
2090  
2091     free(w); 
2092 } 
2093  
2094 static void 
2095 lys_augment_free(struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lys_node_augment *aug, 
2096                  void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
2097 { 
2098     struct lys_node *next, *sub; 
2099  
2100     /* children from a resolved augment are freed under the target node */ 
2101     if (!aug->target || (aug->flags & LYS_NOTAPPLIED)) { 
2102         LY_TREE_FOR_SAFE(aug->child, next, sub) { 
2103             lys_node_free(sub, private_destructor, 0); 
2104         } 
2105     } 
2106  
2107     lydict_remove(ctx, aug->target_name); 
2108     lydict_remove(ctx, aug->dsc); 
2109     lydict_remove(ctx, aug->ref); 
2110  
2111     lys_iffeature_free(ctx, aug->iffeature, aug->iffeature_size, 0, private_destructor); 
2112     lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, aug->ext, aug->ext_size, private_destructor); 
2113  
2114     lys_when_free(ctx, aug->when, private_destructor); 
2115 }

2118 lys_ident_free(struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lys_ident *ident, 
2119                void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
2120 { 
2121     assert(ctx); 
2122     if (!ident) { 
2123         return; 
2124     } 
2125

2127     ly_set_free(ident->der); 
2128     lydict_remove(ctx, ident->name); 
2129     lydict_remove(ctx, ident->dsc); 
2130     lydict_remove(ctx, ident->ref); 
2131     lys_iffeature_free(ctx, ident->iffeature, ident->iffeature_size, 0, private_destructor); 
2132     lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, ident->ext, ident->ext_size, private_destructor); 
2133  
2134 }

2200 {

2205  
2206     for (i = 0; i < leaf->must_size; i++) { 
2207         lys_restr_free(ctx, &leaf->must[i], private_destructor); 
2208     } 
2209     free(leaf->must); 
2210

2212

2214     lydict_remove(ctx, leaf->units); 
2215     lydict_remove(ctx, leaf->dflt);

2231     }

2233  
2234     for (i = 0; i < llist->dflt_size; i++) { 
2235         lydict_remove(ctx, llist->dflt[i]); 
2236     } 
2237     free(llist->dflt); 
2238  
2239     lys_when_free(ctx, llist->when, private_destructor); 
2240  
2241     lys_type_free(ctx, &llist->type, private_destructor); 
2242     lydict_remove(ctx, llist->units); 
2243 }

2245 static void 
2246 lys_list_free(struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lys_node_list *list, 
2247               void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
2248 { 
2249     int i, j; 
2250  
2251     /* handle only specific parts for LY_NODE_LIST */ 
2252     for (i = 0; i < list->tpdf_size; i++) { 
2253         lys_tpdf_free(ctx, &list->tpdf[i], private_destructor); 
2254     } 
2255     free(list->tpdf); 
2256  
2257     for (i = 0; i < list->must_size; i++) { 
2258         lys_restr_free(ctx, &list->must[i], private_destructor); 
2259     } 
2260     free(list->must); 
2261  
2262     lys_when_free(ctx, list->when, private_destructor); 
2263  
2264     for (i = 0; i < list->unique_size; i++) { 
2265         for (j = 0; j < list->unique[i].expr_size; j++) { 
2266             lydict_remove(ctx, list->unique[i].expr[j]); 
2267         } 
2268         free(list->unique[i].expr); 
2269     } 
2270     free(list->unique); 
2271  
2272     free(list->keys); 
2273 }
