3807  
3808 API struct ly_set * 
3809 lys_xpath_atomize(const struct lys_node *ctx_node, enum lyxp_node_type ctx_node_type, const char *expr, int options) 
3810 { 
3811     struct lyxp_set set; 
3812     struct ly_set *ret_set; 
3813     uint32_t i; 
3814  
3815     if (!ctx_node || !expr) { 
3816         return NULL; 
3817     } 
3818  
3819     /* adjust the root */ 
3820     if ((ctx_node_type == LYXP_NODE_ROOT) || (ctx_node_type == LYXP_NODE_ROOT_CONFIG)) { 
3821         do { 
3822             ctx_node = lys_getnext(NULL, NULL, lys_node_module(ctx_node), 0); 
3823         } while ((ctx_node_type == LYXP_NODE_ROOT_CONFIG) && (ctx_node->flags & LYS_CONFIG_R)); 
3824     } 
3825  
3826     memset(&set, 0, sizeof set); 
3827  
3828     if (options & LYXP_MUST) { 
3829         options &= ~LYXP_MUST; 
3830         options |= LYXP_SNODE_MUST; 
3831     } else if (options & LYXP_WHEN) { 
3832         options &= ~LYXP_WHEN; 
3833         options |= LYXP_SNODE_WHEN; 
3834     } else { 
3835         options |= LYXP_SNODE; 
3836     } 
3837  
3838     if (lyxp_atomize(expr, ctx_node, ctx_node_type, &set, options, NULL)) { 
3839         free(set.val.snodes); 
3840         LOGVAL(LYE_SPEC, LY_VLOG_LYS, ctx_node, "Resolving XPath expression \"%s\" failed.", expr); 
3841         return NULL; 
3842     } 
3843  
3844     ret_set = ly_set_new(); 
3845  
3846     for (i = 0; i < set.used; ++i) { 
3847         switch (set.val.snodes[i].type) { 
3848         case LYXP_NODE_ELEM: 
3849             if (ly_set_add(ret_set, set.val.snodes[i].snode, LY_SET_OPT_USEASLIST) == -1) { 
3850                 ly_set_free(ret_set); 
3851                 free(set.val.snodes); 
3852                 return NULL; 
3853             } 
3854             break; 
3855         default: 
3856             /* ignore roots, text and attr should not ever appear */ 
3857             break; 
3858         } 
3859     } 
3860  
3861     free(set.val.snodes); 
3862     return ret_set; 
3863 } 
3864  
3865 API struct ly_set * 
3866 lys_node_xpath_atomize(const struct lys_node *node, int options) 
3867 { 
3868     const struct lys_node *next, *elem, *parent, *tmp; 
3869     struct lyxp_set set; 
3870     struct ly_set *ret_set; 
3871     uint16_t i; 
3872  
3873     if (!node) { 
3874         return NULL; 
3875     } 
3876  
3877     for (parent = node; parent && !(parent->nodetype & (LYS_NOTIF | LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT)); parent = lys_parent(parent)); 
3878     if (!parent) { 
3879         /* not in input, output, or notification */ 
3880         return NULL; 
3881     } 
3882  
3883     ret_set = ly_set_new(); 
3884     if (!ret_set) { 
3885         return NULL; 
3886     } 
3887  
3888     LY_TREE_DFS_BEGIN(node, next, elem) { 
3889         if ((options & LYXP_NO_LOCAL) && !(elem->flags & LYS_XPATH_DEP)) { 
3890             /* elem has no dependencies from other subtrees and local nodes get discarded */ 
3891             goto next_iter; 
3892         } 
3893  
3894         if (lyxp_node_atomize(elem, &set, 0)) { 
3895             ly_set_free(ret_set); 
3896             free(set.val.snodes); 
3897             return NULL; 
3898         } 
3899  
3900         for (i = 0; i < set.used; ++i) { 
3901             switch (set.val.snodes[i].type) { 
3902             case LYXP_NODE_ELEM: 
3903                 if (options & LYXP_NO_LOCAL) { 
3904                     for (tmp = set.val.snodes[i].snode; tmp && (tmp != parent); tmp = lys_parent(tmp)); 
3905                     if (tmp) { 
3906                         /* in local subtree, discard */ 
3907                         break; 
3908                     } 
3909                 } 
3910                 if (ly_set_add(ret_set, set.val.snodes[i].snode, 0) == -1) { 
3911                     ly_set_free(ret_set); 
3912                     free(set.val.snodes); 
3913                     return NULL; 
3914                 } 
3915                 break; 
3916             default: 
3917                 /* ignore roots, text and attr should not ever appear */ 
3918                 break; 
3919             } 
3920         } 
3921  
3922         free(set.val.snodes); 
3923         if (!(options & LYXP_RECURSIVE)) { 
3924             break; 
3925         } 
3926 next_iter: 
3927         LY_TREE_DFS_END(node, next, elem); 
3928     } 
3929  
3930     return ret_set; 
3931 } 
3932  
3933 /* logs */ 
3934 int 
3935 apply_aug(struct lys_node_augment *augment, struct unres_schema *unres) 
3936 { 
3937     struct lys_node *child, *parent; 
3938     int clear_config; 
3939     unsigned int u; 
3940     struct lys_ext_instance *ext; 
3941  
3942     assert(augment->target && (augment->flags & LYS_NOTAPPLIED)); 
3943  
3944     if (!augment->child) { 
3945         /* nothing to apply */ 
3946         goto success; 
3947     } 
3948  
3949     /* check that all the modules are implemented */ 
3950     for (parent = augment->target; parent; parent = lys_parent(parent)) { 
3951         if (!lys_node_module(parent)->implemented) { 
3952             if (lys_set_implemented(lys_node_module(parent))) { 
3953                 LOGERR(ly_errno, "Making the augment target module \"%s\" implemented failed.", lys_node_module(parent)->name); 
3954                 return -1; 
3955             } 
3956         } 
3957     } 
3958  
3959     /* reconnect augmenting data into the target - add them to the target child list */ 
3960     if (augment->target->child) { 
3961         child = augment->target->child->prev; 
3962         child->next = augment->child; 
3963         augment->target->child->prev = augment->child->prev; 
3964         augment->child->prev = child; 
3965     } else { 
3966         augment->target->child = augment->child; 
3967     } 
3968  
3969     /* inherit config information from actual parent */ 
3970     for (parent = augment->target; parent && !(parent->nodetype & (LYS_NOTIF | LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT | LYS_RPC)); parent = lys_parent(parent)); 
3971     clear_config = (parent) ? 1 : 0; 
3972     LY_TREE_FOR(augment->child, child) { 
3973         if (inherit_config_flag(child, augment->target->flags & LYS_CONFIG_MASK, clear_config)) { 
3974             return -1; 
3975         } 
3976     } 
3977  
3978     /* inherit extensions if any */ 
3979     for (u = 0; u < augment->target->ext_size; u++) { 
3980         ext = augment->target->ext[u]; /* shortcut */ 
3981         if (ext && ext->def->plugin && (ext->def->plugin->flags & LYEXT_OPT_INHERIT)) { 
3982             if (unres_schema_add_node(lys_main_module(augment->module), unres, &ext, UNRES_EXT_FINALIZE, NULL) == -1) { 
3983                 /* something really bad happend since the extension finalization is not actually 
3984                  * being resolved while adding into unres, so something more serious with the unres 
3985                  * list itself must happened */ 
3986                 return -1; 
3987             } 
3988         } 
3989     } 
3990  
3991 success: 
3992     /* remove the flag about not applicability */ 
3993     augment->flags &= ~LYS_NOTAPPLIED; 
3994     return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
3995 } 
3996  
3997 static void 
3998 remove_aug(struct lys_node_augment *augment) 
3999 { 
4000     struct lys_node *last, *elem; 
4001  
4002     if ((augment->flags & LYS_NOTAPPLIED) || !augment->target) { 
4003         /* skip already not applied augment */ 
4004         return; 
4005     } 
4006  
4007     elem = augment->child; 
4008     if (elem) { 
4009         LY_TREE_FOR(elem, last) { 
4010             if (!last->next || (last->next->parent != (struct lys_node *)augment)) { 
4011                 break; 
4012             } 
4013         } 
4014         /* elem is first augment child, last is the last child */ 
4015  
4016         /* parent child ptr */ 
4017         if (augment->target->child == elem) { 
4018             augment->target->child = last->next; 
4019         } 
4020  
4021         /* parent child next ptr */ 
4022         if (elem->prev->next) { 
4023             elem->prev->next = last->next; 
4024         } 
4025  
4026         /* parent child prev ptr */ 
4027         if (last->next) { 
4028             last->next->prev = elem->prev; 
4029         } else if (augment->target->child) { 
4030             augment->target->child->prev = elem->prev; 
4031         } 
4032  
4033         /* update augment children themselves */ 
4034         elem->prev = last; 
4035         last->next = NULL; 
4036     } 
4037  
4038     /* augment->target still keeps the resolved target, but for lys_augment_free() 
4039      * we have to keep information that this augment is not applied to free its data */ 
4040     augment->flags |= LYS_NOTAPPLIED; 
4041 } 
4042  
4043 /* 
4044  * @param[in] module - the module where the deviation is defined 
4045  */ 
4046 static void 
4047 lys_switch_deviation(struct lys_deviation *dev, const struct lys_module *module, struct unres_schema *unres) 
4048 { 
4049     int ret, reapply = 0; 
4050     char *parent_path; 
4051     struct lys_node *target = NULL, *parent; 
4052     struct ly_set *set; 
4053  
4054     if (!dev->deviate) { 
4055         return; 
4056     } 
4057  
4058     if (dev->deviate[0].mod == LY_DEVIATE_NO) { 
4059         if (dev->orig_node) { 
4060             /* removing not-supported deviation ... */ 
4061             if (strrchr(dev->target_name, '/') != dev->target_name) { 
4062                 /* ... from a parent */ 
4063  
4064                 /* reconnect to its previous position */ 
4065                 parent = dev->orig_node->parent; 
4066                 if (parent) { 
4067                     dev->orig_node->parent = NULL; 
4068                     /* the original node was actually from augment, we have to get know if the augment is 
4069                      * applied (its module is enabled and implemented). If yes, the node will be connected 
4070                      * to the augment and the linkage with the target will be fixed if needed, otherwise 
4071                      * it will be connected only to the augment */ 
4072                     if (!(parent->flags & LYS_NOTAPPLIED)) { 
4073                         /* start with removing augment if applied before adding nodes, we have to make sure 
4074                          * that everything will be connect correctly */ 
4075                         remove_aug((struct lys_node_augment *)parent); 
4076                         reapply = 1; 
4077                     } 
4078                     /* connect the deviated node back into the augment */ 
4079                     lys_node_addchild(parent, NULL, dev->orig_node); 
4080                     if (reapply) { 
4081                         /* augment is supposed to be applied, so fix pointers in target and the status of the original node */ 
4082                         if (parent->child == dev->orig_node) { 
4083                             /* the only node in augment */ 
4084                             parent->flags |= LYS_NOTAPPLIED; /* allow apply_aug() */ 
4085                             apply_aug((struct lys_node_augment *)parent, unres); 
4086                             /* else other nodes from augment applied, nothing more needed in target, everything was done 
4087                              * by lys_node_addchild() */ 
4088                         } 
4089                     } 
4090                 } else { 
4091                     /* non-augment, non-toplevel */ 
4092                     parent_path = strndup(dev->target_name, strrchr(dev->target_name, '/') - dev->target_name); 
4093                     ret = resolve_schema_nodeid(parent_path, NULL, module, &set, 0, 1); 
4094                     free(parent_path); 
4095                     if (ret == -1) { 
4096                         LOGINT; 
4097                         ly_set_free(set); 
4098                         return; 
4099                     } 
4100                     target = set->set.s[0]; 
4101                     ly_set_free(set); 
4102  
4103                     lys_node_addchild(target, NULL, dev->orig_node); 
4104                 } 
4105             } else { 
4106                 /* ... from top-level data */ 
4107                 lys_node_addchild(NULL, (struct lys_module *)dev->orig_node->module, dev->orig_node); 
4108             } 
4109  
4110             dev->orig_node = NULL; 
4111         } else { 
4112             /* adding not-supported deviation */ 
4113             ret = resolve_schema_nodeid(dev->target_name, NULL, module, &set, 0, 1); 
4114             if (ret == -1) { 
4115                 LOGINT; 
4116                 ly_set_free(set); 
4117                 return; 
4118             } 
4119             target = set->set.s[0]; 
4120             ly_set_free(set); 
4121  
4122             /* unlink and store the original node */ 
4123             parent = target->parent; 
4124             lys_node_unlink(target); 
4125             if (parent && parent->nodetype == LYS_AUGMENT) { 
4126                 /* hack for augment, because when the original will be sometime reconnected back, we actually need 
4127                  * to reconnect it to both - the augment and its target (which is deduced from the deviations target 
4128                  * path), so we need to remember the augment as an addition */ 
4129                 target->parent = parent; 
4130             } 
4131             dev->orig_node = target; 
4132         } 
4133     } else { 
4134         ret = resolve_schema_nodeid(dev->target_name, NULL, module, &set, 0, 1); 
4135         if (ret == -1) { 
4136             LOGINT; 
4137             ly_set_free(set); 
4138             return; 
4139         } 
4140         target = set->set.s[0]; 
4141         ly_set_free(set); 
4142  
4143         lys_node_switch(target, dev->orig_node); 
4144         dev->orig_node = target; 
4145     } 
4146 } 
4147  
4148 /* temporarily removes or applies deviations, updates module deviation flag accordingly */ 
4149 void 
4150 lys_switch_deviations(struct lys_module *module) 
4151 { 
4152     uint32_t i = 0, j; 
4153     const struct lys_module *mod; 
4154     const char *ptr; 
4155     struct unres_schema *unres; 
4156  
4157     if (module->deviated) { 
4158         unres = calloc(1, sizeof *unres); 
4159         LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!unres, LOGMEM, ); 
4160  
4161         while ((mod = ly_ctx_get_module_iter(module->ctx, &i))) { 
4162             if (mod == module) { 
4163                 continue; 
4164             } 
4165  
4166             for (j = 0; j < mod->deviation_size; ++j) { 
4167                 ptr = strstr(mod->deviation[j].target_name, module->name); 
4168                 if (ptr && ptr[strlen(module->name)] == ':') { 
4169                     lys_switch_deviation(&mod->deviation[j], mod, unres); 
4170                 } 
4171             } 
4172         } 
4173  
4174         if (module->deviated == 2) { 
4175             module->deviated = 1; 
4176         } else { 
4177             module->deviated = 2; 
4178         } 
4179         for (j = 0; j < module->inc_size; j++) { 
4180             if (module->inc[j].submodule->deviated) { 
4181                 module->inc[j].submodule->deviated = module->deviated; 
4182             } 
4183         } 
4184  
4185         if (unres->count) { 
4186             resolve_unres_schema(module, unres); 
4187         } 
4188         unres_schema_free(module, &unres, 1); 
4189     } 
4190 } 
4191  
4192 static void 
4193 apply_dev(struct lys_deviation *dev, const struct lys_module *module, struct unres_schema *unres) 
4194 { 
4195     lys_switch_deviation(dev, module, unres); 
4196  
4197     assert(dev->orig_node); 
4198     lys_node_module(dev->orig_node)->deviated = 1; /* main module */ 
4199     dev->orig_node->module->deviated = 1;          /* possible submodule */ 
4200 } 
4201  
4202 static void 
4203 remove_dev(struct lys_deviation *dev, const struct lys_module *module, struct unres_schema *unres) 
4204 { 
4205     uint32_t idx = 0, j; 
4206     const struct lys_module *mod; 
4207     struct lys_module *target_mod, *target_submod; 
4208     const char *ptr; 
4209  
4210     if (dev->orig_node) { 
4211         target_mod = lys_node_module(dev->orig_node); 
4212         target_submod = dev->orig_node->module; 
4213     } else { 
4214         LOGINT; 
4215         return; 
4216     } 
4217     lys_switch_deviation(dev, module, unres); 
4218  
4219     /* clear the deviation flag if possible */ 
4220     while ((mod = ly_ctx_get_module_iter(module->ctx, &idx))) { 
4221         if ((mod == module) || (mod == target_mod)) { 
4222             continue; 
4223         } 
4224  
4225         for (j = 0; j < mod->deviation_size; ++j) { 
4226             ptr = strstr(mod->deviation[j].target_name, target_mod->name); 
4227             if (ptr && (ptr[strlen(target_mod->name)] == ':')) { 
4228                 /* some other module deviation targets the inspected module, flag remains */ 
4229                 break; 
4230             } 
4231         } 
4232  
4233         if (j < mod->deviation_size) { 
4234             break; 
4235         } 
4236     } 
4237  
4238     if (!mod) { 
4239         target_mod->deviated = 0;    /* main module */ 
4240         target_submod->deviated = 0; /* possible submodule */ 
4241     } 
4242 } 
4243  
4244 void 
4245 lys_sub_module_apply_devs_augs(struct lys_module *module) 
4246 { 
4247     uint8_t u, v; 
4248     struct unres_schema *unres; 
4249  
4250     unres = calloc(1, sizeof *unres); 
4251     LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!unres, LOGMEM, ); 
4252  
4253     /* remove applied deviations */ 
4254     for (u = 0; u < module->deviation_size; ++u) { 
4255         apply_dev(&module->deviation[u], module, unres); 
4256     } 
4257     /* remove applied augments */ 
4258     for (u = 0; u < module->augment_size; ++u) { 
4259         apply_aug(&module->augment[u], unres); 
4260     } 
4261  
4262     /* remove deviation and augments defined in submodules */ 
4263     for (v = 0; v < module->inc_size; ++v) { 
4264         for (u = 0; u < module->inc[v].submodule->deviation_size; ++u) { 
4265             apply_dev(&module->inc[v].submodule->deviation[u], module, unres); 
4266         } 
4267  
4268         for (u = 0; u < module->inc[v].submodule->augment_size; ++u) { 
4269             apply_aug(&module->inc[v].submodule->augment[u], unres); 
4270         } 
4271     } 
4272  
4273     if (unres->count) { 
4274         resolve_unres_schema(module, unres); 
4275     } 
4276     /* nothing else left to do even if something is not resolved */ 
4277     unres_schema_free(module, &unres, 1); 
4278 } 
4279  
4280 void 
4281 lys_sub_module_remove_devs_augs(struct lys_module *module) 
4282 { 
4283     uint8_t u, v; 
4284     struct unres_schema *unres; 
4285  
4286     unres = calloc(1, sizeof *unres); 
4287     LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!unres, LOGMEM, ); 
4288  
4289     /* remove applied deviations */ 
4290     for (u = 0; u < module->deviation_size; ++u) { 
4291         remove_dev(&module->deviation[u], module, unres); 
4292     } 
4293     /* remove applied augments */ 
4294     for (u = 0; u < module->augment_size; ++u) { 
4295         remove_aug(&module->augment[u]); 
4296     } 
4297  
4298     /* remove deviation and augments defined in submodules */ 
4299     for (v = 0; v < module->inc_size && module->inc[v].submodule; ++v) { 
4300         for (u = 0; u < module->inc[v].submodule->deviation_size; ++u) { 
4301             remove_dev(&module->inc[v].submodule->deviation[u], module, unres); 
4302         } 
4303  
4304         for (u = 0; u < module->inc[v].submodule->augment_size; ++u) { 
4305             remove_aug(&module->inc[v].submodule->augment[u]); 
4306         } 
4307     } 
4308  
4309     if (unres->count) { 
4310         resolve_unres_schema(module, unres); 
4311     } 
4312     /* nothing else left to do even if something is not resolved */ 
4313     unres_schema_free(module, &unres, 1); 
4314 } 
4315  
4316 static int 
4317 lys_set_implemented_recursion(struct lys_module *module, struct unres_schema *unres) 
4318 { 
4319     struct lys_node *root, *next, *node; 
4320     uint16_t i, j; 
4321  
4322     for (i = 0; i < module->augment_size; i++) { 
4323         /* apply augment */ 
4324         assert(module->augment[i].target); 
4325         if (apply_aug(&module->augment[i], unres)) { 
4326             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
4327         } 
4328     } 
4329  
4330     /* identities */ 
4331     for (i = 0; i < module->ident_size; i++) { 
4332         for (j = 0; j < module->ident[i].base_size; j++) { 
4333             resolve_identity_backlink_update(&module->ident[i], module->ident[i].base[j]); 
4334         } 
4335     } 
4336  
4337     LY_TREE_FOR(module->data, root) { 
4338         /* handle leafrefs and recursively change the implemented flags in the leafref targets */ 
4339         LY_TREE_DFS_BEGIN(root, next, node) { 
4340             if (node->nodetype == LYS_GROUPING) { 
4341                 goto nextsibling; 
4342             } 
4343             if (node->nodetype & (LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST)) { 
4344                 if (((struct lys_node_leaf *)node)->type.base == LY_TYPE_LEAFREF) { 
4345                     if (unres_schema_add_node(module, unres, &((struct lys_node_leaf *)node)->type, 
4346                                               UNRES_TYPE_LEAFREF, node) == -1) { 
4347                         return EXIT_FAILURE; 
4348                     } 
4349                 } 
4350             } 
4351  
4352             /* modified LY_TREE_DFS_END */ 
4353             next = node->child; 
4354             /* child exception for leafs, leaflists and anyxml without children */ 
4355             if (node->nodetype & (LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_ANYDATA)) { 
4356                 next = NULL; 
4357             } 
4358             if (!next) { 
4359 nextsibling: 
4360                 /* no children */ 
4361                 if (node == root) { 
4362                     /* we are done, root has no children */ 
4363                     break; 
4364                 } 
4365                 /* try siblings */ 
4366                 next = node->next; 
4367             } 
4368             while (!next) { 
4369                 /* parent is already processed, go to its sibling */ 
4370                 node = lys_parent(node); 
4371                 /* no siblings, go back through parents */ 
4372                 if (lys_parent(node) == lys_parent(root)) { 
4373                     /* we are done, no next element to process */ 
4374                     break; 
4375                 } 
4376                 next = node->next; 
4377             } 
4378         } 
4379     } 
4380  
4381     return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
4382 } 
4383  
4384 API int 
4385 lys_set_implemented(const struct lys_module *module) 
4386 { 
4387     struct ly_ctx *ctx; 
4388     struct unres_schema *unres; 
4389     int i, j, k, disabled = 0; 
4390  
4391     if (!module) { 
4392         ly_errno = LY_EINVAL; 
4393         return EXIT_FAILURE; 
4394     } 
4395  
4396     module = lys_main_module(module); 
4397  
4398     if (module->disabled) { 
4399         disabled = 1; 
4400         lys_set_enabled(module); 
4401     } 
4402  
4403     if (module->implemented) { 
4404         return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
4405     } 
4406  
4407     ctx = module->ctx; 
4408  
4409     for (i = 0; i < ctx->models.used; ++i) { 
4410         if (module == ctx->models.list[i]) { 
4411             continue; 
4412         } 
4413  
4414         if (!strcmp(module->name, ctx->models.list[i]->name) && ctx->models.list[i]->implemented) { 
4415             LOGERR(LY_EINVAL, "Module \"%s\" in another revision already implemented.", module->name); 
4416             if (disabled) { 
4417                 /* set it back disabled */ 
4418                 lys_set_disabled(module); 
4419             } 
4420             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
4421         } 
4422     } 
4423  
4424     unres = calloc(1, sizeof *unres); 
4425     if (!unres) { 
4426         LOGMEM; 
4427         if (disabled) { 
4428             /* set it back disabled */ 
4429             lys_set_disabled(module); 
4430         } 
4431         return EXIT_FAILURE; 
4432     } 
4433     /* recursively make the module implemented */ 
4434     ((struct lys_module *)module)->implemented = 1; 
4435     if (lys_set_implemented_recursion((struct lys_module *)module, unres)) { 
4436         goto error; 
4437     } 
4438     /* process augments in submodules */ 
4439     for (i = 0; i < module->inc_size && module->inc[i].submodule; ++i) { 
4440         for (j = 0; j < module->inc[i].submodule->augment_size; j++) { 
4441             /* apply augment */ 
4442             assert(module->inc[i].submodule->augment[j].target); 
4443             if (apply_aug(&module->inc[i].submodule->augment[j], unres)) { 
4444                 goto error; 
4445             } 
4446         } 
4447  
4448         /* identities */ 
4449         for (j = 0; j < module->inc[i].submodule->ident_size; j++) { 
4450             for (k = 0; k < module->inc[i].submodule->ident[j].base_size; k++) { 
4451                 resolve_identity_backlink_update(&module->inc[i].submodule->ident[j], 
4452                                                  module->inc[i].submodule->ident[j].base[k]); 
4453             } 
4454         } 
4455     } 
4456     /* try again resolve augments in other modules possibly augmenting this one, 
4457      * since we have just enabled it 
4458      */ 
4459     /* resolve rest of unres items */ 
4460     if (unres->count && resolve_unres_schema((struct lys_module *)module, unres)) { 
4461         goto error; 
4462     } 
4463     unres_schema_free(NULL, &unres, 0); 
4464  
4465     /* reflect implemented flag in submodules */ 
4466     for (i = 0; i < module->inc_size; i++) { 
4467         module->inc[i].submodule->implemented = 1; 
4468     } 
4469  
4470     LOGVRB("Module \"%s%s%s\" now implemented.", module->name, (module->rev_size ? "@" : ""), 
4471            (module->rev_size ? module->rev[0].date : "")); 
4472     return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
4473  
4474 error: 
4475     if (disabled) { 
4476         /* set it back disabled */ 
4477         lys_set_disabled(module); 
4478     } 
4479  
4480     ((struct lys_module *)module)->implemented = 0; 
4481     unres_schema_free((struct lys_module *)module, &unres, 1); 
4482     return EXIT_FAILURE; 
4483 } 
4484  
4485 void 
4486 lys_submodule_module_data_free(struct lys_submodule *submodule) 
4487 { 
4488     struct lys_node *next, *elem; 
4489  
4490     /* remove parsed data */ 
4491     LY_TREE_FOR_SAFE(submodule->belongsto->data, next, elem) { 
4492         if (elem->module == (struct lys_module *)submodule) { 
4493             lys_node_free(elem, NULL, 0); 
4494         } 
4495     } 
4496 } 
4497  
4498 int 
4499 lys_is_key(struct lys_node_list *list, struct lys_node_leaf *leaf) 
4500 { 
4501     uint8_t i; 
4502  
4503     for (i = 0; i < list->keys_size; i++) { 
4504         if (list->keys[i] == leaf) { 
4505             return i + 1; 
4506         } 
4507     } 
4508  
4509     return 0; 
4510 } 
4511  
4512 API char * 
4513 lys_path(const struct lys_node *node) 
4514 { 
4515     char *buf, *result; 
4516     uint16_t start_idx, len; 
4517  
4518     if (!node) { 
4519         LOGERR(LY_EINVAL, "%s: NULL node parameter", __func__); 
4520         return NULL; 
4521     } 
4522  
4523     buf = malloc(LY_BUF_SIZE); 
4524     LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!buf, LOGMEM, NULL); 
4525     start_idx = LY_BUF_SIZE - 1; 
4526  
4527     buf[start_idx] = '\0'; 
4528     if (ly_vlog_build_path_reverse(LY_VLOG_LYS, node, &buf, &start_idx, &len, 1)) { 
4529         free(buf); 
4530         return NULL; 
4531     } 
4532  
4533     result = malloc(len + 1); 
4534     if (!result) { 
4535         LOGMEM; 
4536         free(buf); 
4537         return NULL; 
4538     } 
4539  
4540     result = memcpy(result, &buf[start_idx], len); 
4541     result[len] = '\0'; 
4542     free(buf); 
4543  
4544     return result; 
4545 } 
4546  
4547 API char * 
4548 lys_data_path(const struct lys_node *node) 
4549 { 
4550     char *buf_backup = NULL, *buf = ly_buf(), *result = NULL; 
4551     int i, used; 
4552     struct ly_set *set; 
4553     const struct lys_module *prev_mod; 
4554  
4555     if (!node) { 
4556         LOGERR(LY_EINVAL, "%s: NULL node parameter", __func__); 
4557         return NULL; 
4558     } 
4559  
4560     /* backup the shared internal buffer */ 
4561     if (ly_buf_used && buf[0]) { 
4562         buf_backup = strndup(buf, LY_BUF_SIZE - 1); 
4563     } 
4564     ly_buf_used++; 
4565  
4566     set = ly_set_new(); 
4567     LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(!set, LOGMEM, error); 
4568  
4569     while (node) { 
4570         ly_set_add(set, (void *)node, 0); 
4571         do { 
4572             node = lys_parent(node); 
4573         } while (node && (node->nodetype & (LYS_USES | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_CASE | LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT))); 
4574     } 
4575  
4576     prev_mod = NULL; 
4577     used = 0; 
4578     for (i = set->number - 1; i > -1; --i) { 
4579         node = set->set.s[i]; 
4580         used += sprintf(buf + used, "/%s%s%s", (lys_node_module(node) == prev_mod ? "" : lys_node_module(node)->name), 
4581                         (lys_node_module(node) == prev_mod ? "" : ":"), node->name); 
4582         prev_mod = lys_node_module(node); 
4583     } 
4584  
4585     result = strdup(buf); 
4586     LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(!result, LOGMEM, error); 
4587  
4588 error: 
4589     ly_set_free(set); 
4590     /* restore the shared internal buffer */ 
4591     if (buf_backup) { 
4592         strcpy(buf, buf_backup); 
4593         free(buf_backup); 
4594     } 
4595     ly_buf_used--; 
4596  
4597     return result; 
4598 } 
4599  
4600 struct lys_node_augment * 
4601 lys_getnext_target_aug(struct lys_node_augment *last, const struct lys_module *mod, const struct lys_node *aug_target) 
4602 { 
4603     int i, j, last_found; 
4604  
4605     if (!last) { 
4606         last_found = 1; 
4607     } else { 
4608         last_found = 0; 
4609     } 
4610  
4611     /* search module augments */ 
4612     for (i = 0; i < mod->augment_size; ++i) { 
4613         if (!mod->augment[i].target) { 
4614             /* still unresolved, skip */ 
4615             continue; 
4616         } 
4617  
4618         if (mod->augment[i].target == aug_target) { 
4619             if (last_found) { 
4620                 /* next match after last */ 
4621                 return &mod->augment[i]; 
4622             } 
4623  
4624             if (&mod->augment[i] == last) { 
4625                 last_found = 1; 
4626             } 
4627         } 
4628     } 
4629  
4630     /* search submodule augments */ 
4631     for (i = 0; i < mod->inc_size; ++i) { 
4632         for (j = 0; j < mod->inc[i].submodule->augment_size; ++j) { 
4633             if (!mod->inc[i].submodule->augment[j].target) { 
4634                 continue; 
4635             } 
4636  
4637             if (mod->inc[i].submodule->augment[j].target == aug_target) { 
4638                 if (last_found) { 
4639                     /* next match after last */ 
4640                     return &mod->inc[i].submodule->augment[j]; 
4641                 } 
4642  
4643                 if (&mod->inc[i].submodule->augment[j] == last) { 
4644                     last_found = 1; 
4645                 } 
4646             } 
4647         } 
4648     } 
4649  
4650     return NULL; 
4651 } 
4652  
4653 API struct ly_set * 
4654 lys_find_path(const struct lys_module *cur_module, const struct lys_node *cur_node, const char *path) 
4655 { 
4656     struct ly_set *ret; 
4657     int rc; 
4658  
4659     if ((!cur_module && !cur_node) || !path) { 
4660         return NULL; 
4661     } 
4662  
4663     rc = resolve_schema_nodeid(path, cur_node, cur_module, &ret, 1, 1); 
4664     if (rc == -1) { 
4665         return NULL; 
4666     } 
4667  
4668     return ret; 
4669 } 
4670  
4671 static void 
4672 lys_extcomplex_free_str(struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lys_ext_instance_complex *ext, LY_STMT stmt) 
4673 { 
4674     struct lyext_substmt *info; 
4675     const char **str, ***a; 
4676     int c; 
4677  
4678     str = lys_ext_complex_get_substmt(stmt, ext, &info); 
4679     if (!str || !(*str)) { 
4680         return; 
4681     } 
4682     if (info->cardinality >= LY_STMT_CARD_SOME) { 
4683         /* we have array */ 
4684         a = (const char ***)str; 
4685         for (str = (*(const char ***)str), c = 0; str[c]; c++) { 
4686             lydict_remove(ctx, str[c]); 
4687         } 
4688         free(a[0]); 
4689         if (stmt == LY_STMT_BELONGSTO) { 
4690             for (str = a[1], c = 0; str[c]; c++) { 
4691                 lydict_remove(ctx, str[c]); 
4692             } 
4693             free(a[1]); 
4694         } else if (stmt == LY_STMT_ARGUMENT) { 
4695             free(a[1]); 
4696         } 
4697     } else { 
4698         lydict_remove(ctx, str[0]); 
4699         if (stmt == LY_STMT_BELONGSTO) { 
4700             lydict_remove(ctx, str[1]); 
4701         } 
4702     } 
4703 } 
4704 void 
4705 lys_extension_instances_free(struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lys_ext_instance **e, unsigned int size, 
4706                              void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
4707 { 
4708     unsigned int i, j, k; 
4709     struct lyext_substmt *substmt; 
4710     void **pp, **start; 
4711     struct lys_node *siter, *snext; 
4712  
4713 #define EXTCOMPLEX_FREE_STRUCT(STMT, TYPE, FUNC, FREE, ARGS...)                               \ 
4714     pp = lys_ext_complex_get_substmt(STMT, (struct lys_ext_instance_complex *)e[i], NULL);    \ 
4715     if (!pp || !(*pp)) { break; }                                                             \ 
4716     if (substmt[j].cardinality >= LY_STMT_CARD_SOME) { /* process array */                    \ 
4717         for (start = pp = *pp; *pp; pp++) {                                                   \ 
4718             FUNC(ctx, (TYPE *)(*pp), ##ARGS, private_destructor);                             \ 
4719             if (FREE) { free(*pp); }                                                          \ 
4720         }                                                                                     \ 
4721         free(start);                                                                          \ 
4722     } else { /* single item */                                                                \ 
4723         FUNC(ctx, (TYPE *)(*pp), ##ARGS, private_destructor);                                 \ 
4724         if (FREE) { free(*pp); }                                                              \ 
4725     } 
4726  
4727     if (!size || !e || !(*e)) { 
4728         return; 
4729     } 
4730  
4731     for (i = 0; i < size; i++) { 
4732         if (!e[i]) { 
4733             continue; 
4734         } 
4735  
4736         if (e[i]->flags & (LYEXT_OPT_INHERIT)) { 
4737             /* no free, this is just a shadow copy of the original extension instance */ 
4738         } else { 
4739             if (e[i]->flags & (LYEXT_OPT_YANG)) { 
4740                 free(e[i]->def);     /* remove name of instance extension */ 
4741                 e[i]->def = NULL; 
4742                 yang_free_ext_data((struct yang_ext_substmt *)e[i]->parent); /* remove backup part of yang file */ 
4743             } 
4744             /* remove private object */ 
4745             if (e[i]->priv && private_destructor) { 
4746                 private_destructor((struct lys_node*)e[i], e[i]->priv); 
4747             } 
4748             lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, e[i]->ext, e[i]->ext_size, private_destructor); 
4749             lydict_remove(ctx, e[i]->arg_value); 
4750         } 
4751  
4752         if (e[i]->def && e[i]->def->plugin && e[i]->def->plugin->type == LYEXT_COMPLEX 
4753                 && ((e[i]->flags & LYEXT_OPT_CONTENT) == 0)) { 
4754             substmt = ((struct lys_ext_instance_complex *)e[i])->substmt; 
4755             for (j = 0; substmt[j].stmt; j++) { 
4756                 switch(substmt[j].stmt) { 
4757                 case LY_STMT_DESCRIPTION: 
4758                 case LY_STMT_REFERENCE: 
4759                 case LY_STMT_UNITS: 
4760                 case LY_STMT_ARGUMENT: 
4761                 case LY_STMT_DEFAULT: 
4762                 case LY_STMT_ERRTAG: 
4763                 case LY_STMT_ERRMSG: 
4764                 case LY_STMT_PREFIX: 
4765                 case LY_STMT_NAMESPACE: 
4766                 case LY_STMT_PRESENCE: 
4767                 case LY_STMT_REVISIONDATE: 
4768                 case LY_STMT_KEY: 
4769                 case LY_STMT_BASE: 
4770                 case LY_STMT_BELONGSTO: 
4771                 case LY_STMT_CONTACT: 
4772                 case LY_STMT_ORGANIZATION: 
4773                 case LY_STMT_PATH: 
4774                     lys_extcomplex_free_str(ctx, (struct lys_ext_instance_complex *)e[i], substmt[j].stmt); 
4775                     break; 
4776                 case LY_STMT_TYPE: 
4777                     EXTCOMPLEX_FREE_STRUCT(LY_STMT_TYPE, struct lys_type, lys_type_free, 1); 
4778                     break; 
4779                 case LY_STMT_TYPEDEF: 
4780                     EXTCOMPLEX_FREE_STRUCT(LY_STMT_TYPEDEF, struct lys_tpdf, lys_tpdf_free, 1); 
4781                     break; 
4782                 case LY_STMT_IFFEATURE: 
4783                     EXTCOMPLEX_FREE_STRUCT(LY_STMT_IFFEATURE, struct lys_iffeature, lys_iffeature_free, 0, 1, 0); 
4784                     break; 
4785                 case LY_STMT_MAX: 
4786                 case LY_STMT_MIN: 
4787                 case LY_STMT_POSITION: 
4788                 case LY_STMT_VALUE: 
4789                     pp = (void**)&((struct lys_ext_instance_complex *)e[i])->content[substmt[j].offset]; 
4790                     if (substmt[j].cardinality >= LY_STMT_CARD_SOME && *pp) { 
4791                         for(k = 0; ((uint32_t**)(*pp))[k]; k++) { 
4792                             free(((uint32_t**)(*pp))[k]); 
4793                         } 
4794                     } 
4795                     free(*pp); 
4796                     break; 
4797                 case LY_STMT_DIGITS: 
4798                     if (substmt[j].cardinality >= LY_STMT_CARD_SOME) { 
4799                         /* free the array */ 
4800                         pp = (void**)&((struct lys_ext_instance_complex *)e[i])->content[substmt[j].offset]; 
4801                         free(*pp); 
4802                     } 
4803                     break; 
4804                 case LY_STMT_MODULE: 
4805                     /* modules are part of the context, so they will be freed there */ 
4806                     if (substmt[j].cardinality >= LY_STMT_CARD_SOME) { 
4807                         /* free the array */ 
4808                         pp = (void**)&((struct lys_ext_instance_complex *)e[i])->content[substmt[j].offset]; 
4809                         free(*pp); 
4810                     } 
4811                     break; 
4812                 case LY_STMT_ACTION: 
4813                 case LY_STMT_ANYDATA: 
4814                 case LY_STMT_ANYXML: 
4815                 case LY_STMT_CASE: 
4816                 case LY_STMT_CHOICE: 
4817                 case LY_STMT_CONTAINER: 
4818                 case LY_STMT_GROUPING: 
4819                 case LY_STMT_INPUT: 
4820                 case LY_STMT_LEAF: 
4821                 case LY_STMT_LEAFLIST: 
4822                 case LY_STMT_LIST: 
4823                 case LY_STMT_NOTIFICATION: 
4824                 case LY_STMT_OUTPUT: 
4825                 case LY_STMT_RPC: 
4826                 case LY_STMT_USES: 
4827                     pp = (void**)&((struct lys_ext_instance_complex *)e[i])->content[substmt[j].offset]; 
4828                     LY_TREE_FOR_SAFE((struct lys_node *)(*pp), snext, siter) { 
4829                         lys_node_free(siter, NULL, 0); 
4830                     } 
4831                     *pp = NULL; 
4832                     break; 
4833                 case LY_STMT_UNIQUE: 
4834                     pp = lys_ext_complex_get_substmt(LY_STMT_UNIQUE, (struct lys_ext_instance_complex *)e[i], NULL); 
4835                     if (!pp || !(*pp)) { 
4836                         break; 
4837                     } 
4838                     if (substmt[j].cardinality >= LY_STMT_CARD_SOME) { /* process array */ 
4839                         for (start = pp = *pp; *pp; pp++) { 
4840                             for (k = 0; k < (*(struct lys_unique**)pp)->expr_size; k++) { 
4841                                 lydict_remove(ctx, (*(struct lys_unique**)pp)->expr[k]); 
4842                             } 
4843                             free((*(struct lys_unique**)pp)->expr); 
4844                             free(*pp); 
4845                         } 
4846                         free(start); 
4847                     } else { /* single item */ 
4848                         for (k = 0; k < (*(struct lys_unique**)pp)->expr_size; k++) { 
4849                             lydict_remove(ctx, (*(struct lys_unique**)pp)->expr[k]); 
4850                         } 
4851                         free((*(struct lys_unique**)pp)->expr); 
4852                         free(*pp); 
4853                     } 
4854                     break; 
4855                 case LY_STMT_LENGTH: 
4856                 case LY_STMT_MUST: 
4857                 case LY_STMT_PATTERN: 
4858                 case LY_STMT_RANGE: 
4859                     EXTCOMPLEX_FREE_STRUCT(substmt[j].stmt, struct lys_restr, lys_restr_free, 1); 
4860                     break; 
4861                 case LY_STMT_WHEN: 
4862                     EXTCOMPLEX_FREE_STRUCT(LY_STMT_WHEN, struct lys_when, lys_when_free, 0); 
4863                     break; 
4864                 case LY_STMT_REVISION: 
4865                     pp = lys_ext_complex_get_substmt(LY_STMT_REVISION, (struct lys_ext_instance_complex *)e[i], NULL); 
4866                     if (!pp || !(*pp)) { 
4867                         break; 
4868                     } 
4869                     if (substmt[j].cardinality >= LY_STMT_CARD_SOME) { /* process array */ 
4870                         for (start = pp = *pp; *pp; pp++) { 
4871                             lydict_remove(ctx, (*(struct lys_revision**)pp)->dsc); 
4872                             lydict_remove(ctx, (*(struct lys_revision**)pp)->ref); 
4873                             lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, (*(struct lys_revision**)pp)->ext, 
4874                                                          (*(struct lys_revision**)pp)->ext_size, private_destructor); 
4875                             free(*pp); 
4876                         } 
4877                         free(start); 
4878                     } else { /* single item */ 
4879                         lydict_remove(ctx, (*(struct lys_revision**)pp)->dsc); 
4880                         lydict_remove(ctx, (*(struct lys_revision**)pp)->ref); 
4881                         lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, (*(struct lys_revision**)pp)->ext, 
4882                                                      (*(struct lys_revision**)pp)->ext_size, private_destructor); 
4883                         free(*pp); 
4884                     } 
4885                     break; 
4886                 default: 
4887                     /* nothing to free */ 
4888                     break; 
4889                 } 
4890             } 
4891         } 
4892  
4893         free(e[i]); 
4894     } 
4895     free(e); 
4896  
4897 #undef EXTCOMPLEX_FREE_STRUCT 
4898 }

  41                         int in_grp, int shallow, struct unres_schema *unres);

  43 API const struct lys_node * 
  44 lys_is_disabled(const struct lys_node *node, int recursive) 
  45 { 
  46     int i; 
  47  
  48     if (!node) { 
  49         return NULL; 
  50     } 
  51  
  52 check: 
  53     if (node->nodetype != LYS_INPUT && node->nodetype != LYS_OUTPUT) { 
  54         /* input/output does not have if-feature, so skip them */ 
  55  
  56         /* check local if-features */ 
  57         for (i = 0; i < node->iffeature_size; i++) { 
  58             if (!resolve_iffeature(&node->iffeature[i])) { 
  59                 return node; 
  60             } 
  61         } 
  62     } 
  63  
  64     if (!recursive) { 
  65         return NULL; 
  66     } 
  67  
  68     /* go through parents */ 
  69     if (node->nodetype == LYS_AUGMENT) { 
  70         /* go to parent actually means go to the target node */ 
  71         node = ((struct lys_node_augment *)node)->target; 
  72         if (!node) { 
  73             /* unresolved augment, let's say it's enabled */ 
  74             return NULL; 
  75         } 
  76     } else if (node->parent) { 
  77         node = node->parent; 
  78     } else { 
  79         return NULL; 
  80     } 
  81  
  82     if (recursive == 2) { 
  83         /* continue only if the node cannot have a data instance */ 
  84         if (node->nodetype & (LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST)) { 
  85             return NULL; 
  86         } 
  87     } 
  88     goto check; 
  89 } 
  90  
  91 int 
  92 lys_get_sibling(const struct lys_node *siblings, const char *mod_name, int mod_name_len, const char *name, 
  93                 int nam_len, LYS_NODE type, const struct lys_node **ret) 
  94 { 
  95     const struct lys_node *node, *parent = NULL; 
  96     const struct lys_module *mod = NULL; 
  97     const char *node_mod_name; 
  98  
  99     assert(siblings && mod_name && name); 
 100     assert(!(type & (LYS_USES | LYS_GROUPING))); 
 101  
 102     /* fill the lengths in case the caller is so indifferent */ 
 103     if (!mod_name_len) { 
 104         mod_name_len = strlen(mod_name); 
 105     } 
 106     if (!nam_len) { 
 107         nam_len = strlen(name); 
 108     } 
 109  
 110     while (siblings && (siblings->nodetype == LYS_USES)) { 
 111         siblings = siblings->child; 
 112     } 
 113     if (!siblings) { 
 114         /* unresolved uses */ 
 115         return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 116     } 
 117  
 118     if (siblings->nodetype == LYS_GROUPING) { 
 119         for (node = siblings; (node->nodetype == LYS_GROUPING) && (node->prev != siblings); node = node->prev); 
 120         if (node->nodetype == LYS_GROUPING) { 
 121             /* we went through all the siblings, only groupings there - no valid sibling */ 
 122             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 123         } 
 124         /* update siblings to be valid */ 
 125         siblings = node; 
 126     } 
 127  
 128     /* set parent correctly */ 
 129     parent = lys_parent(siblings); 
 130  
 131     /* go up all uses */ 
 132     while (parent && (parent->nodetype == LYS_USES)) { 
 133         parent = lys_parent(parent); 
 134     } 
 135  
 136     if (!parent) { 
 137         /* handle situation when there is a top-level uses referencing a foreign grouping */ 
 138         for (node = siblings; lys_parent(node) && (node->nodetype == LYS_USES); node = lys_parent(node)); 
 139         mod = lys_node_module(node); 
 140     } 
 141  
 142     /* try to find the node */ 
 143     node = NULL; 
 144     while ((node = lys_getnext(node, parent, mod, LYS_GETNEXT_WITHCHOICE | LYS_GETNEXT_WITHCASE | LYS_GETNEXT_WITHINOUT))) { 
 145         if (!type || (node->nodetype & type)) { 
 146             /* module name comparison */ 
 147             node_mod_name = lys_node_module(node)->name; 
 148             if (!ly_strequal(node_mod_name, mod_name, 1) && (strncmp(node_mod_name, mod_name, mod_name_len) || node_mod_name[mod_name_len])) { 
 149                 continue; 
 150             } 
 151  
 152             /* direct name check */ 
 153             if (ly_strequal(node->name, name, 1) || (!strncmp(node->name, name, nam_len) && !node->name[nam_len])) { 
 154                 if (ret) { 
 155                     *ret = node; 
 156                 } 
 157                 return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
 158             } 
 159         } 
 160     } 
 161  
 162     return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 163 } 
 164  
 165 int 
 166 lys_getnext_data(const struct lys_module *mod, const struct lys_node *parent, const char *name, int nam_len, 
 167                  LYS_NODE type, const struct lys_node **ret) 
 168 { 
 169     const struct lys_node *node; 
 170  
 171     assert((mod || parent) && name); 
 172     assert(!(type & (LYS_AUGMENT | LYS_USES | LYS_GROUPING | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_CASE | LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT))); 
 173  
 174     if (!mod) { 
 175         mod = lys_node_module(parent); 
 176     } 
 177  
 178     /* try to find the node */ 
 179     node = NULL; 
 180     while ((node = lys_getnext(node, parent, mod, 0))) { 
 181         if (!type || (node->nodetype & type)) { 
 182             /* module check */ 
 183             if (lys_node_module(node) != lys_main_module(mod)) { 
 184                 continue; 
 185             } 
 186  
 187             /* direct name check */ 
 188             if (!strncmp(node->name, name, nam_len) && !node->name[nam_len]) { 
 189                 if (ret) { 
 190                     *ret = node; 
 191                 } 
 192                 return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
 193             } 
 194         } 
 195     } 
 196  
 197     return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 198 } 
 199  
 200 API const struct lys_node * 
 201 lys_getnext(const struct lys_node *last, const struct lys_node *parent, const struct lys_module *module, int options) 
 202 { 
 203     const struct lys_node *next, *aug_parent; 
 204     struct lys_node **snode; 
 205  
 206     if ((!parent && !module) || (parent && (parent->nodetype == LYS_USES) && !(options & LYS_GETNEXT_PARENTUSES))) { 
 207         LOGERR(LY_EINVAL, "%s: Invalid parameter.", __func__); 
 208         return NULL; 
 209     } 
 210  
 211     if (!last) { 
 212         /* first call */ 
 213  
 214         /* get know where to start */ 
 215         if (parent) { 
 216             /* schema subtree */ 
 217             snode = lys_child(parent, LYS_UNKNOWN); 
 218             /* do not return anything if the augment does not have any children */ 
 219             if (!snode || !(*snode) || ((parent->nodetype == LYS_AUGMENT) && ((*snode)->parent != parent))) { 
 220                 return NULL; 
 221             } 
 222             next = last = *snode; 
 223         } else { 
 224             /* top level data */ 
 225             assert(module); 
 226             next = last = module->data; 
 227         } 
 228     } else if ((last->nodetype == LYS_USES) && (options & LYS_GETNEXT_INTOUSES) && last->child) { 
 229         /* continue with uses content */ 
 230         next = last->child; 
 231     } else { 
 232         /* continue after the last returned value */ 
 233         next = last->next; 
 234     } 
 235  
 236 repeat: 
 237     if (parent && (parent->nodetype == LYS_AUGMENT) && next) { 
 238         /* do not return anything outside the parent augment */ 
 239         aug_parent = next->parent; 
 240         do { 
 241             while (aug_parent && (aug_parent->nodetype != LYS_AUGMENT)) { 
 242                 aug_parent = aug_parent->parent; 
 243             } 
 244             if (aug_parent) { 
 245                 if (aug_parent == parent) { 
 246                     break; 
 247                 } 
 248                 aug_parent = ((struct lys_node_augment *)aug_parent)->target; 
 249             } 
 250  
 251         } while (aug_parent); 
 252         if (!aug_parent) { 
 253             return NULL; 
 254         } 
 255     } 
 256     while (next && (next->nodetype == LYS_GROUPING)) { 
 257         if (options & LYS_GETNEXT_WITHGROUPING) { 
 258             return next; 
 259         } 
 260         next = next->next; 
 261     } 
 262  
 263     if (!next) {     /* cover case when parent is augment */ 
 264         if (!last || last->parent == parent || lys_parent(last) == parent) { 
 265             /* no next element */ 
 266             return NULL; 
 267         } 
 268         last = lys_parent(last); 
 269         next = last->next; 
 270         goto repeat; 
 271     } else { 
 272         last = next; 
 273     } 
 274  
 275     switch (next->nodetype) { 
 276     case LYS_INPUT: 
 277     case LYS_OUTPUT: 
 278         if (options & LYS_GETNEXT_WITHINOUT) { 
 279             return next; 
 280         } else if (next->child) { 
 281             next = next->child; 
 282         } else { 
 283             next = next->next; 
 284         } 
 285         goto repeat; 
 286  
 287     case LYS_CASE: 
 288         if (options & LYS_GETNEXT_WITHCASE) { 
 289             return next; 
 290         } else if (next->child) { 
 291             next = next->child; 
 292         } else { 
 293             next = next->next; 
 294         } 
 295         goto repeat; 
 296  
 297     case LYS_USES: 
 298         /* go into */ 
 299         if (options & LYS_GETNEXT_WITHUSES) { 
 300             return next; 
 301         } else if (next->child) { 
 302             next = next->child; 
 303         } else { 
 304             next = next->next; 
 305         } 
 306         goto repeat; 
 307  
 308     case LYS_RPC: 
 309     case LYS_ACTION: 
 310     case LYS_NOTIF: 
 311     case LYS_LEAF: 
 312     case LYS_ANYXML: 
 313     case LYS_ANYDATA: 
 314     case LYS_LIST: 
 315     case LYS_LEAFLIST: 
 316         return next; 
 317  
 318     case LYS_CONTAINER: 
 319         if (!((struct lys_node_container *)next)->presence && (options & LYS_GETNEXT_INTONPCONT)) { 
 320             if (next->child) { 
 321                 /* go into */ 
 322                 next = next->child; 
 323             } else { 
 324                 next = next->next; 
 325             } 
 326             goto repeat; 
 327         } else { 
 328             return next; 
 329         } 
 330  
 331     case LYS_CHOICE: 
 332         if (options & LYS_GETNEXT_WITHCHOICE) { 
 333             return next; 
 334         } else if (next->child) { 
 335             /* go into */ 
 336             next = next->child; 
 337         } else { 
 338             next = next->next; 
 339         } 
 340         goto repeat; 
 341  
 342     default: 
 343         /* we should not be here */ 
 344         return NULL; 
 345     } 
 346 } 
 347  
 348 void 
 349 lys_node_unlink(struct lys_node *node) 
 350 { 
 351     struct lys_node *parent, *first, **pp; 
 352     struct lys_module *main_module; 
 353  
 354     if (!node) { 
 355         return; 
 356     } 
 357  
 358     /* unlink from data model if necessary */ 
 359     if (node->module) { 
 360         /* get main module with data tree */ 
 361         main_module = lys_node_module(node); 
 362         if (main_module->data == node) { 
 363             main_module->data = node->next; 
 364         } 
 365     } 
 366  
 367     /* store pointers to important nodes */ 
 368     parent = node->parent; 
 369     if (parent && (parent->nodetype == LYS_AUGMENT)) { 
 370         /* handle augments - first, unlink it from the augment parent ... */ 
 371         if (parent->child == node) { 
 372             parent->child = (node->next && node->next->parent == parent) ? node->next : NULL; 
 373         } 
 374  
 375         if (parent->flags & LYS_NOTAPPLIED) { 
 376             /* data are not connected in the target, so we cannot continue with the target as a parent */ 
 377             parent = NULL; 
 378         } else { 
 379             /* data are connected in target, so we will continue with the target as a parent */ 
 380             parent = ((struct lys_node_augment *)parent)->target; 
 381         } 
 382     } 
 383  
 384     /* unlink from parent */ 
 385     if (parent) { 
 386         if (parent->nodetype == LYS_EXT) { 
 387             pp = (struct lys_node **)lys_ext_complex_get_substmt(lys_snode2stmt(node->nodetype), 
 388                                                                  (struct lys_ext_instance_complex*)parent, NULL); 
 389             if (*pp == node) { 
 390                 *pp = node->next; 
 391             } 
 392         } else if (parent->child == node) { 
 393             parent->child = node->next; 
 394         } 
 395         node->parent = NULL; 
 396     } 
 397  
 398     /* unlink from siblings */ 
 399     if (node->prev == node) { 
 400         /* there are no more siblings */ 
 401         return; 
 402     } 
 403     if (node->next) { 
 404         node->next->prev = node->prev; 
 405     } else { 
 406         /* unlinking the last element */ 
 407         if (parent) { 
 408             if (parent->nodetype == LYS_EXT) { 
 409                 first = *(struct lys_node **)pp; 
 410             } else { 
 411                 first = parent->child; 
 412             } 
 413         } else { 
 414             first = node; 
 415             while (first->prev->next) { 
 416                 first = first->prev; 
 417             } 
 418         } 
 419         first->prev = node->prev; 
 420     } 
 421     if (node->prev->next) { 
 422         node->prev->next = node->next; 
 423     } 
 424  
 425     /* clean up the unlinked element */ 
 426     node->next = NULL; 
 427     node->prev = node; 
 428 } 
 429  
 430 struct lys_node_grp * 
 431 lys_find_grouping_up(const char *name, struct lys_node *start) 
 432 { 
 433     struct lys_node *par_iter, *iter, *stop; 
 434  
 435     for (par_iter = start; par_iter; par_iter = par_iter->parent) { 
 436         /* top-level augment, look into module (uses augment is handled correctly below) */ 
 437         if (par_iter->parent && !par_iter->parent->parent && (par_iter->parent->nodetype == LYS_AUGMENT)) { 
 438             par_iter = lys_main_module(par_iter->parent->module)->data; 
 439             if (!par_iter) { 
 440                 break; 
 441             } 
 442         } 
 443  
 444         if (par_iter->parent && (par_iter->parent->nodetype & (LYS_CHOICE | LYS_CASE | LYS_AUGMENT | LYS_USES))) { 
 445             continue; 
 446         } 
 447  
 448         for (iter = par_iter, stop = NULL; iter; iter = iter->prev) { 
 449             if (!stop) { 
 450                 stop = par_iter; 
 451             } else if (iter == stop) { 
 452                 break; 
 453             } 
 454             if (iter->nodetype != LYS_GROUPING) { 
 455                 continue; 
 456             } 
 457  
 458             if (!strcmp(name, iter->name)) { 
 459                 return (struct lys_node_grp *)iter; 
 460             } 
 461         } 
 462     } 
 463  
 464     return NULL; 
 465 } 
 466  
 467 /* 
 468  * get next grouping in the root's subtree, in the 
 469  * first call, tha last is NULL 
 470  */ 
 471 static struct lys_node_grp * 
 472 lys_get_next_grouping(struct lys_node_grp *lastgrp, struct lys_node *root) 
 473 { 
 474     struct lys_node *last = (struct lys_node *)lastgrp; 
 475     struct lys_node *next; 
 476  
 477     assert(root); 
 478  
 479     if (!last) { 
 480         last = root; 
 481     } 
 482  
 483     while (1) { 
 484         if ((last->nodetype & (LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_LIST | LYS_GROUPING | LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT))) { 
 485             next = last->child; 
 486         } else { 
 487             next = NULL; 
 488         } 
 489         if (!next) { 
 490             if (last == root) { 
 491                 /* we are done */ 
 492                 return NULL; 
 493             } 
 494  
 495             /* no children, go to siblings */ 
 496             next = last->next; 
 497         } 
 498         while (!next) { 
 499             /* go back through parents */ 
 500             if (lys_parent(last) == root) { 
 501                 /* we are done */ 
 502                 return NULL; 
 503             } 
 504             next = last->next; 
 505             last = lys_parent(last); 
 506         } 
 507  
 508         if (next->nodetype == LYS_GROUPING) { 
 509             return (struct lys_node_grp *)next; 
 510         } 
 511  
 512         last = next; 
 513     } 
 514 } 
 515  
 516 /* logs directly */ 
 517 int 
 518 lys_check_id(struct lys_node *node, struct lys_node *parent, struct lys_module *module) 
 519 { 
 520     struct lys_node *start, *stop, *iter; 
 521     struct lys_node_grp *grp; 
 522     int down, up; 
 523  
 524     assert(node); 
 525  
 526     if (!parent) { 
 527         assert(module); 
 528     } else { 
 529         module = parent->module; 
 530     } 
 531     module = lys_main_module(module); 
 532  
 533     switch (node->nodetype) { 
 534     case LYS_GROUPING: 
 535         /* 6.2.1, rule 6 */ 
 536         if (parent) { 
 537             start = *lys_child(parent, LYS_GROUPING); 
 538             if (!start) { 
 539                 down = 0; 
 540                 start = parent; 
 541             } else { 
 542                 down = 1; 
 543             } 
 544             if (parent->nodetype == LYS_EXT) { 
 545                 up = 0; 
 546             } else { 
 547                 up = 1; 
 548             } 
 549         } else { 
 550             down = up = 1; 
 551             start = module->data; 
 552         } 
 553         /* go up */ 
 554         if (up && lys_find_grouping_up(node->name, start)) { 
 555             LOGVAL(LYE_DUPID, LY_VLOG_LYS, node, "grouping", node->name); 
 556             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 557         } 
 558         /* go down, because grouping can be defined after e.g. container in which is collision */ 
 559         if (down) { 
 560             for (iter = start, stop = NULL; iter; iter = iter->prev) { 
 561                 if (!stop) { 
 562                     stop = start; 
 563                 } else if (iter == stop) { 
 564                     break; 
 565                 } 
 566                 if (!(iter->nodetype & (LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_LIST | LYS_GROUPING | LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT))) { 
 567                     continue; 
 568                 } 
 569  
 570                 grp = NULL; 
 571                 while ((grp = lys_get_next_grouping(grp, iter))) { 
 572                     if (ly_strequal(node->name, grp->name, 1)) { 
 573                         LOGVAL(LYE_DUPID,LY_VLOG_LYS, node, "grouping", node->name); 
 574                         return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 575                     } 
 576                 } 
 577             } 
 578         } 
 579         break; 
 580     case LYS_LEAF: 
 581     case LYS_LEAFLIST: 
 582     case LYS_LIST: 
 583     case LYS_CONTAINER: 
 584     case LYS_CHOICE: 
 585     case LYS_ANYDATA: 
 586         /* 6.2.1, rule 7 */ 
 587         if (parent) { 
 588             iter = parent; 
 589             while (iter && (iter->nodetype & (LYS_USES | LYS_CASE | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_AUGMENT))) { 
 590                 if (iter->nodetype == LYS_AUGMENT) { 
 591                     if (((struct lys_node_augment *)iter)->target) { 
 592                         /* augment is resolved, go up */ 
 593                         iter = ((struct lys_node_augment *)iter)->target; 
 594                         continue; 
 595                     } 
 596                     /* augment is not resolved, this is the final parent */ 
 597                     break; 
 598                 } 
 599                 iter = iter->parent; 
 600             } 
 601  
 602             if (!iter) { 
 603                 stop = NULL; 
 604                 iter = module->data; 
 605             } else if (iter->nodetype == LYS_EXT) { 
 606                 stop = iter; 
 607                 iter = *lys_child(iter, node->nodetype); 
 608             } else { 
 609                 stop = iter; 
 610                 iter = iter->child; 
 611             } 
 612         } else { 
 613             stop = NULL; 
 614             iter = module->data; 
 615         } 
 616         while (iter) { 
 617             if (iter->nodetype & (LYS_USES | LYS_CASE)) { 
 618                 iter = iter->child; 
 619                 continue; 
 620             } 
 621  
 622             if (iter->nodetype & (LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST | LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_ANYDATA)) { 
 623                 if (iter->module == node->module && ly_strequal(iter->name, node->name, 1)) { 
 624                     LOGVAL(LYE_DUPID, LY_VLOG_LYS, node, strnodetype(node->nodetype), node->name); 
 625                     return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 626                 } 
 627             } 
 628  
 629             /* special case for choice - we must check the choice's name as 
 630              * well as the names of nodes under the choice 
 631              */ 
 632             if (iter->nodetype == LYS_CHOICE) { 
 633                 iter = iter->child; 
 634                 continue; 
 635             } 
 636  
 637             /* go to siblings */ 
 638             if (!iter->next) { 
 639                 /* no sibling, go to parent's sibling */ 
 640                 do { 
 641                     /* for parent LYS_AUGMENT */ 
 642                     if (iter->parent == stop) { 
 643                         iter = stop; 
 644                         break; 
 645                     } 
 646                     iter = lys_parent(iter); 
 647                     if (iter && iter->next) { 
 648                         break; 
 649                     } 
 650                 } while (iter != stop); 
 651  
 652                 if (iter == stop) { 
 653                     break; 
 654                 } 
 655             } 
 656             iter = iter->next; 
 657         } 
 658         break; 
 659     case LYS_CASE: 
 660         /* 6.2.1, rule 8 */ 
 661         if (parent) { 
 662             start = *lys_child(parent, LYS_CASE); 
 663         } else { 
 664             start = module->data; 
 665         } 
 666  
 667         LY_TREE_FOR(start, iter) { 
 668             if (!(iter->nodetype & (LYS_ANYDATA | LYS_CASE | LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST))) { 
 669                 continue; 
 670             } 
 671  
 672             if (iter->module == node->module && ly_strequal(iter->name, node->name, 1)) { 
 673                 LOGVAL(LYE_DUPID, LY_VLOG_LYS, node, "case", node->name); 
 674                 return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 675             } 
 676         } 
 677         break; 
 678     default: 
 679         /* no check needed */ 
 680         break; 
 681     } 
 682  
 683     return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
 684 } 
 685  
 686 /* logs directly */ 
 687 int 
 688 lys_node_addchild(struct lys_node *parent, struct lys_module *module, struct lys_node *child) 
 689 { 
 690     struct lys_node *iter, **pchild; 
 691     struct lys_node_inout *in, *out, *inout; 
 692     struct lys_node_case *c; 
 693     int type, shortcase = 0; 
 694     void *p; 
 695     struct lyext_substmt *info = NULL; 
 696  
 697     assert(child); 
 698  
 699     if (parent) { 
 700         type = parent->nodetype; 
 701         module = parent->module; 
 702     } else { 
 703         assert(module); 
 704         assert(!(child->nodetype & (LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT))); 
 705         type = 0; 
 706     } 
 707  
 708     /* checks */ 
 709     switch (type) { 
 710     case LYS_CONTAINER: 
 711     case LYS_LIST: 
 712     case LYS_GROUPING: 
 713     case LYS_USES: 
 714         if (!(child->nodetype & 
 715                 (LYS_ANYDATA | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_GROUPING | LYS_LEAF | 
 716                  LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST | LYS_USES | LYS_ACTION | LYS_NOTIF))) { 
 717             LOGVAL(LYE_INCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, parent, strnodetype(child->nodetype), strnodetype(parent->nodetype)); 
 718             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 719         } 
 720         break; 
 721     case LYS_INPUT: 
 722     case LYS_OUTPUT: 
 723     case LYS_NOTIF: 
 724         if (!(child->nodetype & 
 725                 (LYS_ANYDATA | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_GROUPING | LYS_LEAF | 
 726                  LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST | LYS_USES))) { 
 727             LOGVAL(LYE_INCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, parent, strnodetype(child->nodetype), strnodetype(parent->nodetype)); 
 728             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 729         } 
 730         break; 
 731     case LYS_CHOICE: 
 732         if (!(child->nodetype & 
 733                 (LYS_ANYDATA | LYS_CASE | LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST | LYS_CHOICE))) { 
 734             LOGVAL(LYE_INCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, parent, strnodetype(child->nodetype), "choice"); 
 735             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 736         } 
 737         if (child->nodetype != LYS_CASE) { 
 738             shortcase = 1; 
 739         } 
 740         break; 
 741     case LYS_CASE: 
 742         if (!(child->nodetype & 
 743                 (LYS_ANYDATA | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST | LYS_USES))) { 
 744             LOGVAL(LYE_INCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, parent, strnodetype(child->nodetype), "case"); 
 745             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 746         } 
 747         break; 
 748     case LYS_RPC: 
 749     case LYS_ACTION: 
 750         if (!(child->nodetype & (LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT | LYS_GROUPING))) { 
 751             LOGVAL(LYE_INCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, parent, strnodetype(child->nodetype), "rpc"); 
 752             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 753         } 
 754         break; 
 755     case LYS_LEAF: 
 756     case LYS_LEAFLIST: 
 757     case LYS_ANYXML: 
 758     case LYS_ANYDATA: 
 759         LOGVAL(LYE_INCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, parent, strnodetype(child->nodetype), strnodetype(parent->nodetype)); 
 760         LOGVAL(LYE_SPEC, LY_VLOG_PREV, NULL, "The \"%s\" statement cannot have any data substatement.", 
 761                strnodetype(parent->nodetype)); 
 762         return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 763     case LYS_AUGMENT: 
 764         if (!(child->nodetype & 
 765                 (LYS_ANYDATA | LYS_CASE | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_LEAF 
 766                 | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST | LYS_USES | LYS_ACTION | LYS_NOTIF))) { 
 767             LOGVAL(LYE_INCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, parent, strnodetype(child->nodetype), strnodetype(parent->nodetype)); 
 768             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 769         } 
 770         break; 
 771     case LYS_UNKNOWN: 
 772         /* top level */ 
 773         if (!(child->nodetype & 
 774                 (LYS_ANYDATA | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_LEAF | LYS_GROUPING 
 775                 | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST | LYS_USES | LYS_RPC | LYS_NOTIF | LYS_AUGMENT))) { 
 776             LOGVAL(LYE_INCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, parent, strnodetype(child->nodetype), "(sub)module"); 
 777             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 778         } 
 779         break; 
 780     case LYS_EXT: 
 781         /* plugin-defined */ 
 782         p = lys_ext_complex_get_substmt(lys_snode2stmt(child->nodetype), (struct lys_ext_instance_complex*)parent, &info); 
 783         if (!p) { 
 784             LOGVAL(LYE_INCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, parent, strnodetype(child->nodetype), 
 785                    ((struct lys_ext_instance_complex*)parent)->def->name); 
 786             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 787         } 
 788         /* TODO check cardinality */ 
 789         break; 
 790     } 
 791  
 792     /* check identifier uniqueness */ 
 793     if (!(module->ctx->models.flags & LY_CTX_TRUSTED) && lys_check_id(child, parent, module)) { 
 794         return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 795     } 
 796  
 797     if (child->parent) { 
 798         lys_node_unlink(child); 
 799     } 
 800  
 801     if ((child->nodetype & (LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT)) && parent->nodetype != LYS_EXT) { 
 802         /* replace the implicit input/output node */ 
 803         if (child->nodetype == LYS_OUTPUT) { 
 804             inout = (struct lys_node_inout *)parent->child->next; 
 805         } else { /* LYS_INPUT */ 
 806             inout = (struct lys_node_inout *)parent->child; 
 807             parent->child = child; 
 808         } 
 809         if (inout->next) { 
 810             child->next = inout->next; 
 811             inout->next->prev = child; 
 812             inout->next = NULL; 
 813         } else { 
 814             parent->child->prev = child; 
 815         } 
 816         child->prev = inout->prev; 
 817         if (inout->prev->next) { 
 818             inout->prev->next = child; 
 819         } 
 820         inout->prev = (struct lys_node *)inout; 
 821         child->parent = parent; 
 822         inout->parent = NULL; 
 823         lys_node_free((struct lys_node *)inout, NULL, 0); 
 824     } else { 
 825         if (shortcase) { 
 826             /* create the implicit case to allow it to serve as a target of the augments, 
 827              * it won't be printed, but it will be present in the tree */ 
 828             c = calloc(1, sizeof *c); 
 829             LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!c, LOGMEM, EXIT_FAILURE); 
 830             c->name = lydict_insert(module->ctx, child->name, 0); 
 831             c->flags = LYS_IMPLICIT; 
 832             c->module = module; 
 833             c->nodetype = LYS_CASE; 
 834             c->prev = (struct lys_node*)c; 
 835             lys_node_addchild(parent, module, (struct lys_node*)c); 
 836             parent = (struct lys_node*)c; 
 837         } 
 838         /* connect the child correctly */ 
 839         if (!parent) { 
 840             if (module->data) { 
 841                 module->data->prev->next = child; 
 842                 child->prev = module->data->prev; 
 843                 module->data->prev = child; 
 844             } else { 
 845                 module->data = child; 
 846             } 
 847         } else { 
 848             pchild = lys_child(parent, child->nodetype); 
 849             assert(pchild); 
 850  
 851             child->parent = parent; 
 852             if (!(*pchild)) { 
 853                 /* the only/first child of the parent */ 
 854                 *pchild = child; 
 855                 iter = child; 
 856             } else { 
 857                 /* add a new child at the end of parent's child list */ 
 858                 iter = (*pchild)->prev; 
 859                 iter->next = child; 
 860                 child->prev = iter; 
 861             } 
 862             while (iter->next) { 
 863                 iter = iter->next; 
 864                 iter->parent = parent; 
 865             } 
 866             (*pchild)->prev = iter; 
 867         } 
 868     } 
 869  
 870     /* check config value (but ignore them in groupings and augments) */ 
 871     for (iter = parent; iter && !(iter->nodetype & (LYS_GROUPING | LYS_AUGMENT | LYS_EXT)); iter = iter->parent); 
 872     if (parent && !iter) { 
 873         for (iter = child; iter && !(iter->nodetype & (LYS_NOTIF | LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT | LYS_RPC)); iter = iter->parent); 
 874         if (!iter && (parent->flags & LYS_CONFIG_R) && (child->flags & LYS_CONFIG_W)) { 
 875             LOGVAL(LYE_INARG, LY_VLOG_LYS, child, "true", "config"); 
 876             LOGVAL(LYE_SPEC, LY_VLOG_PREV, NULL, "State nodes cannot have configuration nodes as children."); 
 877             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 878         } 
 879     } 
 880  
 881     /* propagate information about status data presence */ 
 882     if ((child->nodetype & (LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST | LYS_ANYDATA)) && 
 883             (child->flags & LYS_INCL_STATUS)) { 
 884         for(iter = parent; iter; iter = lys_parent(iter)) { 
 885             /* store it only into container or list - the only data inner nodes */ 
 886             if (iter->nodetype & (LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_LIST)) { 
 887                 if (iter->flags & LYS_INCL_STATUS) { 
 888                     /* done, someone else set it already from here */ 
 889                     break; 
 890                 } 
 891                 /* set flag about including status data */ 
 892                 iter->flags |= LYS_INCL_STATUS; 
 893             } 
 894         } 
 895     } 
 896  
 897     /* create implicit input/output nodes to have available them as possible target for augment */ 
 898     if ((child->nodetype & (LYS_RPC | LYS_ACTION)) && !child->child) { 
 899         in = calloc(1, sizeof *in); 
 900         out = calloc(1, sizeof *out); 
 901         if (!in || !out) { 
 902             LOGMEM; 
 903             free(in); 
 904             free(out); 
 905             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
 906         } 
 907         in->nodetype = LYS_INPUT; 
 908         in->name = lydict_insert(child->module->ctx, "input", 5); 
 909         out->nodetype = LYS_OUTPUT; 
 910         out->name = lydict_insert(child->module->ctx, "output", 6); 
 911         in->module = out->module = child->module; 
 912         in->parent = out->parent = child; 
 913         in->flags = out->flags = LYS_IMPLICIT; 
 914         in->next = (struct lys_node *)out; 
 915         in->prev = (struct lys_node *)out; 
 916         out->prev = (struct lys_node *)in; 
 917         child->child = (struct lys_node *)in; 
 918     } 
 919     return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
 920 } 
 921  
 922 const struct lys_module * 
 923 lys_parse_mem_(struct ly_ctx *ctx, const char *data, LYS_INFORMAT format, int internal, int implement) 
 924 { 
 925     char *enlarged_data = NULL; 
 926     struct lys_module *mod = NULL; 
 927     unsigned int len; 
 928     struct ly_ctx *ctx_prev = ly_parser_data.ctx; 
 929  
 930     ly_err_clean(ctx, 1); 
 931  
 932     if (!ctx || !data) { 
 933         LOGERR(LY_EINVAL, "%s: Invalid parameter.", __func__); 
 934         return NULL; 
 935     } 
 936  
 937     /* set parser context */ 
 938     ly_parser_data.ctx = ctx; 
 939  
 940     if (!internal && format == LYS_IN_YANG) { 
 941         /* enlarge data by 2 bytes for flex */ 
 942         len = strlen(data); 
 943         enlarged_data = malloc((len + 2) * sizeof *enlarged_data); 
 944         LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!enlarged_data, LOGMEM, NULL); 
 945         memcpy(enlarged_data, data, len); 
 946         enlarged_data[len] = enlarged_data[len + 1] = '\0'; 
 947         data = enlarged_data; 
 948     } 
 949  
 950     switch (format) { 
 951     case LYS_IN_YIN: 
 952         mod = yin_read_module(ctx, data, NULL, implement); 
 953         break; 
 954     case LYS_IN_YANG: 
 955         mod = yang_read_module(ctx, data, 0, NULL, implement); 
 956         break; 
 957     default: 
 958         LOGERR(LY_EINVAL, "Invalid schema input format."); 
 959         break; 
 960     } 
 961  
 962     free(enlarged_data); 
 963  
 964     /* hack for NETCONF's edit-config's operation attribute. It is not defined in the schema, but since libyang 
 965      * implements YANG metadata (annotations), we need its definition. Because the ietf-netconf schema is not the 
 966      * internal part of libyang, we cannot add the annotation into the schema source, but we do it here to have 
 967      * the anotation definitions available in the internal schema structure. There is another hack in schema 
 968      * printers to do not print this internally added annotation. */ 
 969     if (mod && ly_strequal(mod->name, "ietf-netconf", 0)) { 
 970         if (lyp_add_ietf_netconf_annotations(mod)) { 
 971             lys_free(mod, NULL, 1); 
 972             return NULL; 
 973         } 
 974     } 
 975  
 976     /* reset parser context */ 
 977     ly_parser_data.ctx = ctx_prev; 
 978  
 979     return mod; 
 980 } 
 981  
 982 API const struct lys_module * 
 983 lys_parse_mem(struct ly_ctx *ctx, const char *data, LYS_INFORMAT format) 
 984 { 
 985     return lys_parse_mem_(ctx, data, format, 0, 1); 
 986 } 
 987  
 988 struct lys_submodule * 
 989 lys_sub_parse_mem(struct lys_module *module, const char *data, LYS_INFORMAT format, struct unres_schema *unres) 
 990 { 
 991     char *enlarged_data = NULL; 
 992     struct lys_submodule *submod = NULL; 
 993     unsigned int len; 
 994  
 995     assert(module); 
 996     assert(data); 
 997  
 998     if (format == LYS_IN_YANG) { 
 999         /* enlarge data by 2 bytes for flex */ 
1000         len = strlen(data);
