2218 static void 
2219 lys_leaflist_free(struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lys_node_leaflist *llist, 
2220                   void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
2221 { 
2222     int i; 
2223  
2224     if (llist->backlinks) { 
2225         /* leafref backlinks */ 
2226         ly_set_free(llist->backlinks); 
2227     } 
2228  
2229     for (i = 0; i < llist->must_size; i++) { 
2230         lys_restr_free(ctx, &llist->must[i], private_destructor); 
2231     } 
2232     free(llist->must); 
2233  
2234     for (i = 0; i < llist->dflt_size; i++) { 
2235         lydict_remove(ctx, llist->dflt[i]); 
2236     } 
2237     free(llist->dflt); 
2238  
2239     lys_when_free(ctx, llist->when, private_destructor); 
2240  
2241     lys_type_free(ctx, &llist->type, private_destructor); 
2242     lydict_remove(ctx, llist->units); 
2243 } 
2244  
2245 static void 
2246 lys_list_free(struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lys_node_list *list, 
2247               void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
2248 { 
2249     int i, j; 
2250  
2251     /* handle only specific parts for LY_NODE_LIST */ 
2252     for (i = 0; i < list->tpdf_size; i++) { 
2253         lys_tpdf_free(ctx, &list->tpdf[i], private_destructor); 
2254     } 
2255     free(list->tpdf); 
2256  
2257     for (i = 0; i < list->must_size; i++) { 
2258         lys_restr_free(ctx, &list->must[i], private_destructor); 
2259     } 
2260     free(list->must); 
2261  
2262     lys_when_free(ctx, list->when, private_destructor); 
2263  
2264     for (i = 0; i < list->unique_size; i++) { 
2265         for (j = 0; j < list->unique[i].expr_size; j++) { 
2266             lydict_remove(ctx, list->unique[i].expr[j]); 
2267         } 
2268         free(list->unique[i].expr); 
2269     } 
2270     free(list->unique); 
2271  
2272     free(list->keys); 
2273 } 
2274  
2275 static void 
2276 lys_container_free(struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lys_node_container *cont, 
2277                    void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
2278 { 
2279     int i; 
2280  
2281     /* handle only specific parts for LY_NODE_CONTAINER */ 
2282     lydict_remove(ctx, cont->presence); 
2283  
2284     for (i = 0; i < cont->tpdf_size; i++) { 
2285         lys_tpdf_free(ctx, &cont->tpdf[i], private_destructor); 
2286     } 
2287     free(cont->tpdf); 
2288  
2289     for (i = 0; i < cont->must_size; i++) { 
2290         lys_restr_free(ctx, &cont->must[i], private_destructor); 
2291     } 
2292     free(cont->must); 
2293  
2294     lys_when_free(ctx, cont->when, private_destructor); 
2295 } 
2296  
2297 static void 
2298 lys_feature_free(struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lys_feature *f, 
2299                  void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
2300 { 
2301     lydict_remove(ctx, f->name); 
2302     lydict_remove(ctx, f->dsc); 
2303     lydict_remove(ctx, f->ref); 
2304     lys_iffeature_free(ctx, f->iffeature, f->iffeature_size, 0, private_destructor); 
2305     ly_set_free(f->depfeatures); 
2306     lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, f->ext, f->ext_size, private_destructor); 
2307 } 
2308  
2309 static void 
2310 lys_extension_free(struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lys_ext *e, 
2311                    void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
2312 { 
2313     lydict_remove(ctx, e->name); 
2314     lydict_remove(ctx, e->dsc); 
2315     lydict_remove(ctx, e->ref); 
2316     lydict_remove(ctx, e->argument); 
2317     lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, e->ext, e->ext_size, private_destructor); 
2318 } 
2319  
2320 static void 
2321 lys_deviation_free(struct lys_module *module, struct lys_deviation *dev, 
2322                    void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
2323 { 
2324     int i, j, k; 
2325     struct ly_ctx *ctx; 
2326     struct lys_node *next, *elem; 
2327  
2328     ctx = module->ctx; 
2329  
2330     lydict_remove(ctx, dev->target_name); 
2331     lydict_remove(ctx, dev->dsc); 
2332     lydict_remove(ctx, dev->ref); 
2333     lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, dev->ext, dev->ext_size, private_destructor); 
2334  
2335     if (!dev->deviate) { 
2336         return ; 
2337     } 
2338  
2339     /* the module was freed, but we only need the context from orig_node, use ours */ 
2340     if (dev->deviate[0].mod == LY_DEVIATE_NO) { 
2341         /* it's actually a node subtree, we need to update modules on all the nodes :-/ */ 
2342         LY_TREE_DFS_BEGIN(dev->orig_node, next, elem) { 
2343             elem->module = module; 
2344  
2345             LY_TREE_DFS_END(dev->orig_node, next, elem); 
2346         } 
2347         lys_node_free(dev->orig_node, NULL, 0); 
2348     } else { 
2349         /* it's just a shallow copy, freeing one node */ 
2350         dev->orig_node->module = module; 
2351         lys_node_free(dev->orig_node, NULL, 1); 
2352     } 
2353  
2354     for (i = 0; i < dev->deviate_size; i++) { 
2355         lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, dev->deviate[i].ext, dev->deviate[i].ext_size, private_destructor); 
2356  
2357         for (j = 0; j < dev->deviate[i].dflt_size; j++) { 
2358             lydict_remove(ctx, dev->deviate[i].dflt[j]); 
2359         } 
2360         free(dev->deviate[i].dflt); 
2361  
2362         lydict_remove(ctx, dev->deviate[i].units); 
2363  
2364         if (dev->deviate[i].mod == LY_DEVIATE_DEL) { 
2365             for (j = 0; j < dev->deviate[i].must_size; j++) { 
2366                 lys_restr_free(ctx, &dev->deviate[i].must[j], private_destructor); 
2367             } 
2368             free(dev->deviate[i].must); 
2369  
2370             for (j = 0; j < dev->deviate[i].unique_size; j++) { 
2371                 for (k = 0; k < dev->deviate[i].unique[j].expr_size; k++) { 
2372                     lydict_remove(ctx, dev->deviate[i].unique[j].expr[k]); 
2373                 } 
2374                 free(dev->deviate[i].unique[j].expr); 
2375             } 
2376             free(dev->deviate[i].unique); 
2377         } 
2378     } 
2379     free(dev->deviate); 
2380 } 
2381  
2382 static void 
2383 lys_uses_free(struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lys_node_uses *uses, 
2384               void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
2385 { 
2386     int i, j; 
2387  
2388     for (i = 0; i < uses->refine_size; i++) { 
2389         lydict_remove(ctx, uses->refine[i].target_name); 
2390         lydict_remove(ctx, uses->refine[i].dsc); 
2391         lydict_remove(ctx, uses->refine[i].ref); 
2392  
2393         lys_iffeature_free(ctx, uses->refine[i].iffeature, uses->refine[i].iffeature_size, 0, private_destructor); 
2394  
2395         for (j = 0; j < uses->refine[i].must_size; j++) { 
2396             lys_restr_free(ctx, &uses->refine[i].must[j], private_destructor); 
2397         } 
2398         free(uses->refine[i].must); 
2399  
2400         for (j = 0; j < uses->refine[i].dflt_size; j++) { 
2401             lydict_remove(ctx, uses->refine[i].dflt[j]); 
2402         } 
2403         free(uses->refine[i].dflt); 
2404  
2405         lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, uses->refine[i].ext, uses->refine[i].ext_size, private_destructor); 
2406  
2407         if (uses->refine[i].target_type & LYS_CONTAINER) { 
2408             lydict_remove(ctx, uses->refine[i].mod.presence); 
2409         } 
2410     } 
2411     free(uses->refine); 
2412  
2413     for (i = 0; i < uses->augment_size; i++) { 
2414         lys_augment_free(ctx, &uses->augment[i], private_destructor); 
2415     } 
2416     free(uses->augment); 
2417  
2418     lys_when_free(ctx, uses->when, private_destructor); 
2419 } 
2420  
2421 void 
2422 lys_node_free(struct lys_node *node, void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv), int shallow) 
2423 { 
2424     struct ly_ctx *ctx; 
2425     struct lys_node *sub, *next; 
2426  
2427     if (!node) { 
2428         return; 
2429     } 
2430  
2431     assert(node->module); 
2432     assert(node->module->ctx); 
2433  
2434     ctx = node->module->ctx; 
2435  
2436     /* remove private object */ 
2437     if (node->priv && private_destructor) { 
2438         private_destructor(node, node->priv); 
2439     } 
2440  
2441     /* common part */ 
2442     lydict_remove(ctx, node->name); 
2443     if (!(node->nodetype & (LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT))) { 
2444         lys_iffeature_free(ctx, node->iffeature, node->iffeature_size, shallow, private_destructor); 
2445         lydict_remove(ctx, node->dsc); 
2446         lydict_remove(ctx, node->ref); 
2447     } 
2448  
2449     if (!shallow && !(node->nodetype & (LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST))) { 
2450         LY_TREE_FOR_SAFE(node->child, next, sub) { 
2451             lys_node_free(sub, private_destructor, 0); 
2452         } 
2453     } 
2454  
2455     lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, node->ext, node->ext_size, private_destructor); 
2456  
2457     /* specific part */ 
2458     switch (node->nodetype) { 
2459     case LYS_CONTAINER: 
2460         lys_container_free(ctx, (struct lys_node_container *)node, private_destructor); 
2461         break; 
2462     case LYS_CHOICE: 
2463         lys_when_free(ctx, ((struct lys_node_choice *)node)->when, private_destructor); 
2464         break; 
2465     case LYS_LEAF: 
2466         lys_leaf_free(ctx, (struct lys_node_leaf *)node, private_destructor); 
2467         break; 
2468     case LYS_LEAFLIST: 
2469         lys_leaflist_free(ctx, (struct lys_node_leaflist *)node, private_destructor); 
2470         break; 
2471     case LYS_LIST: 
2472         lys_list_free(ctx, (struct lys_node_list *)node, private_destructor); 
2473         break; 
2474     case LYS_ANYXML: 
2475     case LYS_ANYDATA: 
2476         lys_anydata_free(ctx, (struct lys_node_anydata *)node, private_destructor); 
2477         break; 
2478     case LYS_USES: 
2479         lys_uses_free(ctx, (struct lys_node_uses *)node, private_destructor); 
2480         break; 
2481     case LYS_CASE: 
2482         lys_when_free(ctx, ((struct lys_node_case *)node)->when, private_destructor); 
2483         break; 
2484     case LYS_AUGMENT: 
2485         /* do nothing */ 
2486         break; 
2487     case LYS_GROUPING: 
2488     case LYS_RPC: 
2489     case LYS_ACTION: 
2490         lys_grp_free(ctx, (struct lys_node_grp *)node, private_destructor); 
2491         break; 
2492     case LYS_NOTIF: 
2493         lys_notif_free(ctx, (struct lys_node_notif *)node, private_destructor); 
2494         break; 
2495     case LYS_INPUT: 
2496     case LYS_OUTPUT: 
2497         lys_inout_free(ctx, (struct lys_node_inout *)node, private_destructor); 
2498         break; 
2499     case LYS_EXT: 
2500     case LYS_UNKNOWN: 
2501         LOGINT; 
2502         break; 
2503     } 
2504  
2505     /* again common part */ 
2506     lys_node_unlink(node); 
2507     free(node); 
2508 } 
2509  
2510 API struct lys_module * 
2511 lys_implemented_module(const struct lys_module *mod) 
2512 { 
2513     struct ly_ctx *ctx; 
2514     int i; 
2515  
2516     if (!mod || mod->implemented) { 
2517         /* invalid argument or the module itself is implemented */ 
2518         return (struct lys_module *)mod; 
2519     } 
2520  
2521     ctx = mod->ctx; 
2522     for (i = 0; i < ctx->models.used; i++) { 
2523         if (!ctx->models.list[i]->implemented) { 
2524             continue; 
2525         } 
2526  
2527         if (ly_strequal(mod->name, ctx->models.list[i]->name, 1)) { 
2528             /* we have some revision of the module implemented */ 
2529             return ctx->models.list[i]; 
2530         } 
2531     } 
2532  
2533     /* we have no revision of the module implemented, return the module itself, 
2534      * it is up to the caller to set the module implemented when needed */ 
2535     return (struct lys_module *)mod; 
2536 } 
2537  
2538 /* free_int_mods - flag whether to free the internal modules as well */ 
2539 static void 
2540 module_free_common(struct lys_module *module, void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
2541 { 
2542     struct ly_ctx *ctx; 
2543     struct lys_node *next, *iter; 
2544     unsigned int i; 
2545  
2546     assert(module->ctx); 
2547     ctx = module->ctx; 
2548  
2549     /* just free the import array, imported modules will stay in the context */ 
2550     for (i = 0; i < module->imp_size; i++) { 
2551         lydict_remove(ctx, module->imp[i].prefix); 
2552         lydict_remove(ctx, module->imp[i].dsc); 
2553         lydict_remove(ctx, module->imp[i].ref); 
2554         lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, module->imp[i].ext, module->imp[i].ext_size, private_destructor); 
2555     } 
2556     free(module->imp); 
2557  
2558     /* submodules don't have data tree, the data nodes 
2559      * are placed in the main module altogether */ 
2560     if (!module->type) { 
2561         LY_TREE_FOR_SAFE(module->data, next, iter) { 
2562             lys_node_free(iter, private_destructor, 0); 
2563         } 
2564     } 
2565  
2566     lydict_remove(ctx, module->dsc); 
2567     lydict_remove(ctx, module->ref); 
2568     lydict_remove(ctx, module->org); 
2569     lydict_remove(ctx, module->contact); 
2570     lydict_remove(ctx, module->filepath); 
2571  
2572     /* revisions */ 
2573     for (i = 0; i < module->rev_size; i++) { 
2574         lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, module->rev[i].ext, module->rev[i].ext_size, private_destructor); 
2575         lydict_remove(ctx, module->rev[i].dsc); 
2576         lydict_remove(ctx, module->rev[i].ref); 
2577     } 
2578     free(module->rev); 
2579  
2580     /* identities */ 
2581     for (i = 0; i < module->ident_size; i++) { 
2582         lys_ident_free(ctx, &module->ident[i], private_destructor); 
2583     } 
2584     module->ident_size = 0; 
2585     free(module->ident); 
2586  
2587     /* typedefs */ 
2588     for (i = 0; i < module->tpdf_size; i++) { 
2589         lys_tpdf_free(ctx, &module->tpdf[i], private_destructor); 
2590     } 
2591     free(module->tpdf); 
2592  
2593     /* extension instances */ 
2594     lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, module->ext, module->ext_size, private_destructor); 
2595  
2596     /* include */ 
2597     for (i = 0; i < module->inc_size; i++) { 
2598         lydict_remove(ctx, module->inc[i].dsc); 
2599         lydict_remove(ctx, module->inc[i].ref); 
2600         lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, module->inc[i].ext, module->inc[i].ext_size, private_destructor); 
2601         /* complete submodule free is done only from main module since 
2602          * submodules propagate their includes to the main module */ 
2603         if (!module->type) { 
2604             lys_submodule_free(module->inc[i].submodule, private_destructor); 
2605         } 
2606     } 
2607     free(module->inc); 
2608  
2609     /* augment */ 
2610     for (i = 0; i < module->augment_size; i++) { 
2611         lys_augment_free(ctx, &module->augment[i], private_destructor); 
2612     } 
2613     free(module->augment); 
2614  
2615     /* features */ 
2616     for (i = 0; i < module->features_size; i++) { 
2617         lys_feature_free(ctx, &module->features[i], private_destructor); 
2618     } 
2619     free(module->features); 
2620  
2621     /* deviations */ 
2622     for (i = 0; i < module->deviation_size; i++) { 
2623         lys_deviation_free(module, &module->deviation[i], private_destructor); 
2624     } 
2625     free(module->deviation); 
2626  
2627     /* extensions */ 
2628     for (i = 0; i < module->extensions_size; i++) { 
2629         lys_extension_free(ctx, &module->extensions[i], private_destructor); 
2630     } 
2631     free(module->extensions); 
2632  
2633     lydict_remove(ctx, module->name); 
2634     lydict_remove(ctx, module->prefix); 
2635 } 
2636  
2637 void 
2638 lys_submodule_free(struct lys_submodule *submodule, void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
2639 { 
2640     if (!submodule) { 
2641         return; 
2642     } 
2643  
2644     /* common part with struct ly_module */ 
2645     module_free_common((struct lys_module *)submodule, private_destructor); 
2646  
2647     /* no specific items to free */ 
2648  
2649     free(submodule); 
2650 } 
2651  
2652 int 
2653 lys_ingrouping(const struct lys_node *node) 
2654 { 
2655     const struct lys_node *iter = node; 
2656     assert(node); 
2657  
2658     for(iter = node; iter && iter->nodetype != LYS_GROUPING; iter = lys_parent(iter)); 
2659     if (!iter) { 
2660         return 0; 
2661     } else { 
2662         return 1; 
2663     } 
2664 } 
2665  
2666 /* 
2667  * final: 0 - do not change config flags; 1 - inherit config flags from the parent; 2 - remove config flags 
2668  */ 
2669 static struct lys_node * 
2670 lys_node_dup_recursion(struct lys_module *module, struct lys_node *parent, const struct lys_node *node, 
2671                        struct unres_schema *unres, int shallow, int finalize) 
2672 { 
2673     struct lys_node *retval = NULL, *iter, *p; 
2674     struct lys_module *tmp_mod; 
2675     struct ly_ctx *ctx = module->ctx; 
2676     int i, j, rc; 
2677     unsigned int size, size1, size2; 
2678     struct unres_list_uniq *unique_info; 
2679     uint16_t flags; 
2680  
2681     struct lys_node_container *cont = NULL; 
2682     struct lys_node_container *cont_orig = (struct lys_node_container *)node; 
2683     struct lys_node_choice *choice = NULL; 
2684     struct lys_node_choice *choice_orig = (struct lys_node_choice *)node; 
2685     struct lys_node_leaf *leaf = NULL; 
2686     struct lys_node_leaf *leaf_orig = (struct lys_node_leaf *)node; 
2687     struct lys_node_leaflist *llist = NULL; 
2688     struct lys_node_leaflist *llist_orig = (struct lys_node_leaflist *)node; 
2689     struct lys_node_list *list = NULL; 
2690     struct lys_node_list *list_orig = (struct lys_node_list *)node; 
2691     struct lys_node_anydata *any = NULL; 
2692     struct lys_node_anydata *any_orig = (struct lys_node_anydata *)node; 
2693     struct lys_node_uses *uses = NULL; 
2694     struct lys_node_uses *uses_orig = (struct lys_node_uses *)node; 
2695     struct lys_node_rpc_action *rpc = NULL; 
2696     struct lys_node_inout *io = NULL; 
2697     struct lys_node_notif *ntf = NULL; 
2698     struct lys_node_case *cs = NULL; 
2699     struct lys_node_case *cs_orig = (struct lys_node_case *)node; 
2700  
2701     /* we cannot just duplicate memory since the strings are stored in 
2702      * dictionary and we need to update dictionary counters. 
2703      */ 
2704  
2705     switch (node->nodetype) { 
2706     case LYS_CONTAINER: 
2707         cont = calloc(1, sizeof *cont); 
2708         retval = (struct lys_node *)cont; 
2709         break; 
2710  
2711     case LYS_CHOICE: 
2712         choice = calloc(1, sizeof *choice); 
2713         retval = (struct lys_node *)choice; 
2714         break; 
2715  
2716     case LYS_LEAF: 
2717         leaf = calloc(1, sizeof *leaf); 
2718         retval = (struct lys_node *)leaf; 
2719         break; 
2720  
2721     case LYS_LEAFLIST: 
2722         llist = calloc(1, sizeof *llist); 
2723         retval = (struct lys_node *)llist; 
2724         break; 
2725  
2726     case LYS_LIST: 
2727         list = calloc(1, sizeof *list); 
2728         retval = (struct lys_node *)list; 
2729         break; 
2730  
2731     case LYS_ANYXML: 
2732     case LYS_ANYDATA: 
2733         any = calloc(1, sizeof *any); 
2734         retval = (struct lys_node *)any; 
2735         break; 
2736  
2737     case LYS_USES: 
2738         uses = calloc(1, sizeof *uses); 
2739         retval = (struct lys_node *)uses; 
2740         break; 
2741  
2742     case LYS_CASE: 
2743         cs = calloc(1, sizeof *cs); 
2744         retval = (struct lys_node *)cs; 
2745         break; 
2746  
2747     case LYS_RPC: 
2748     case LYS_ACTION: 
2749         rpc = calloc(1, sizeof *rpc); 
2750         retval = (struct lys_node *)rpc; 
2751         break; 
2752  
2753     case LYS_INPUT: 
2754     case LYS_OUTPUT: 
2755         io = calloc(1, sizeof *io); 
2756         retval = (struct lys_node *)io; 
2757         break; 
2758  
2759     case LYS_NOTIF: 
2760         ntf = calloc(1, sizeof *ntf); 
2761         retval = (struct lys_node *)ntf; 
2762         break; 
2763  
2764     default: 
2765         LOGINT; 
2766         goto error; 
2767     } 
2768     LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!retval, LOGMEM, NULL); 
2769  
2770     /* 
2771      * duplicate generic part of the structure 
2772      */ 
2773     retval->name = lydict_insert(ctx, node->name, 0); 
2774     retval->dsc = lydict_insert(ctx, node->dsc, 0); 
2775     retval->ref = lydict_insert(ctx, node->ref, 0); 
2776     retval->flags = node->flags; 
2777  
2778     retval->module = module; 
2779     retval->nodetype = node->nodetype; 
2780  
2781     retval->prev = retval; 
2782  
2783     retval->ext_size = node->ext_size; 
2784     if (lys_ext_dup(module, node->ext, node->ext_size, retval, LYEXT_PAR_NODE, &retval->ext, shallow, unres)) { 
2785         goto error; 
2786     } 
2787  
2788     if (node->iffeature_size) { 
2789         retval->iffeature_size = node->iffeature_size; 
2790         retval->iffeature = calloc(retval->iffeature_size, sizeof *retval->iffeature); 
2791         LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(!retval->iffeature, LOGMEM, error); 
2792     } 
2793  
2794     if (!shallow) { 
2795         for (i = 0; i < node->iffeature_size; ++i) { 
2796             resolve_iffeature_getsizes(&node->iffeature[i], &size1, &size2); 
2797             if (size1) { 
2798                 /* there is something to duplicate */ 
2799  
2800                 /* duplicate compiled expression */ 
2801                 size = (size1 / 4) + (size1 % 4) ? 1 : 0; 
2802                 retval->iffeature[i].expr = malloc(size * sizeof *retval->iffeature[i].expr); 
2803                 LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(!retval->iffeature[i].expr, LOGMEM, error); 
2804                 memcpy(retval->iffeature[i].expr, node->iffeature[i].expr, size * sizeof *retval->iffeature[i].expr); 
2805  
2806                 /* list of feature pointer must be updated to point to the resulting tree */ 
2807                 retval->iffeature[i].features = calloc(size2, sizeof *retval->iffeature[i].features); 
2808                 LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(!retval->iffeature[i].features, LOGMEM; free(retval->iffeature[i].expr), error); 
2809  
2810                 for (j = 0; (unsigned int)j < size2; j++) { 
2811                     rc = unres_schema_dup(module, unres, &node->iffeature[i].features[j], UNRES_IFFEAT, 
2812                                           &retval->iffeature[i].features[j]); 
2813                     if (rc == EXIT_FAILURE) { 
2814                         /* feature is resolved in origin, so copy it 
2815                          * - duplication is used for instantiating groupings 
2816                          * and if-feature inside grouping is supposed to be 
2817                          * resolved inside the original grouping, so we want 
2818                          * to keep pointers to features from the grouping 
2819                          * context */ 
2820                         retval->iffeature[i].features[j] = node->iffeature[i].features[j]; 
2821                     } else if (rc == -1) { 
2822                         goto error; 
2823                     } /* else unres was duplicated */ 
2824                 } 
2825             } 
2826  
2827             /* duplicate if-feature's extensions */ 
2828             retval->iffeature[i].ext_size = node->iffeature[i].ext_size; 
2829             if (lys_ext_dup(module, node->iffeature[i].ext, node->iffeature[i].ext_size, 
2830                             &retval->iffeature[i], LYEXT_PAR_IFFEATURE, &retval->iffeature[i].ext, shallow, unres)) { 
2831                 goto error; 
2832             } 
2833         } 
2834  
2835         /* inherit config flags */ 
2836         p = parent; 
2837         do { 
2838             for (iter = p; iter && (iter->nodetype == LYS_USES); iter = iter->parent); 
2839         } while (iter && iter->nodetype == LYS_AUGMENT && (p = lys_parent(iter))); 
2840         if (iter) { 
2841             flags = iter->flags & LYS_CONFIG_MASK; 
2842         } else { 
2843             /* default */ 
2844             flags = LYS_CONFIG_W; 
2845         } 
2846  
2847         switch (finalize) { 
2848         case 1: 
2849             /* inherit config flags */ 
2850             if (retval->flags & LYS_CONFIG_SET) { 
2851                 /* skip nodes with an explicit config value */ 
2852                 if ((flags & LYS_CONFIG_R) && (retval->flags & LYS_CONFIG_W)) { 
2853                     LOGVAL(LYE_INARG, LY_VLOG_LYS, retval, "true", "config"); 
2854                     LOGVAL(LYE_SPEC, LY_VLOG_PREV, NULL, "State nodes cannot have configuration nodes as children."); 
2855                     goto error; 
2856                 } 
2857                 break; 
2858             } 
2859  
2860             if (retval->nodetype != LYS_USES) { 
2861                 retval->flags = (retval->flags & ~LYS_CONFIG_MASK) | flags; 
2862             } 
2863  
2864             /* inherit status */ 
2865             if ((parent->flags & LYS_STATUS_MASK) > (retval->flags & LYS_STATUS_MASK)) { 
2866                 /* but do it only in case the parent has a stonger status */ 
2867                 retval->flags &= ~LYS_STATUS_MASK; 
2868                 retval->flags |= (parent->flags & LYS_STATUS_MASK); 
2869             } 
2870             break; 
2871         case 2: 
2872             /* erase config flags */ 
2873             retval->flags &= ~LYS_CONFIG_MASK; 
2874             retval->flags &= ~LYS_CONFIG_SET; 
2875             break; 
2876         } 
2877  
2878         /* connect it to the parent */ 
2879         if (lys_node_addchild(parent, retval->module, retval)) { 
2880             goto error; 
2881         } 
2882  
2883         /* go recursively */ 
2884         if (!(node->nodetype & (LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST))) { 
2885             LY_TREE_FOR(node->child, iter) { 
2886                 if (iter->nodetype & LYS_GROUPING) { 
2887                     /* do not instantiate groupings */ 
2888                     continue; 
2889                 } 
2890                 if (!lys_node_dup_recursion(module, retval, iter, unres, 0, finalize)) { 
2891                     goto error; 
2892                 } 
2893             } 
2894         } 
2895  
2896         if (finalize == 1) { 
2897             /* check that configuration lists have keys 
2898              * - we really want to check keys_size in original node, because the keys are 
2899              * not yet resolved here, it is done below in nodetype specific part */ 
2900             if ((retval->nodetype == LYS_LIST) && (retval->flags & LYS_CONFIG_W) 
2901                     && !((struct lys_node_list *)node)->keys_size) { 
2902                 LOGVAL(LYE_MISSCHILDSTMT, LY_VLOG_LYS, retval, "key", "list"); 
2903                 goto error; 
2904             } 
2905         } 
2906     } else { 
2907         memcpy(retval->iffeature, node->iffeature, retval->iffeature_size * sizeof *retval->iffeature); 
2908     } 
2909  
2910     /* 
2911      * duplicate specific part of the structure 
2912      */ 
2913     switch (node->nodetype) { 
2914     case LYS_CONTAINER: 
2915         if (cont_orig->when) { 
2916             cont->when = lys_when_dup(module, cont_orig->when, shallow, unres); 
2917             LY_CHECK_GOTO(!cont->when, error); 
2918         } 
2919         cont->presence = lydict_insert(ctx, cont_orig->presence, 0); 
2920  
2921         if (cont_orig->must) { 
2922             cont->must = lys_restr_dup(module, cont_orig->must, cont_orig->must_size, shallow, unres); 
2923             LY_CHECK_GOTO(!cont->must, error); 
2924             cont->must_size = cont_orig->must_size; 
2925         } 
2926  
2927         /* typedefs are not needed in instantiated grouping, nor the deviation's shallow copy */ 
2928  
2929         break; 
2930     case LYS_CHOICE: 
2931         if (choice_orig->when) { 
2932             choice->when = lys_when_dup(module, choice_orig->when, shallow, unres); 
2933             LY_CHECK_GOTO(!choice->when, error); 
2934         } 
2935  
2936         if (!shallow) { 
2937             if (choice_orig->dflt) { 
2938                 rc = lys_get_sibling(choice->child, lys_node_module(retval)->name, 0, choice_orig->dflt->name, 0, 
2939                                             LYS_ANYDATA | LYS_CASE | LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_LIST, 
2940                                             (const struct lys_node **)&choice->dflt); 
2941                 if (rc) { 
2942                     if (rc == EXIT_FAILURE) { 
2943                         LOGINT; 
2944                     } 
2945                     goto error; 
2946                 } 
2947             } else { 
2948                 /* useless to check return value, we don't know whether 
2949                 * there really wasn't any default defined or it just hasn't 
2950                 * been resolved, we just hope for the best :) 
2951                 */ 
2952                 unres_schema_dup(module, unres, choice_orig, UNRES_CHOICE_DFLT, choice); 
2953             } 
2954         } else { 
2955             choice->dflt = choice_orig->dflt; 
2956         } 
2957         break; 
2958  
2959     case LYS_LEAF: 
2960         if (lys_type_dup(module, retval, &(leaf->type), &(leaf_orig->type), lys_ingrouping(retval), shallow, unres)) { 
2961             goto error; 
2962         } 
2963         leaf->units = lydict_insert(module->ctx, leaf_orig->units, 0); 
2964  
2965         if (leaf_orig->dflt) { 
2966             leaf->dflt = lydict_insert(ctx, leaf_orig->dflt, 0); 
2967             if (!lys_ingrouping(retval) || (leaf->type.base != LY_TYPE_LEAFREF)) { 
2968                 /* problem is when it is an identityref referencing an identity from a module 
2969                  * and we are using the grouping in a different module */ 
2970                 if (leaf->type.base == LY_TYPE_IDENT) { 
2971                     tmp_mod = leaf_orig->module; 
2972                 } else { 
2973                     tmp_mod = module; 
2974                 } 
2975                 if (unres_schema_add_node(tmp_mod, unres, &leaf->type, UNRES_TYPE_DFLT, 
2976                                           (struct lys_node *)(&leaf->dflt)) == -1) { 
2977                     goto error; 
2978                 } 
2979             } 
2980         } 
2981  
2982         if (leaf_orig->must) { 
2983             leaf->must = lys_restr_dup(module, leaf_orig->must, leaf_orig->must_size, shallow, unres); 
2984             LY_CHECK_GOTO(!leaf->must, error); 
2985             leaf->must_size = leaf_orig->must_size; 
2986         } 
2987  
2988         if (leaf_orig->when) { 
2989             leaf->when = lys_when_dup(module, leaf_orig->when, shallow, unres); 
2990             LY_CHECK_GOTO(!leaf->when, error); 
2991         } 
2992         break; 
2993  
2994     case LYS_LEAFLIST: 
2995         if (lys_type_dup(module, retval, &(llist->type), &(llist_orig->type), lys_ingrouping(retval), shallow, unres)) { 
2996             goto error; 
2997         } 
2998         llist->units = lydict_insert(module->ctx, llist_orig->units, 0); 
2999  
3000         llist->min = llist_orig->min; 
3001         llist->max = llist_orig->max; 
3002  
3003         if (llist_orig->must) { 
3004             llist->must = lys_restr_dup(module, llist_orig->must, llist_orig->must_size, shallow, unres); 
3005             LY_CHECK_GOTO(!llist->must, error); 
3006             llist->must_size = llist_orig->must_size; 
3007         } 
3008  
3009         if (llist_orig->dflt) { 
3010             llist->dflt = malloc(llist_orig->dflt_size * sizeof *llist->dflt); 
3011             LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(!llist->dflt, LOGMEM, error); 
3012             llist->dflt_size = llist_orig->dflt_size; 
3013  
3014             for (i = 0; i < llist->dflt_size; i++) { 
3015                 llist->dflt[i] = lydict_insert(ctx, llist_orig->dflt[i], 0); 
3016                 if (!lys_ingrouping(retval) || (llist->type.base != LY_TYPE_LEAFREF)) { 
3017                     if ((llist->type.base == LY_TYPE_IDENT) && !strchr(llist->dflt[i], ':') && (module != llist_orig->module)) { 
3018                         tmp_mod = llist_orig->module; 
3019                     } else { 
3020                         tmp_mod = module; 
3021                     } 
3022                     if (unres_schema_add_node(tmp_mod, unres, &llist->type, UNRES_TYPE_DFLT, 
3023                                               (struct lys_node *)(&llist->dflt[i])) == -1) { 
3024                         goto error; 
3025                     } 
3026                 } 
3027             } 
3028         } 
3029  
3030         if (llist_orig->when) { 
3031             llist->when = lys_when_dup(module, llist_orig->when, shallow, unres); 
3032         } 
3033         break; 
3034  
3035     case LYS_LIST: 
3036         list->min = list_orig->min; 
3037         list->max = list_orig->max; 
3038  
3039         if (list_orig->must) { 
3040             list->must = lys_restr_dup(module, list_orig->must, list_orig->must_size, shallow, unres); 
3041             LY_CHECK_GOTO(!list->must, error); 
3042             list->must_size = list_orig->must_size; 
3043         } 
3044  
3045         /* typedefs are not needed in instantiated grouping, nor the deviation's shallow copy */ 
3046  
3047         if (list_orig->keys_size) { 
3048             list->keys = calloc(list_orig->keys_size, sizeof *list->keys); 
3049             LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(!list->keys, LOGMEM, error); 
3050             list->keys_str = lydict_insert(ctx, list_orig->keys_str, 0); 
3051             list->keys_size = list_orig->keys_size; 
3052  
3053             if (!shallow) { 
3054                 /* the keys are going to be resolved only if the list is instantiated in data tree, not just 
3055                  * in another grouping */ 
3056                 for (iter = parent; iter && iter->nodetype != LYS_GROUPING; iter = iter->parent); 
3057                 if (!iter && unres_schema_add_node(module, unres, list, UNRES_LIST_KEYS, NULL) == -1) { 
3058                     goto error; 
3059                 } 
3060             } else { 
3061                 memcpy(list->keys, list_orig->keys, list_orig->keys_size * sizeof *list->keys); 
3062             } 
3063         } 
3064  
3065         if (list_orig->unique) { 
3066             list->unique = malloc(list_orig->unique_size * sizeof *list->unique); 
3067             LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(!list->unique, LOGMEM, error); 
3068             list->unique_size = list_orig->unique_size; 
3069  
3070             for (i = 0; i < list->unique_size; ++i) { 
3071                 list->unique[i].expr = malloc(list_orig->unique[i].expr_size * sizeof *list->unique[i].expr); 
3072                 LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(!list->unique[i].expr, LOGMEM, error); 
3073                 list->unique[i].expr_size = list_orig->unique[i].expr_size; 
3074                 for (j = 0; j < list->unique[i].expr_size; j++) { 
3075                     list->unique[i].expr[j] = lydict_insert(ctx, list_orig->unique[i].expr[j], 0); 
3076  
3077                     /* if it stays in unres list, duplicate it also there */ 
3078                     unique_info = malloc(sizeof *unique_info); 
3079                     LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(!unique_info, LOGMEM, error); 
3080                     unique_info->list = (struct lys_node *)list; 
3081                     unique_info->expr = list->unique[i].expr[j]; 
3082                     unique_info->trg_type = &list->unique[i].trg_type; 
3083                     unres_schema_dup(module, unres, &list_orig, UNRES_LIST_UNIQ, unique_info); 
3084                 } 
3085             } 
3086         } 
3087  
3088         if (list_orig->when) { 
3089             list->when = lys_when_dup(module, list_orig->when, shallow, unres); 
3090             LY_CHECK_GOTO(!list->when, error); 
3091         } 
3092         break; 
3093  
3094     case LYS_ANYXML: 
3095     case LYS_ANYDATA: 
3096         if (any_orig->must) { 
3097             any->must = lys_restr_dup(module, any_orig->must, any_orig->must_size, shallow, unres); 
3098             LY_CHECK_GOTO(!any->must, error); 
3099             any->must_size = any_orig->must_size; 
3100         } 
3101  
3102         if (any_orig->when) { 
3103             any->when = lys_when_dup(module, any_orig->when, shallow, unres); 
3104             LY_CHECK_GOTO(!any->when, error); 
3105         } 
3106         break; 
3107  
3108     case LYS_USES: 
3109         uses->grp = uses_orig->grp; 
3110  
3111         if (uses_orig->when) { 
3112             uses->when = lys_when_dup(module, uses_orig->when, shallow, unres); 
3113             LY_CHECK_GOTO(!uses->when, error); 
3114         } 
3115         /* it is not needed to duplicate refine, nor augment. They are already applied to the uses children */ 
3116         break; 
3117  
3118     case LYS_CASE: 
3119         if (cs_orig->when) { 
3120             cs->when = lys_when_dup(module, cs_orig->when, shallow, unres); 
3121             LY_CHECK_GOTO(!cs->when, error); 
3122         } 
3123         break; 
3124  
3125     case LYS_ACTION: 
3126     case LYS_RPC: 
3127     case LYS_INPUT: 
3128     case LYS_OUTPUT: 
3129     case LYS_NOTIF: 
3130         /* typedefs are not needed in instantiated grouping, nor the deviation's shallow copy */ 
3131         break; 
3132  
3133     default: 
3134         /* LY_NODE_AUGMENT */ 
3135         LOGINT; 
3136         goto error; 
3137     } 
3138  
3139     return retval; 
3140  
3141 error: 
3142  
3143     lys_node_free(retval, NULL, 0); 
3144     return NULL; 
3145 } 
3146  
3147 int 
3148 lys_has_xpath(const struct lys_node *node) 
3149 { 
3150     assert(node); 
3151  
3152     switch (node->nodetype) { 
3153     case LYS_AUGMENT: 
3154         if (((struct lys_node_augment *)node)->when) { 
3155             return 1; 
3156         } 
3157         break; 
3158     case LYS_CASE: 
3159         if (((struct lys_node_case *)node)->when) { 
3160             return 1; 
3161         } 
3162         break; 
3163     case LYS_CHOICE: 
3164         if (((struct lys_node_choice *)node)->when) { 
3165             return 1; 
3166         } 
3167         break; 
3168     case LYS_ANYDATA: 
3169         if (((struct lys_node_anydata *)node)->when || ((struct lys_node_anydata *)node)->must_size) { 
3170             return 1; 
3171         } 
3172         break; 
3173     case LYS_LEAF: 
3174         if (((struct lys_node_leaf *)node)->when || ((struct lys_node_leaf *)node)->must_size) { 
3175             return 1; 
3176         } 
3177         break; 
3178     case LYS_LEAFLIST: 
3179         if (((struct lys_node_leaflist *)node)->when || ((struct lys_node_leaflist *)node)->must_size) { 
3180             return 1; 
3181         } 
3182         break; 
3183     case LYS_LIST: 
3184         if (((struct lys_node_list *)node)->when || ((struct lys_node_list *)node)->must_size) { 
3185             return 1; 
3186         } 
3187         break; 
3188     case LYS_CONTAINER: 
3189         if (((struct lys_node_container *)node)->when || ((struct lys_node_container *)node)->must_size) { 
3190             return 1; 
3191         } 
3192         break; 
3193     case LYS_INPUT: 
3194     case LYS_OUTPUT: 
3195         if (((struct lys_node_inout *)node)->must_size) { 
3196             return 1; 
3197         } 
3198         break; 
3199     case LYS_NOTIF: 
3200         if (((struct lys_node_notif *)node)->must_size) { 
3201             return 1; 
3202         } 
3203         break; 
3204     case LYS_USES: 
3205         if (((struct lys_node_uses *)node)->when) { 
3206             return 1; 
3207         } 
3208         break; 
3209     default: 
3210         /* does not have XPath */ 
3211         break; 
3212     } 
3213  
3214     return 0; 
3215 } 
3216  
3217 /* 
3218  * shallow - 
3219  *         - do not inherit status from the parent 
3220  */ 
3221 struct lys_node * 
3222 lys_node_dup(struct lys_module *module, struct lys_node *parent, const struct lys_node *node, 
3223              struct unres_schema *unres, int shallow) 
3224 { 
3225     struct lys_node *p = NULL; 
3226     int finalize = 0; 
3227     struct lys_node *result, *iter, *next; 
3228  
3229     if (!shallow) { 
3230         /* get know where in schema tree we are to know what should be done during instantiation of the grouping */ 
3231         for (p = parent; 
3232              p && !(p->nodetype & (LYS_NOTIF | LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT | LYS_RPC | LYS_ACTION | LYS_GROUPING)); 
3233              p = lys_parent(p)); 
3234         finalize = p ? ((p->nodetype == LYS_GROUPING) ? 0 : 2) : 1; 
3235     } 
3236  
3237     result = lys_node_dup_recursion(module, parent, node, unres, shallow, finalize); 
3238     if (finalize) { 
3239         /* check xpath expressions in the instantiated tree */ 
3240         for (iter = next = result; iter; iter = next) { 
3241             if (lys_has_xpath(iter) && unres_schema_add_node(module, unres, iter, UNRES_XPATH, NULL) == -1) { 
3242                 /* invalid xpath */ 
3243                 return NULL; 
3244             } 
3245  
3246             /* select next item */ 
3247             if (iter->nodetype & (LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_ANYDATA | LYS_GROUPING)) { 
3248                 /* child exception for leafs, leaflists and anyxml without children, ignore groupings */ 
3249                 next = NULL; 
3250             } else { 
3251                 next = iter->child; 
3252             } 
3253             if (!next) { 
3254                 /* no children, try siblings */ 
3255                 if (iter == result) { 
3256                     /* we are done, no next element to process */ 
3257                     break; 
3258                 } 
3259                 next = iter->next; 
3260             } 
3261             while (!next) { 
3262                 /* parent is already processed, go to its sibling */ 
3263                 iter = lys_parent(iter); 
3264                 if (lys_parent(iter) == lys_parent(result)) { 
3265                     /* we are done, no next element to process */ 
3266                     break; 
3267                 } 
3268                 next = iter->next; 
3269             } 
3270         } 
3271     } 
3272  
3273     return result; 
3274 } 
3275  
3276 void 
3277 lys_node_switch(struct lys_node *dst, struct lys_node *src) 
3278 { 
3279     struct lys_node *child; 
3280  
3281     assert((dst->module == src->module) && ly_strequal(dst->name, src->name, 1) && (dst->nodetype == src->nodetype)); 
3282  
3283     /* sibling next */ 
3284     if (dst->prev->next) { 
3285         dst->prev->next = src; 
3286     } 
3287  
3288     /* sibling prev */ 
3289     if (dst->next) { 
3290         dst->next->prev = src; 
3291     } else { 
3292         for (child = dst->prev; child->prev->next; child = child->prev); 
3293         child->prev = src; 
3294     } 
3295  
3296     /* next */ 
3297     src->next = dst->next; 
3298     dst->next = NULL; 
3299  
3300     /* prev */ 
3301     if (dst->prev != dst) { 
3302         src->prev = dst->prev; 
3303     } 
3304     dst->prev = dst; 
3305  
3306     /* parent child */ 
3307     if (dst->parent) { 
3308         if (dst->parent->child == dst) { 
3309             dst->parent->child = src; 
3310         } 
3311     } else if (lys_main_module(dst->module)->data == dst) { 
3312         lys_main_module(dst->module)->data = src; 
3313     } 
3314  
3315     /* parent */ 
3316     src->parent = dst->parent;    dst->parent = NULL; 
3317  
3318  
3319     /* child parent */ 
3320     LY_TREE_FOR(dst->child, child) { 
3321         if (child->parent == dst) { 
3322             child->parent = src; 
3323         } 
3324     } 
3325  
3326     /* child */ 
3327     src->child = dst->child; 
3328     dst->child = NULL; 
3329  
3330     /* node-specific data */ 
3331     switch (dst->nodetype) { 
3332     case LYS_CONTAINER: 
3333         ((struct lys_node_container *)src)->tpdf_size = ((struct lys_node_container *)dst)->tpdf_size; 
3334         ((struct lys_node_container *)src)->tpdf = ((struct lys_node_container *)dst)->tpdf; 
3335         ((struct lys_node_container *)dst)->tpdf_size = 0; 
3336         ((struct lys_node_container *)dst)->tpdf = NULL; 
3337         break; 
3338     case LYS_LIST: 
3339         ((struct lys_node_list *)src)->tpdf_size = ((struct lys_node_list *)dst)->tpdf_size; 
3340         ((struct lys_node_list *)src)->tpdf = ((struct lys_node_list *)dst)->tpdf; 
3341         ((struct lys_node_list *)dst)->tpdf_size = 0; 
3342         ((struct lys_node_list *)dst)->tpdf = NULL; 
3343         break; 
3344     case LYS_RPC: 
3345     case LYS_ACTION: 
3346         ((struct lys_node_rpc_action *)src)->tpdf_size = ((struct lys_node_rpc_action *)dst)->tpdf_size; 
3347         ((struct lys_node_rpc_action *)src)->tpdf = ((struct lys_node_rpc_action *)dst)->tpdf; 
3348         ((struct lys_node_rpc_action *)dst)->tpdf_size = 0; 
3349         ((struct lys_node_rpc_action *)dst)->tpdf = NULL; 
3350         break; 
3351     case LYS_NOTIF: 
3352         ((struct lys_node_notif *)src)->tpdf_size = ((struct lys_node_notif *)dst)->tpdf_size; 
3353         ((struct lys_node_notif *)src)->tpdf = ((struct lys_node_notif *)dst)->tpdf; 
3354         ((struct lys_node_notif *)dst)->tpdf_size = 0; 
3355         ((struct lys_node_notif *)dst)->tpdf = NULL; 
3356         break; 
3357     case LYS_INPUT: 
3358     case LYS_OUTPUT: 
3359         ((struct lys_node_inout *)src)->tpdf_size = ((struct lys_node_inout *)dst)->tpdf_size; 
3360         ((struct lys_node_inout *)src)->tpdf = ((struct lys_node_inout *)dst)->tpdf; 
3361         ((struct lys_node_inout *)dst)->tpdf_size = 0; 
3362         ((struct lys_node_inout *)dst)->tpdf = NULL; 
3363         break; 
3364     default: 
3365         /* nothing special */ 
3366         break; 
3367     } 
3368  
3369 } 
3370  
3371 void 
3372 lys_free(struct lys_module *module, void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv), int remove_from_ctx) 
3373 { 
3374     struct ly_ctx *ctx; 
3375     int i; 
3376  
3377     if (!module) { 
3378         return; 
3379     } 
3380  
3381     /* remove schema from the context */ 
3382     ctx = module->ctx; 
3383     if (remove_from_ctx && ctx->models.used) { 
3384         for (i = 0; i < ctx->models.used; i++) { 
3385             if (ctx->models.list[i] == module) { 
3386                 /* move all the models to not change the order in the list */ 
3387                 ctx->models.used--; 
3388                 memmove(&ctx->models.list[i], ctx->models.list[i + 1], (ctx->models.used - i) * sizeof *ctx->models.list); 
3389                 ctx->models.list[ctx->models.used] = NULL; 
3390                 /* we are done */ 
3391                 break; 
3392             } 
3393         } 
3394     } 
3395  
3396     /* common part with struct ly_submodule */ 
3397     module_free_common(module, private_destructor); 
3398  
3399     /* specific items to free */ 
3400     lydict_remove(ctx, module->ns); 
3401  
3402     free(module); 
3403 } 
3404  
3405 static void 
3406 lys_features_disable_recursive(struct lys_feature *f) 
3407 { 
3408     unsigned int i; 
3409     struct lys_feature *depf; 
3410  
3411     /* disable the feature */ 
3412     f->flags &= ~LYS_FENABLED; 
3413  
3414     /* by disabling feature we have to disable also all features that depends on this feature */ 
3415     if (f->depfeatures) { 
3416         for (i = 0; i < f->depfeatures->number; i++) { 
3417             depf = (struct lys_feature *)f->depfeatures->set.g[i]; 
3418             if (depf->flags & LYS_FENABLED) { 
3419                 lys_features_disable_recursive(depf); 
3420             } 
3421         } 
3422     } 
3423 } 
3424  
3425  
3426 /* 
3427  * op: 1 - enable, 0 - disable 
3428  */ 
3429 static int 
3430 lys_features_change(const struct lys_module *module, const char *name, int op) 
3431 { 
3432     int all = 0; 
3433     int i, j, k; 
3434     int progress, faili, failj, failk; 
3435  
3436     uint8_t fsize; 
3437     struct lys_feature *f; 
3438  
3439     if (!module || !name || !strlen(name)) { 
3440         return EXIT_FAILURE; 
3441     } 
3442  
3443     if (!strcmp(name, "*")) { 
3444         /* enable all */ 
3445         all = 1; 
3446     } 
3447  
3448     progress = failk = 1; 
3449     while (progress && failk) { 
3450         for (i = -1, failk = progress = 0; i < module->inc_size; i++) { 
3451             if (i == -1) { 
3452                 fsize = module->features_size; 
3453                 f = module->features; 
3454             } else { 
3455                 fsize = module->inc[i].submodule->features_size; 
3456                 f = module->inc[i].submodule->features; 
3457             } 
3458  
3459             for (j = 0; j < fsize; j++) { 
3460                 if (all || !strcmp(f[j].name, name)) { 
3461                     if ((op && (f[j].flags & LYS_FENABLED)) || (!op && !(f[j].flags & LYS_FENABLED))) { 
3462                         if (all) { 
3463                             /* skip already set features */ 
3464                             continue; 
3465                         } else { 
3466                             /* feature already set correctly */ 
3467                             return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
3468                         } 
3469                     } 
3470  
3471                     if (op) { 
3472                         /* check referenced features if they are enabled */ 
3473                         for (k = 0; k < f[j].iffeature_size; k++) { 
3474                             if (!resolve_iffeature(&f[j].iffeature[k])) { 
3475                                 if (all) { 
3476                                     faili = i; 
3477                                     failj = j; 
3478                                     failk = k + 1; 
3479                                     break; 
3480                                 } else { 
3481                                     LOGERR(LY_EINVAL, "Feature \"%s\" is disabled by its %d. if-feature condition.", 
3482                                            f[j].name, k + 1); 
3483                                     return EXIT_FAILURE; 
3484                                 } 
3485                             } 
3486                         } 
3487  
3488                         if (k == f[j].iffeature_size) { 
3489                             /* the last check passed, do the change */ 
3490                             f[j].flags |= LYS_FENABLED; 
3491                             progress++; 
3492                         } 
3493                     } else { 
3494                         lys_features_disable_recursive(&f[j]); 
3495                         progress++; 
3496                     } 
3497                     if (!all) { 
3498                         /* stop in case changing a single feature */ 
3499                         return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
3500                     } 
3501                 } 
3502             } 
3503         } 
3504     } 
3505     if (failk) { 
3506         /* print info about the last failing feature */ 
3507         LOGERR(LY_EINVAL, "Feature \"%s\" is disabled by its %d. if-feature condition.", 
3508                faili == -1 ? module->features[failj].name : module->inc[faili].submodule->features[failj].name, failk); 
3509         return EXIT_FAILURE; 
3510     } 
3511  
3512     if (all) { 
3513         return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
3514     } else { 
3515         /* the specified feature not found */ 
3516         return EXIT_FAILURE; 
3517     } 
3518 } 
3519  
3520 API int 
3521 lys_features_enable(const struct lys_module *module, const char *feature) 
3522 { 
3523     return lys_features_change(module, feature, 1); 
3524 } 
3525  
3526 API int 
3527 lys_features_disable(const struct lys_module *module, const char *feature) 
3528 { 
3529     return lys_features_change(module, feature, 0); 
3530 } 
3531  
3532 API int 
3533 lys_features_state(const struct lys_module *module, const char *feature) 
3534 { 
3535     int i, j; 
3536  
3537     if (!module || !feature) { 
3538         return -1; 
3539     } 
3540  
3541     /* search for the specified feature */ 
3542     /* module itself */ 
3543     for (i = 0; i < module->features_size; i++) { 
3544         if (!strcmp(feature, module->features[i].name)) { 
3545             if (module->features[i].flags & LYS_FENABLED) { 
3546                 return 1; 
3547             } else { 
3548                 return 0; 
3549             } 
3550         } 
3551     } 
3552  
3553     /* submodules */ 
3554     for (j = 0; j < module->inc_size; j++) { 
3555         for (i = 0; i < module->inc[j].submodule->features_size; i++) { 
3556             if (!strcmp(feature, module->inc[j].submodule->features[i].name)) { 
3557                 if (module->inc[j].submodule->features[i].flags & LYS_FENABLED) { 
3558                     return 1; 
3559                 } else { 
3560                     return 0; 
3561                 } 
3562             } 
3563         } 
3564     } 
3565  
3566     /* feature definition not found */ 
3567     return -1; 
3568 } 
3569  
3570 API const char ** 
3571 lys_features_list(const struct lys_module *module, uint8_t **states) 
3572 { 
3573     const char **result = NULL; 
3574     int i, j; 
3575     unsigned int count; 
3576  
3577     if (!module) { 
3578         return NULL; 
3579     } 
3580  
3581     count = module->features_size; 
3582     for (i = 0; i < module->inc_size; i++) { 
3583         count += module->inc[i].submodule->features_size; 
3584     } 
3585     result = malloc((count + 1) * sizeof *result); 
3586     LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!result, LOGMEM, NULL); 
3587  
3588     if (states) { 
3589         *states = malloc((count + 1) * sizeof **states); 
3590         LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!(*states), LOGMEM; free(result), NULL); 
3591     } 
3592     count = 0; 
3593  
3594     /* module itself */ 
3595     for (i = 0; i < module->features_size; i++) { 
3596         result[count] = module->features[i].name; 
3597         if (states) { 
3598             if (module->features[i].flags & LYS_FENABLED) { 
3599                 (*states)[count] = 1; 
3600             } else { 
3601                 (*states)[count] = 0; 
3602             } 
3603         } 
3604         count++; 
3605     } 
3606  
3607     /* submodules */ 
3608     for (j = 0; j < module->inc_size; j++) { 
3609         for (i = 0; i < module->inc[j].submodule->features_size; i++) { 
3610             result[count] = module->inc[j].submodule->features[i].name; 
3611             if (states) { 
3612                 if (module->inc[j].submodule->features[i].flags & LYS_FENABLED) { 
3613                     (*states)[count] = 1; 
3614                 } else { 
3615                     (*states)[count] = 0; 
3616                 } 
3617             } 
3618             count++; 
3619         } 
3620     } 
3621  
3622     /* terminating NULL byte */ 
3623     result[count] = NULL; 
3624  
3625     return result; 
3626 }

3639 lys_main_module(const struct lys_module *module) 
3640 { 
3641     if (!module) {

3648 API struct lys_node * 
3649 lys_parent(const struct lys_node *node) 
3650 { 
3651     struct lys_node *parent; 
3652  
3653     if (!node) { 
3654         return NULL; 
3655     } 
3656  
3657     if (node->nodetype == LYS_EXT) { 
3658         if (((struct lys_ext_instance_complex*)node)->parent_type != LYEXT_PAR_NODE) { 
3659             return NULL; 
3660         } 
3661         parent = (struct lys_node*)((struct lys_ext_instance_complex*)node)->parent; 
3662     } else if (!node->parent) { 
3663         return NULL; 
3664     } else { 
3665         parent = node->parent; 
3666     } 
3667  
3668     if (parent->nodetype == LYS_AUGMENT) { 
3669         return ((struct lys_node_augment *)parent)->target; 
3670     } else { 
3671         return parent; 
3672     } 
3673 } 
3674  
3675 struct lys_node ** 
3676 lys_child(const struct lys_node *node, LYS_NODE nodetype) 
3677 { 
3678     void *pp; 
3679     assert(node); 
3680  
3681     if (node->nodetype == LYS_EXT) { 
3682         pp = lys_ext_complex_get_substmt(lys_snode2stmt(nodetype), (struct lys_ext_instance_complex*)node, NULL); 
3683         if (!pp) { 
3684             return NULL; 
3685         } 
3686         return (struct lys_node **)pp; 
3687     } else if (node->nodetype & (LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_ANYDATA)) { 
3688         return NULL; 
3689     } else { 
3690         return (struct lys_node **)&node->child; 
3691     } 
3692 }

3703  
3704     prev = node->priv; 
3705     ((struct lys_node *)node)->priv = priv; 
3706

3709  
3710 int 
3711 lys_leaf_add_leafref_target(struct lys_node_leaf *leafref_target, struct lys_node *leafref) 
3712 { 
3713     struct lys_node_leaf *iter = leafref_target; 
3714  
3715     if (!(leafref_target->nodetype & (LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST))) { 
3716         LOGINT; 
3717         return -1; 
3718     } 
3719  
3720     /* check for config flag */ 
3721     if (((struct lys_node_leaf*)leafref)->type.info.lref.req != -1 && 
3722             (leafref->flags & LYS_CONFIG_W) && (leafref_target->flags & LYS_CONFIG_R)) { 
3723         LOGVAL(LYE_SPEC, LY_VLOG_LYS, leafref, 
3724                "The leafref %s is config but refers to a non-config %s.", 
3725                strnodetype(leafref->nodetype), strnodetype(leafref_target->nodetype)); 
3726         return -1; 
3727     } 
3728     /* check for cycles */ 
3729     while (iter && iter->type.base == LY_TYPE_LEAFREF) { 
3730         if ((void *)iter == (void *)leafref) { 
3731             /* cycle detected */ 
3732             LOGVAL(LYE_CIRC_LEAFREFS, LY_VLOG_LYS, leafref); 
3733             return -1; 
3734         } 
3735         iter = iter->type.info.lref.target; 
3736     } 
3737  
3738     /* create fake child - the ly_set structure to hold the list of 
3739      * leafrefs referencing the leaf(-list) */ 
3740     if (!leafref_target->backlinks) { 
3741         leafref_target->backlinks = (void*)ly_set_new(); 
3742         if (!leafref_target->backlinks) { 
3743             LOGMEM; 
3744             return -1; 
3745         } 
3746     } 
3747     ly_set_add(leafref_target->backlinks, leafref, 0); 
3748  
3749     return 0; 
3750 }

3753 #if 0 
3754  
3755 static const char * 
3756 lys_data_path_reverse(const struct lys_node *node, char * const buf, uint32_t buf_len) 
3757 { 
3758     struct lys_module *prev_mod; 
3759     uint32_t str_len, mod_len, buf_idx; 
3760  
3761     if (!(node->nodetype & (LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_LIST | LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_ANYDATA))) { 
3762         LOGINT; 
3763         return NULL; 
3764     } 
3765  
3766     buf_idx = buf_len - 1; 
3767     buf[buf_idx] = '\0'; 
3768  
3769     while (node) { 
3770         if (lys_parent(node)) { 
3771             prev_mod = lys_node_module(lys_parent(node)); 
3772         } else { 
3773             prev_mod = NULL; 
3774         } 
3775  
3776         if (node->nodetype & (LYS_CONTAINER | LYS_LIST | LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_ANYDATA)) { 
3777             str_len = strlen(node->name); 
3778  
3779             if (prev_mod != node->module) { 
3780                 mod_len = strlen(node->module->name); 
3781             } else { 
3782                 mod_len = 0; 
3783             } 
3784  
3785             if (buf_idx < 1 + (mod_len ? mod_len + 1 : 0) + str_len) { 
3786                 LOGINT; 
3787                 return NULL; 
3788             } 
3789  
3790             buf_idx -= 1 + (mod_len ? mod_len + 1 : 0) + str_len; 
3791  
3792             buf[buf_idx] = '/'; 
3793             if (mod_len) { 
3794                 memcpy(buf + buf_idx + 1, node->module->name, mod_len); 
3795                 buf[buf_idx + 1 + mod_len] = ':'; 
3796             } 
3797             memcpy(buf + buf_idx + 1 + (mod_len ? mod_len + 1 : 0), node->name, str_len); 
3798         } 
3799  
3800         node = lys_parent(node); 
3801     } 
3802  
3803     return buf + buf_idx; 
3804 }
