3807  
3808 API struct ly_set * 
3809 lys_xpath_atomize(const struct lys_node *ctx_node, enum lyxp_node_type ctx_node_type, const char *expr, int options) 
3810 { 
3811     struct lyxp_set set; 
3812     struct ly_set *ret_set; 
3813     uint32_t i; 
3814  
3815     if (!ctx_node || !expr) { 
3816         return NULL; 
3817     } 
3818  
3819     /* adjust the root */ 
3820     if ((ctx_node_type == LYXP_NODE_ROOT) || (ctx_node_type == LYXP_NODE_ROOT_CONFIG)) { 
3821         do { 
3822             ctx_node = lys_getnext(NULL, NULL, lys_node_module(ctx_node), 0); 
3823         } while ((ctx_node_type == LYXP_NODE_ROOT_CONFIG) && (ctx_node->flags & LYS_CONFIG_R)); 
3824     } 
3825  
3826     memset(&set, 0, sizeof set); 
3827  
3828     if (options & LYXP_MUST) { 
3829         options &= ~LYXP_MUST; 
3830         options |= LYXP_SNODE_MUST; 
3831     } else if (options & LYXP_WHEN) { 
3832         options &= ~LYXP_WHEN; 
3833         options |= LYXP_SNODE_WHEN; 
3834     } else { 
3835         options |= LYXP_SNODE; 
3836     } 
3837  
3838     if (lyxp_atomize(expr, ctx_node, ctx_node_type, &set, options, NULL)) { 
3839         free(set.val.snodes); 
3840         LOGVAL(LYE_SPEC, LY_VLOG_LYS, ctx_node, "Resolving XPath expression \"%s\" failed.", expr); 
3841         return NULL; 
3842     } 
3843  
3844     ret_set = ly_set_new(); 
3845  
3846     for (i = 0; i < set.used; ++i) { 
3847         switch (set.val.snodes[i].type) { 
3848         case LYXP_NODE_ELEM: 
3849             if (ly_set_add(ret_set, set.val.snodes[i].snode, LY_SET_OPT_USEASLIST) == -1) { 
3850                 ly_set_free(ret_set); 
3851                 free(set.val.snodes); 
3852                 return NULL; 
3853             } 
3854             break; 
3855         default: 
3856             /* ignore roots, text and attr should not ever appear */ 
3857             break; 
3858         } 
3859     } 
3860  
3861     free(set.val.snodes); 
3862     return ret_set; 
3863 } 
3864  
3865 API struct ly_set * 
3866 lys_node_xpath_atomize(const struct lys_node *node, int options) 
3867 { 
3868     const struct lys_node *next, *elem, *parent, *tmp; 
3869     struct lyxp_set set; 
3870     struct ly_set *ret_set; 
3871     uint16_t i; 
3872  
3873     if (!node) { 
3874         return NULL; 
3875     } 
3876  
3877     for (parent = node; parent && !(parent->nodetype & (LYS_NOTIF | LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT)); parent = lys_parent(parent)); 
3878     if (!parent) { 
3879         /* not in input, output, or notification */ 
3880         return NULL; 
3881     } 
3882  
3883     ret_set = ly_set_new(); 
3884     if (!ret_set) { 
3885         return NULL; 
3886     } 
3887  
3888     LY_TREE_DFS_BEGIN(node, next, elem) { 
3889         if ((options & LYXP_NO_LOCAL) && !(elem->flags & LYS_XPATH_DEP)) { 
3890             /* elem has no dependencies from other subtrees and local nodes get discarded */ 
3891             goto next_iter; 
3892         } 
3893  
3894         if (lyxp_node_atomize(elem, &set, 0)) { 
3895             ly_set_free(ret_set); 
3896             free(set.val.snodes); 
3897             return NULL; 
3898         } 
3899  
3900         for (i = 0; i < set.used; ++i) { 
3901             switch (set.val.snodes[i].type) { 
3902             case LYXP_NODE_ELEM: 
3903                 if (options & LYXP_NO_LOCAL) { 
3904                     for (tmp = set.val.snodes[i].snode; tmp && (tmp != parent); tmp = lys_parent(tmp)); 
3905                     if (tmp) { 
3906                         /* in local subtree, discard */ 
3907                         break; 
3908                     } 
3909                 } 
3910                 if (ly_set_add(ret_set, set.val.snodes[i].snode, 0) == -1) { 
3911                     ly_set_free(ret_set); 
3912                     free(set.val.snodes); 
3913                     return NULL; 
3914                 } 
3915                 break; 
3916             default: 
3917                 /* ignore roots, text and attr should not ever appear */ 
3918                 break; 
3919             } 
3920         } 
3921  
3922         free(set.val.snodes); 
3923         if (!(options & LYXP_RECURSIVE)) { 
3924             break; 
3925         } 
3926 next_iter: 
3927         LY_TREE_DFS_END(node, next, elem); 
3928     } 
3929  
3930     return ret_set; 
3931 } 
3932  
3933 /* logs */ 
3934 int 
3935 apply_aug(struct lys_node_augment *augment, struct unres_schema *unres) 
3936 { 
3937     struct lys_node *child, *parent; 
3938     int clear_config; 
3939     unsigned int u; 
3940     struct lys_ext_instance *ext; 
3941  
3942     assert(augment->target && (augment->flags & LYS_NOTAPPLIED)); 
3943  
3944     if (!augment->child) { 
3945         /* nothing to apply */ 
3946         goto success; 
3947     } 
3948  
3949     /* check that all the modules are implemented */ 
3950     for (parent = augment->target; parent; parent = lys_parent(parent)) { 
3951         if (!lys_node_module(parent)->implemented) { 
3952             if (lys_set_implemented(lys_node_module(parent))) { 
3953                 LOGERR(ly_errno, "Making the augment target module \"%s\" implemented failed.", lys_node_module(parent)->name); 
3954                 return -1; 
3955             } 
3956         } 
3957     } 
3958  
3959     /* reconnect augmenting data into the target - add them to the target child list */ 
3960     if (augment->target->child) { 
3961         child = augment->target->child->prev; 
3962         child->next = augment->child; 
3963         augment->target->child->prev = augment->child->prev; 
3964         augment->child->prev = child; 
3965     } else { 
3966         augment->target->child = augment->child; 
3967     } 
3968  
3969     /* inherit config information from actual parent */ 
3970     for (parent = augment->target; parent && !(parent->nodetype & (LYS_NOTIF | LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT | LYS_RPC)); parent = lys_parent(parent)); 
3971     clear_config = (parent) ? 1 : 0; 
3972     LY_TREE_FOR(augment->child, child) { 
3973         if (inherit_config_flag(child, augment->target->flags & LYS_CONFIG_MASK, clear_config)) { 
3974             return -1; 
3975         } 
3976     } 
3977  
3978     /* inherit extensions if any */ 
3979     for (u = 0; u < augment->target->ext_size; u++) { 
3980         ext = augment->target->ext[u]; /* shortcut */ 
3981         if (ext && ext->def->plugin && (ext->def->plugin->flags & LYEXT_OPT_INHERIT)) { 
3982             if (unres_schema_add_node(lys_main_module(augment->module), unres, &ext, UNRES_EXT_FINALIZE, NULL) == -1) { 
3983                 /* something really bad happend since the extension finalization is not actually 
3984                  * being resolved while adding into unres, so something more serious with the unres 
3985                  * list itself must happened */ 
3986                 return -1; 
3987             } 
3988         } 
3989     } 
3990  
3991 success: 
3992     /* remove the flag about not applicability */ 
3993     augment->flags &= ~LYS_NOTAPPLIED; 
3994     return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
3995 } 
3996  
3997 static void 
3998 remove_aug(struct lys_node_augment *augment) 
3999 { 
4000     struct lys_node *last, *elem; 
4001  
4002     if ((augment->flags & LYS_NOTAPPLIED) || !augment->target) { 
4003         /* skip already not applied augment */ 
4004         return; 
4005     } 
4006  
4007     elem = augment->child; 
4008     if (elem) { 
4009         LY_TREE_FOR(elem, last) { 
4010             if (!last->next || (last->next->parent != (struct lys_node *)augment)) { 
4011                 break; 
4012             } 
4013         } 
4014         /* elem is first augment child, last is the last child */ 
4015  
4016         /* parent child ptr */ 
4017         if (augment->target->child == elem) { 
4018             augment->target->child = last->next; 
4019         } 
4020  
4021         /* parent child next ptr */ 
4022         if (elem->prev->next) { 
4023             elem->prev->next = last->next; 
4024         } 
4025  
4026         /* parent child prev ptr */ 
4027         if (last->next) { 
4028             last->next->prev = elem->prev; 
4029         } else if (augment->target->child) { 
4030             augment->target->child->prev = elem->prev; 
4031         } 
4032  
4033         /* update augment children themselves */ 
4034         elem->prev = last; 
4035         last->next = NULL; 
4036     } 
4037  
4038     /* augment->target still keeps the resolved target, but for lys_augment_free() 
4039      * we have to keep information that this augment is not applied to free its data */ 
4040     augment->flags |= LYS_NOTAPPLIED; 
4041 } 
4042  
4043 /* 
4044  * @param[in] module - the module where the deviation is defined 
4045  */ 
4046 static void 
4047 lys_switch_deviation(struct lys_deviation *dev, const struct lys_module *module, struct unres_schema *unres) 
4048 { 
4049     int ret, reapply = 0; 
4050     char *parent_path; 
4051     struct lys_node *target = NULL, *parent; 
4052     struct ly_set *set; 
4053  
4054     if (!dev->deviate) { 
4055         return; 
4056     } 
4057  
4058     if (dev->deviate[0].mod == LY_DEVIATE_NO) { 
4059         if (dev->orig_node) { 
4060             /* removing not-supported deviation ... */ 
4061             if (strrchr(dev->target_name, '/') != dev->target_name) { 
4062                 /* ... from a parent */ 
4063  
4064                 /* reconnect to its previous position */ 
4065                 parent = dev->orig_node->parent; 
4066                 if (parent) { 
4067                     dev->orig_node->parent = NULL; 
4068                     /* the original node was actually from augment, we have to get know if the augment is 
4069                      * applied (its module is enabled and implemented). If yes, the node will be connected 
4070                      * to the augment and the linkage with the target will be fixed if needed, otherwise 
4071                      * it will be connected only to the augment */ 
4072                     if (!(parent->flags & LYS_NOTAPPLIED)) { 
4073                         /* start with removing augment if applied before adding nodes, we have to make sure 
4074                          * that everything will be connect correctly */ 
4075                         remove_aug((struct lys_node_augment *)parent); 
4076                         reapply = 1; 
4077                     } 
4078                     /* connect the deviated node back into the augment */ 
4079                     lys_node_addchild(parent, NULL, dev->orig_node); 
4080                     if (reapply) { 
4081                         /* augment is supposed to be applied, so fix pointers in target and the status of the original node */ 
4082                         parent->flags |= LYS_NOTAPPLIED; /* allow apply_aug() */ 
4083                         apply_aug((struct lys_node_augment *)parent, unres); 
4084                     } 
4085                 } else { 
4086                     /* non-augment, non-toplevel */ 
4087                     parent_path = strndup(dev->target_name, strrchr(dev->target_name, '/') - dev->target_name); 
4088                     ret = resolve_schema_nodeid(parent_path, NULL, module, &set, 0, 1); 
4089                     free(parent_path); 
4090                     if (ret == -1) { 
4091                         LOGINT; 
4092                         ly_set_free(set); 
4093                         return; 
4094                     } 
4095                     target = set->set.s[0]; 
4096                     ly_set_free(set); 
4097  
4098                     lys_node_addchild(target, NULL, dev->orig_node); 
4099                 } 
4100             } else { 
4101                 /* ... from top-level data */ 
4102                 lys_node_addchild(NULL, (struct lys_module *)dev->orig_node->module, dev->orig_node); 
4103             } 
4104  
4105             dev->orig_node = NULL; 
4106         } else { 
4107             /* adding not-supported deviation */ 
4108             ret = resolve_schema_nodeid(dev->target_name, NULL, module, &set, 0, 1); 
4109             if (ret == -1) { 
4110                 LOGINT; 
4111                 ly_set_free(set); 
4112                 return; 
4113             } 
4114             target = set->set.s[0]; 
4115             ly_set_free(set); 
4116  
4117             /* unlink and store the original node */ 
4118             parent = target->parent; 
4119             lys_node_unlink(target); 
4120             if (parent && parent->nodetype == LYS_AUGMENT) { 
4121                 /* hack for augment, because when the original will be sometime reconnected back, we actually need 
4122                  * to reconnect it to both - the augment and its target (which is deduced from the deviations target 
4123                  * path), so we need to remember the augment as an addition */ 
4124                 target->parent = parent; 
4125             } 
4126             dev->orig_node = target; 
4127         } 
4128     } else { 
4129         ret = resolve_schema_nodeid(dev->target_name, NULL, module, &set, 0, 1); 
4130         if (ret == -1) { 
4131             LOGINT; 
4132             ly_set_free(set); 
4133             return; 
4134         } 
4135         target = set->set.s[0]; 
4136         ly_set_free(set); 
4137  
4138         lys_node_switch(target, dev->orig_node); 
4139         dev->orig_node = target; 
4140     } 
4141 } 
4142  
4143 /* temporarily removes or applies deviations, updates module deviation flag accordingly */ 
4144 void 
4145 lys_switch_deviations(struct lys_module *module) 
4146 { 
4147     uint32_t i = 0, j; 
4148     const struct lys_module *mod; 
4149     const char *ptr; 
4150     struct unres_schema *unres; 
4151  
4152     if (module->deviated) { 
4153         unres = calloc(1, sizeof *unres); 
4154         LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!unres, LOGMEM, ); 
4155  
4156         while ((mod = ly_ctx_get_module_iter(module->ctx, &i))) { 
4157             if (mod == module) { 
4158                 continue; 
4159             } 
4160  
4161             for (j = 0; j < mod->deviation_size; ++j) { 
4162                 ptr = strstr(mod->deviation[j].target_name, module->name); 
4163                 if (ptr && ptr[strlen(module->name)] == ':') { 
4164                     lys_switch_deviation(&mod->deviation[j], mod, unres); 
4165                 } 
4166             } 
4167         } 
4168  
4169         if (module->deviated == 2) { 
4170             module->deviated = 1; 
4171         } else { 
4172             module->deviated = 2; 
4173         } 
4174         for (j = 0; j < module->inc_size; j++) { 
4175             if (module->inc[j].submodule->deviated) { 
4176                 module->inc[j].submodule->deviated = module->deviated; 
4177             } 
4178         } 
4179  
4180         if (unres->count) { 
4181             resolve_unres_schema(module, unres); 
4182         } 
4183         unres_schema_free(module, &unres, 1); 
4184     } 
4185 } 
4186  
4187 static void 
4188 apply_dev(struct lys_deviation *dev, const struct lys_module *module, struct unres_schema *unres) 
4189 { 
4190     lys_switch_deviation(dev, module, unres); 
4191  
4192     assert(dev->orig_node); 
4193     lys_node_module(dev->orig_node)->deviated = 1; /* main module */ 
4194     dev->orig_node->module->deviated = 1;          /* possible submodule */ 
4195 } 
4196  
4197 static void 
4198 remove_dev(struct lys_deviation *dev, const struct lys_module *module, struct unres_schema *unres) 
4199 { 
4200     uint32_t idx = 0, j; 
4201     const struct lys_module *mod; 
4202     struct lys_module *target_mod, *target_submod; 
4203     const char *ptr; 
4204  
4205     if (dev->orig_node) { 
4206         target_mod = lys_node_module(dev->orig_node); 
4207         target_submod = dev->orig_node->module; 
4208     } else { 
4209         LOGINT; 
4210         return; 
4211     } 
4212     lys_switch_deviation(dev, module, unres); 
4213  
4214     /* clear the deviation flag if possible */ 
4215     while ((mod = ly_ctx_get_module_iter(module->ctx, &idx))) { 
4216         if ((mod == module) || (mod == target_mod)) { 
4217             continue; 
4218         } 
4219  
4220         for (j = 0; j < mod->deviation_size; ++j) { 
4221             ptr = strstr(mod->deviation[j].target_name, target_mod->name); 
4222             if (ptr && (ptr[strlen(target_mod->name)] == ':')) { 
4223                 /* some other module deviation targets the inspected module, flag remains */ 
4224                 break; 
4225             } 
4226         } 
4227  
4228         if (j < mod->deviation_size) { 
4229             break; 
4230         } 
4231     } 
4232  
4233     if (!mod) { 
4234         target_mod->deviated = 0;    /* main module */ 
4235         target_submod->deviated = 0; /* possible submodule */ 
4236     } 
4237 } 
4238  
4239 void 
4240 lys_sub_module_apply_devs_augs(struct lys_module *module) 
4241 { 
4242     uint8_t u, v; 
4243     struct unres_schema *unres; 
4244  
4245     unres = calloc(1, sizeof *unres); 
4246     LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!unres, LOGMEM, ); 
4247  
4248     /* remove applied deviations */ 
4249     for (u = 0; u < module->deviation_size; ++u) { 
4250         apply_dev(&module->deviation[u], module, unres); 
4251     } 
4252     /* remove applied augments */ 
4253     for (u = 0; u < module->augment_size; ++u) { 
4254         apply_aug(&module->augment[u], unres); 
4255     } 
4256  
4257     /* remove deviation and augments defined in submodules */ 
4258     for (v = 0; v < module->inc_size; ++v) { 
4259         for (u = 0; u < module->inc[v].submodule->deviation_size; ++u) { 
4260             apply_dev(&module->inc[v].submodule->deviation[u], module, unres); 
4261         } 
4262  
4263         for (u = 0; u < module->inc[v].submodule->augment_size; ++u) { 
4264             apply_aug(&module->inc[v].submodule->augment[u], unres); 
4265         } 
4266     } 
4267  
4268     if (unres->count) { 
4269         resolve_unres_schema(module, unres); 
4270     } 
4271     /* nothing else left to do even if something is not resolved */ 
4272     unres_schema_free(module, &unres, 1); 
4273 } 
4274  
4275 void 
4276 lys_sub_module_remove_devs_augs(struct lys_module *module) 
4277 { 
4278     uint8_t u, v; 
4279     struct unres_schema *unres; 
4280  
4281     unres = calloc(1, sizeof *unres); 
4282     LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!unres, LOGMEM, ); 
4283  
4284     /* remove applied deviations */ 
4285     for (u = 0; u < module->deviation_size; ++u) { 
4286         remove_dev(&module->deviation[u], module, unres); 
4287     } 
4288     /* remove applied augments */ 
4289     for (u = 0; u < module->augment_size; ++u) { 
4290         remove_aug(&module->augment[u]); 
4291     } 
4292  
4293     /* remove deviation and augments defined in submodules */ 
4294     for (v = 0; v < module->inc_size && module->inc[v].submodule; ++v) { 
4295         for (u = 0; u < module->inc[v].submodule->deviation_size; ++u) { 
4296             remove_dev(&module->inc[v].submodule->deviation[u], module, unres); 
4297         } 
4298  
4299         for (u = 0; u < module->inc[v].submodule->augment_size; ++u) { 
4300             remove_aug(&module->inc[v].submodule->augment[u]); 
4301         } 
4302     } 
4303  
4304     if (unres->count) { 
4305         resolve_unres_schema(module, unres); 
4306     } 
4307     /* nothing else left to do even if something is not resolved */ 
4308     unres_schema_free(module, &unres, 1); 
4309 } 
4310  
4311 static int 
4312 lys_set_implemented_recursion(struct lys_module *module, struct unres_schema *unres) 
4313 { 
4314     struct lys_node *root, *next, *node; 
4315     uint16_t i, j; 
4316  
4317     for (i = 0; i < module->augment_size; i++) { 
4318         /* apply augment */ 
4319         assert(module->augment[i].target); 
4320         if (apply_aug(&module->augment[i], unres)) { 
4321             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
4322         } 
4323     } 
4324  
4325     /* identities */ 
4326     for (i = 0; i < module->ident_size; i++) { 
4327         for (j = 0; j < module->ident[i].base_size; j++) { 
4328             resolve_identity_backlink_update(&module->ident[i], module->ident[i].base[j]); 
4329         } 
4330     } 
4331  
4332     LY_TREE_FOR(module->data, root) { 
4333         /* handle leafrefs and recursively change the implemented flags in the leafref targets */ 
4334         LY_TREE_DFS_BEGIN(root, next, node) { 
4335             if (node->nodetype == LYS_GROUPING) { 
4336                 goto nextsibling; 
4337             } 
4338             if (node->nodetype & (LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST)) { 
4339                 if (((struct lys_node_leaf *)node)->type.base == LY_TYPE_LEAFREF) { 
4340                     if (unres_schema_add_node(module, unres, &((struct lys_node_leaf *)node)->type, 
4341                                               UNRES_TYPE_LEAFREF, node) == -1) { 
4342                         return EXIT_FAILURE; 
4343                     } 
4344                 } 
4345             } 
4346  
4347             /* modified LY_TREE_DFS_END */ 
4348             next = node->child; 
4349             /* child exception for leafs, leaflists and anyxml without children */ 
4350             if (node->nodetype & (LYS_LEAF | LYS_LEAFLIST | LYS_ANYDATA)) { 
4351                 next = NULL; 
4352             } 
4353             if (!next) { 
4354 nextsibling: 
4355                 /* no children */ 
4356                 if (node == root) { 
4357                     /* we are done, root has no children */ 
4358                     break; 
4359                 } 
4360                 /* try siblings */ 
4361                 next = node->next; 
4362             } 
4363             while (!next) { 
4364                 /* parent is already processed, go to its sibling */ 
4365                 node = lys_parent(node); 
4366                 /* no siblings, go back through parents */ 
4367                 if (lys_parent(node) == lys_parent(root)) { 
4368                     /* we are done, no next element to process */ 
4369                     break; 
4370                 } 
4371                 next = node->next; 
4372             } 
4373         } 
4374     } 
4375  
4376     return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
4377 } 
4378  
4379 API int 
4380 lys_set_implemented(const struct lys_module *module) 
4381 { 
4382     struct ly_ctx *ctx; 
4383     struct unres_schema *unres; 
4384     int i, j, k, disabled = 0; 
4385  
4386     if (!module) { 
4387         ly_errno = LY_EINVAL; 
4388         return EXIT_FAILURE; 
4389     } 
4390  
4391     module = lys_main_module(module); 
4392  
4393     if (module->disabled) { 
4394         disabled = 1; 
4395         lys_set_enabled(module); 
4396     } 
4397  
4398     if (module->implemented) { 
4399         return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
4400     } 
4401  
4402     ctx = module->ctx; 
4403  
4404     for (i = 0; i < ctx->models.used; ++i) { 
4405         if (module == ctx->models.list[i]) { 
4406             continue; 
4407         } 
4408  
4409         if (!strcmp(module->name, ctx->models.list[i]->name) && ctx->models.list[i]->implemented) { 
4410             LOGERR(LY_EINVAL, "Module \"%s\" in another revision already implemented.", module->name); 
4411             if (disabled) { 
4412                 /* set it back disabled */ 
4413                 lys_set_disabled(module); 
4414             } 
4415             return EXIT_FAILURE; 
4416         } 
4417     } 
4418  
4419     unres = calloc(1, sizeof *unres); 
4420     if (!unres) { 
4421         LOGMEM; 
4422         if (disabled) { 
4423             /* set it back disabled */ 
4424             lys_set_disabled(module); 
4425         } 
4426         return EXIT_FAILURE; 
4427     } 
4428     /* recursively make the module implemented */ 
4429     ((struct lys_module *)module)->implemented = 1; 
4430     if (lys_set_implemented_recursion((struct lys_module *)module, unres)) { 
4431         goto error; 
4432     } 
4433     /* process augments in submodules */ 
4434     for (i = 0; i < module->inc_size && module->inc[i].submodule; ++i) { 
4435         for (j = 0; j < module->inc[i].submodule->augment_size; j++) { 
4436             /* apply augment */ 
4437             assert(module->inc[i].submodule->augment[j].target); 
4438             if (apply_aug(&module->inc[i].submodule->augment[j], unres)) { 
4439                 goto error; 
4440             } 
4441         } 
4442  
4443         /* identities */ 
4444         for (j = 0; j < module->inc[i].submodule->ident_size; j++) { 
4445             for (k = 0; k < module->inc[i].submodule->ident[j].base_size; k++) { 
4446                 resolve_identity_backlink_update(&module->inc[i].submodule->ident[j], 
4447                                                  module->inc[i].submodule->ident[j].base[k]); 
4448             } 
4449         } 
4450     } 
4451     /* try again resolve augments in other modules possibly augmenting this one, 
4452      * since we have just enabled it 
4453      */ 
4454     /* resolve rest of unres items */ 
4455     if (unres->count && resolve_unres_schema((struct lys_module *)module, unres)) { 
4456         goto error; 
4457     } 
4458     unres_schema_free(NULL, &unres, 0); 
4459  
4460     /* reflect implemented flag in submodules */ 
4461     for (i = 0; i < module->inc_size; i++) { 
4462         module->inc[i].submodule->implemented = 1; 
4463     } 
4464  
4465     LOGVRB("Module \"%s%s%s\" now implemented.", module->name, (module->rev_size ? "@" : ""), 
4466            (module->rev_size ? module->rev[0].date : "")); 
4467     return EXIT_SUCCESS; 
4468  
4469 error: 
4470     if (disabled) { 
4471         /* set it back disabled */ 
4472         lys_set_disabled(module); 
4473     } 
4474  
4475     ((struct lys_module *)module)->implemented = 0; 
4476     unres_schema_free((struct lys_module *)module, &unres, 1); 
4477     return EXIT_FAILURE; 
4478 } 
4479  
4480 void 
4481 lys_submodule_module_data_free(struct lys_submodule *submodule) 
4482 { 
4483     struct lys_node *next, *elem; 
4484  
4485     /* remove parsed data */ 
4486     LY_TREE_FOR_SAFE(submodule->belongsto->data, next, elem) { 
4487         if (elem->module == (struct lys_module *)submodule) { 
4488             lys_node_free(elem, NULL, 0); 
4489         } 
4490     } 
4491 } 
4492  
4493 int 
4494 lys_is_key(struct lys_node_list *list, struct lys_node_leaf *leaf) 
4495 { 
4496     uint8_t i; 
4497  
4498     for (i = 0; i < list->keys_size; i++) { 
4499         if (list->keys[i] == leaf) { 
4500             return i + 1; 
4501         } 
4502     } 
4503  
4504     return 0; 
4505 } 
4506  
4507 API char * 
4508 lys_path(const struct lys_node *node) 
4509 { 
4510     char *buf, *result; 
4511     uint16_t start_idx, len; 
4512  
4513     if (!node) { 
4514         LOGERR(LY_EINVAL, "%s: NULL node parameter", __func__); 
4515         return NULL; 
4516     } 
4517  
4518     buf = malloc(LY_BUF_SIZE); 
4519     LY_CHECK_ERR_RETURN(!buf, LOGMEM, NULL); 
4520     start_idx = LY_BUF_SIZE - 1; 
4521  
4522     buf[start_idx] = '\0'; 
4523     if (ly_vlog_build_path_reverse(LY_VLOG_LYS, node, &buf, &start_idx, &len, 1)) { 
4524         free(buf); 
4525         return NULL; 
4526     } 
4527  
4528     result = malloc(len + 1); 
4529     if (!result) { 
4530         LOGMEM; 
4531         free(buf); 
4532         return NULL; 
4533     } 
4534  
4535     result = memcpy(result, &buf[start_idx], len); 
4536     result[len] = '\0'; 
4537     free(buf); 
4538  
4539     return result; 
4540 } 
4541  
4542 API char * 
4543 lys_data_path(const struct lys_node *node) 
4544 { 
4545     char *buf_backup = NULL, *buf = ly_buf(), *result = NULL; 
4546     int i, used; 
4547     struct ly_set *set; 
4548     const struct lys_module *prev_mod; 
4549  
4550     if (!node) { 
4551         LOGERR(LY_EINVAL, "%s: NULL node parameter", __func__); 
4552         return NULL; 
4553     } 
4554  
4555     /* backup the shared internal buffer */ 
4556     if (ly_buf_used && buf[0]) { 
4557         buf_backup = strndup(buf, LY_BUF_SIZE - 1); 
4558     } 
4559     ly_buf_used++; 
4560  
4561     set = ly_set_new(); 
4562     LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(!set, LOGMEM, error); 
4563  
4564     while (node) { 
4565         ly_set_add(set, (void *)node, 0); 
4566         do { 
4567             node = lys_parent(node); 
4568         } while (node && (node->nodetype & (LYS_USES | LYS_CHOICE | LYS_CASE | LYS_INPUT | LYS_OUTPUT))); 
4569     } 
4570  
4571     prev_mod = NULL; 
4572     used = 0; 
4573     for (i = set->number - 1; i > -1; --i) { 
4574         node = set->set.s[i]; 
4575         used += sprintf(buf + used, "/%s%s%s", (lys_node_module(node) == prev_mod ? "" : lys_node_module(node)->name), 
4576                         (lys_node_module(node) == prev_mod ? "" : ":"), node->name); 
4577         prev_mod = lys_node_module(node); 
4578     } 
4579  
4580     result = strdup(buf); 
4581     LY_CHECK_ERR_GOTO(!result, LOGMEM, error); 
4582  
4583 error: 
4584     ly_set_free(set); 
4585     /* restore the shared internal buffer */ 
4586     if (buf_backup) { 
4587         strcpy(buf, buf_backup); 
4588         free(buf_backup); 
4589     } 
4590     ly_buf_used--; 
4591  
4592     return result; 
4593 } 
4594  
4595 struct lys_node_augment * 
4596 lys_getnext_target_aug(struct lys_node_augment *last, const struct lys_module *mod, const struct lys_node *aug_target) 
4597 { 
4598     int i, j, last_found; 
4599  
4600     if (!last) { 
4601         last_found = 1; 
4602     } else { 
4603         last_found = 0; 
4604     } 
4605  
4606     /* search module augments */ 
4607     for (i = 0; i < mod->augment_size; ++i) { 
4608         if (!mod->augment[i].target) { 
4609             /* still unresolved, skip */ 
4610             continue; 
4611         } 
4612  
4613         if (mod->augment[i].target == aug_target) { 
4614             if (last_found) { 
4615                 /* next match after last */ 
4616                 return &mod->augment[i]; 
4617             } 
4618  
4619             if (&mod->augment[i] == last) { 
4620                 last_found = 1; 
4621             } 
4622         } 
4623     } 
4624  
4625     /* search submodule augments */ 
4626     for (i = 0; i < mod->inc_size; ++i) { 
4627         for (j = 0; j < mod->inc[i].submodule->augment_size; ++j) { 
4628             if (!mod->inc[i].submodule->augment[j].target) { 
4629                 continue; 
4630             } 
4631  
4632             if (mod->inc[i].submodule->augment[j].target == aug_target) { 
4633                 if (last_found) { 
4634                     /* next match after last */ 
4635                     return &mod->inc[i].submodule->augment[j]; 
4636                 } 
4637  
4638                 if (&mod->inc[i].submodule->augment[j] == last) { 
4639                     last_found = 1; 
4640                 } 
4641             } 
4642         } 
4643     } 
4644  
4645     return NULL; 
4646 } 
4647  
4648 API struct ly_set * 
4649 lys_find_path(const struct lys_module *cur_module, const struct lys_node *cur_node, const char *path) 
4650 { 
4651     struct ly_set *ret; 
4652     int rc; 
4653  
4654     if ((!cur_module && !cur_node) || !path) { 
4655         return NULL; 
4656     } 
4657  
4658     rc = resolve_schema_nodeid(path, cur_node, cur_module, &ret, 1, 1); 
4659     if (rc == -1) { 
4660         return NULL; 
4661     } 
4662  
4663     return ret; 
4664 } 
4665  
4666 static void 
4667 lys_extcomplex_free_str(struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lys_ext_instance_complex *ext, LY_STMT stmt) 
4668 { 
4669     struct lyext_substmt *info; 
4670     const char **str, ***a; 
4671     int c; 
4672  
4673     str = lys_ext_complex_get_substmt(stmt, ext, &info); 
4674     if (!str || !(*str)) { 
4675         return; 
4676     } 
4677     if (info->cardinality >= LY_STMT_CARD_SOME) { 
4678         /* we have array */ 
4679         a = (const char ***)str; 
4680         for (str = (*(const char ***)str), c = 0; str[c]; c++) { 
4681             lydict_remove(ctx, str[c]); 
4682         } 
4683         free(a[0]); 
4684         if (stmt == LY_STMT_BELONGSTO) { 
4685             for (str = a[1], c = 0; str[c]; c++) { 
4686                 lydict_remove(ctx, str[c]); 
4687             } 
4688             free(a[1]); 
4689         } else if (stmt == LY_STMT_ARGUMENT) { 
4690             free(a[1]); 
4691         } 
4692     } else { 
4693         lydict_remove(ctx, str[0]); 
4694         if (stmt == LY_STMT_BELONGSTO) { 
4695             lydict_remove(ctx, str[1]); 
4696         } 
4697     } 
4698 } 
4699 void 
4700 lys_extension_instances_free(struct ly_ctx *ctx, struct lys_ext_instance **e, unsigned int size, 
4701                              void (*private_destructor)(const struct lys_node *node, void *priv)) 
4702 { 
4703     unsigned int i, j, k; 
4704     struct lyext_substmt *substmt; 
4705     void **pp, **start; 
4706     struct lys_node *siter, *snext; 
4707  
4708 #define EXTCOMPLEX_FREE_STRUCT(STMT, TYPE, FUNC, FREE, ARGS...)                               \ 
4709     pp = lys_ext_complex_get_substmt(STMT, (struct lys_ext_instance_complex *)e[i], NULL);    \ 
4710     if (!pp || !(*pp)) { break; }                                                             \ 
4711     if (substmt[j].cardinality >= LY_STMT_CARD_SOME) { /* process array */                    \ 
4712         for (start = pp = *pp; *pp; pp++) {                                                   \ 
4713             FUNC(ctx, (TYPE *)(*pp), ##ARGS, private_destructor);                             \ 
4714             if (FREE) { free(*pp); }                                                          \ 
4715         }                                                                                     \ 
4716         free(start);                                                                          \ 
4717     } else { /* single item */                                                                \ 
4718         FUNC(ctx, (TYPE *)(*pp), ##ARGS, private_destructor);                                 \ 
4719         if (FREE) { free(*pp); }                                                              \ 
4720     } 
4721  
4722     if (!size || !e || !(*e)) { 
4723         return; 
4724     } 
4725  
4726     for (i = 0; i < size; i++) { 
4727         if (!e[i]) { 
4728             continue; 
4729         } 
4730  
4731         if (e[i]->flags & (LYEXT_OPT_INHERIT)) { 
4732             /* no free, this is just a shadow copy of the original extension instance */ 
4733         } else { 
4734             if (e[i]->flags & (LYEXT_OPT_YANG)) { 
4735                 free(e[i]->def);     /* remove name of instance extension */ 
4736                 e[i]->def = NULL; 
4737                 yang_free_ext_data((struct yang_ext_substmt *)e[i]->parent); /* remove backup part of yang file */ 
4738             } 
4739             /* remove private object */ 
4740             if (e[i]->priv && private_destructor) { 
4741                 private_destructor((struct lys_node*)e[i], e[i]->priv); 
4742             } 
4743             lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, e[i]->ext, e[i]->ext_size, private_destructor); 
4744             lydict_remove(ctx, e[i]->arg_value); 
4745         } 
4746  
4747         if (e[i]->def && e[i]->def->plugin && e[i]->def->plugin->type == LYEXT_COMPLEX 
4748                 && ((e[i]->flags & LYEXT_OPT_CONTENT) == 0)) { 
4749             substmt = ((struct lys_ext_instance_complex *)e[i])->substmt; 
4750             for (j = 0; substmt[j].stmt; j++) { 
4751                 switch(substmt[j].stmt) { 
4752                 case LY_STMT_DESCRIPTION: 
4753                 case LY_STMT_REFERENCE: 
4754                 case LY_STMT_UNITS: 
4755                 case LY_STMT_ARGUMENT: 
4756                 case LY_STMT_DEFAULT: 
4757                 case LY_STMT_ERRTAG: 
4758                 case LY_STMT_ERRMSG: 
4759                 case LY_STMT_PREFIX: 
4760                 case LY_STMT_NAMESPACE: 
4761                 case LY_STMT_PRESENCE: 
4762                 case LY_STMT_REVISIONDATE: 
4763                 case LY_STMT_KEY: 
4764                 case LY_STMT_BASE: 
4765                 case LY_STMT_BELONGSTO: 
4766                 case LY_STMT_CONTACT: 
4767                 case LY_STMT_ORGANIZATION: 
4768                 case LY_STMT_PATH: 
4769                     lys_extcomplex_free_str(ctx, (struct lys_ext_instance_complex *)e[i], substmt[j].stmt); 
4770                     break; 
4771                 case LY_STMT_TYPE: 
4772                     EXTCOMPLEX_FREE_STRUCT(LY_STMT_TYPE, struct lys_type, lys_type_free, 1); 
4773                     break; 
4774                 case LY_STMT_TYPEDEF: 
4775                     EXTCOMPLEX_FREE_STRUCT(LY_STMT_TYPEDEF, struct lys_tpdf, lys_tpdf_free, 1); 
4776                     break; 
4777                 case LY_STMT_IFFEATURE: 
4778                     EXTCOMPLEX_FREE_STRUCT(LY_STMT_IFFEATURE, struct lys_iffeature, lys_iffeature_free, 0, 1, 0); 
4779                     break; 
4780                 case LY_STMT_MAX: 
4781                 case LY_STMT_MIN: 
4782                 case LY_STMT_POSITION: 
4783                 case LY_STMT_VALUE: 
4784                     pp = (void**)&((struct lys_ext_instance_complex *)e[i])->content[substmt[j].offset]; 
4785                     if (substmt[j].cardinality >= LY_STMT_CARD_SOME && *pp) { 
4786                         for(k = 0; ((uint32_t**)(*pp))[k]; k++) { 
4787                             free(((uint32_t**)(*pp))[k]); 
4788                         } 
4789                     } 
4790                     free(*pp); 
4791                     break; 
4792                 case LY_STMT_DIGITS: 
4793                     if (substmt[j].cardinality >= LY_STMT_CARD_SOME) { 
4794                         /* free the array */ 
4795                         pp = (void**)&((struct lys_ext_instance_complex *)e[i])->content[substmt[j].offset]; 
4796                         free(*pp); 
4797                     } 
4798                     break; 
4799                 case LY_STMT_MODULE: 
4800                     /* modules are part of the context, so they will be freed there */ 
4801                     if (substmt[j].cardinality >= LY_STMT_CARD_SOME) { 
4802                         /* free the array */ 
4803                         pp = (void**)&((struct lys_ext_instance_complex *)e[i])->content[substmt[j].offset]; 
4804                         free(*pp); 
4805                     } 
4806                     break; 
4807                 case LY_STMT_ACTION: 
4808                 case LY_STMT_ANYDATA: 
4809                 case LY_STMT_ANYXML: 
4810                 case LY_STMT_CASE: 
4811                 case LY_STMT_CHOICE: 
4812                 case LY_STMT_CONTAINER: 
4813                 case LY_STMT_GROUPING: 
4814                 case LY_STMT_INPUT: 
4815                 case LY_STMT_LEAF: 
4816                 case LY_STMT_LEAFLIST: 
4817                 case LY_STMT_LIST: 
4818                 case LY_STMT_NOTIFICATION: 
4819                 case LY_STMT_OUTPUT: 
4820                 case LY_STMT_RPC: 
4821                 case LY_STMT_USES: 
4822                     pp = (void**)&((struct lys_ext_instance_complex *)e[i])->content[substmt[j].offset]; 
4823                     LY_TREE_FOR_SAFE((struct lys_node *)(*pp), snext, siter) { 
4824                         lys_node_free(siter, NULL, 0); 
4825                     } 
4826                     *pp = NULL; 
4827                     break; 
4828                 case LY_STMT_UNIQUE: 
4829                     pp = lys_ext_complex_get_substmt(LY_STMT_UNIQUE, (struct lys_ext_instance_complex *)e[i], NULL); 
4830                     if (!pp || !(*pp)) { 
4831                         break; 
4832                     } 
4833                     if (substmt[j].cardinality >= LY_STMT_CARD_SOME) { /* process array */ 
4834                         for (start = pp = *pp; *pp; pp++) { 
4835                             for (k = 0; k < (*(struct lys_unique**)pp)->expr_size; k++) { 
4836                                 lydict_remove(ctx, (*(struct lys_unique**)pp)->expr[k]); 
4837                             } 
4838                             free((*(struct lys_unique**)pp)->expr); 
4839                             free(*pp); 
4840                         } 
4841                         free(start); 
4842                     } else { /* single item */ 
4843                         for (k = 0; k < (*(struct lys_unique**)pp)->expr_size; k++) { 
4844                             lydict_remove(ctx, (*(struct lys_unique**)pp)->expr[k]); 
4845                         } 
4846                         free((*(struct lys_unique**)pp)->expr); 
4847                         free(*pp); 
4848                     } 
4849                     break; 
4850                 case LY_STMT_LENGTH: 
4851                 case LY_STMT_MUST: 
4852                 case LY_STMT_PATTERN: 
4853                 case LY_STMT_RANGE: 
4854                     EXTCOMPLEX_FREE_STRUCT(substmt[j].stmt, struct lys_restr, lys_restr_free, 1); 
4855                     break; 
4856                 case LY_STMT_WHEN: 
4857                     EXTCOMPLEX_FREE_STRUCT(LY_STMT_WHEN, struct lys_when, lys_when_free, 0); 
4858                     break; 
4859                 case LY_STMT_REVISION: 
4860                     pp = lys_ext_complex_get_substmt(LY_STMT_REVISION, (struct lys_ext_instance_complex *)e[i], NULL); 
4861                     if (!pp || !(*pp)) { 
4862                         break; 
4863                     } 
4864                     if (substmt[j].cardinality >= LY_STMT_CARD_SOME) { /* process array */ 
4865                         for (start = pp = *pp; *pp; pp++) { 
4866                             lydict_remove(ctx, (*(struct lys_revision**)pp)->dsc); 
4867                             lydict_remove(ctx, (*(struct lys_revision**)pp)->ref); 
4868                             lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, (*(struct lys_revision**)pp)->ext, 
4869                                                          (*(struct lys_revision**)pp)->ext_size, private_destructor); 
4870                             free(*pp); 
4871                         } 
4872                         free(start); 
4873                     } else { /* single item */ 
4874                         lydict_remove(ctx, (*(struct lys_revision**)pp)->dsc); 
4875                         lydict_remove(ctx, (*(struct lys_revision**)pp)->ref); 
4876                         lys_extension_instances_free(ctx, (*(struct lys_revision**)pp)->ext, 
4877                                                      (*(struct lys_revision**)pp)->ext_size, private_destructor); 
4878                         free(*pp); 
4879                     } 
4880                     break; 
4881                 default: 
4882                     /* nothing to free */ 
4883                     break; 
4884                 } 
4885             } 
4886         } 
4887  
4888         free(e[i]); 
4889     } 
4890     free(e); 
4891  
4892 #undef EXTCOMPLEX_FREE_STRUCT 
4893 } 
4894
